    public static void RunValidation()
    {
        if (EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode) throw new InvalidOperationException("Manual1619 requires idle main Editor.");
        EnsureNoPriorExternalCompanionActive();
        S005NativeOwnedActorBridge1619.Validate();
        if (ReadPoint == null || TracePath == null || ProfileRoot==null || RecoveryType==null || NativePlayerLabel == null || NativeEnemyLabel == null || NativeClockLabel == null) throw new MissingMethodException("Required existing read-only harness observer bridge absent.");
        if (File.Exists(Evidence) || File.Exists(CheckpointArchive)) throw new IOException("Preserve existing1619 observation evidence.");
        using (File.Open(Evidence,FileMode.CreateNew,FileAccess.Write,FileShare.Read)) { }
        teardownStarted=false;
        try
        {
            S005CatalogTouch1619.Start();
            S005PassengerCarrierIdentity1619.Initialize(Report+"native1619-passenger-carrier-identity.jsonl");
            S005SouthFlank1619.Start();
            S005EarlyRoute1619.Start();
            S005ManualHomeDefense1619.Start();
S005ManualFogFallback1619.Start(()=>S005ManualHomeDefense1619.InitialDefenseReady&&S005ManualHomeDefense1619.ArmoredStaged);
            S005FreshRocketeerPlanner1619.Start();
        }
        catch { StopAllExternalCompanions("CompanionStartupFailure"); throw; }
        everPlaying=setupSeen=bindingSeen=purposeSeen=activeControlSeen=resultSeen=returnSeen=false;trackedPaidReceipt=0;trackedPaidStable="";
        finishedVictorySeen=manualPlaySeen=checkpointArchived=restoredSeen=postTerminalProfileReported=companionFailureClosed=false; witnessedAttempt=initialAttempt=saveRoot=nativeSavePath=""; lastPausedElapsed=0;
        receiptStates.Clear(); lastRows.Clear(); next=0; began=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;
        EditorApplication.update += Observe;
        Canvas.willRenderCanvases += ObserveRendered;
        try
        {
            // Exact existing EN entry semantics, with the same normal menu owner
            // configured for FA before it caches locale. QA startup flags only.
            var harness=typeof(SkirmishS002AriaRunHarness);
            var entry=harness.GetMethod("RunEditorJourney",BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Static,null,
                new[]{typeof(string),typeof(bool),typeof(bool),typeof(string),typeof(int)},null);
            var done=harness.GetField("playerResumeProbeDone",BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Static);
            if(entry==null||done==null)throw new MissingMethodException("Existing paid manual journey owner unavailable.");
            entry.Invoke(null,new object[]{"fa-IR",true,false,"S005",155926});
            done.SetValue(null,false);
            Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable("WARLINE_S005_PLAYER_RESUME_PROBE","1");
            Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable("WARLINE_S005_COMBAT_RESUME_PROBE","1");
            Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable("WARLINE_S005_SAVE_ROLE","ApcHeavy");
        }
        catch { StopAllExternalCompanions("StartupFailure"); throw; }
        bool flags=Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("WARLINE_S004_MENU_JOURNEY")=="1"
            && Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("WARLINE_SKIRMISH_CATALOG")=="S005"
            && Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("WARLINE_S002_LOCALE")=="fa-IR"
            && Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("WARLINE_S002_SEED")=="155926"
            && Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("WARLINE_TERMINAL_ACTION")=="MAIN MENU"
            && Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("WARLINE_S004_MANUAL_PROBE")=="1"
            && Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("WARLINE_S005_PLAYER_RESUME_PROBE")=="1"
            && Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("WARLINE_S005_COMBAT_RESUME_PROBE")=="1"
            && Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("WARLINE_S005_SAVE_ROLE")=="ApcHeavy";
        foreach(string name in new[]{"WARLINE_SKIRMISH_STARTUP_ONLY","WARLINE_SKIRMISH_INPUT_DIAGNOSTIC","WARLINE_S005_NATIVE_RECOVERY_ONLY","WARLINE_S005_PAID_BUILDING_CLEARANCE_ONLY","WARLINE_S004_STOP_PROBE"}) flags &= string.IsNullOrEmpty(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(name));
        Append(new Row{stage="NormalDriverFlags",normalDriverFlags=flags,reason="Existing public HeavyAPC paidR2/manual/SaveContinue full path; no funding/state/forced outcome injections"});
        Append(new Row {stage="StartedExistingPublicJourney",reason="Existing normal RunEditorJourney FA plus exact public HeavyAPC save-entry QA flags; observerPlusSameNativeManualTouchFogApproach=1; noGameplayStateWrites=1"});
    }
    static void Append(Row row)
    {
        string key=row.stage+"|"+row.controlPath+"|"+row.reservation+"|"+(row.stage=="NativeRenderedResultText" ? row.title : "");
        string state=JsonUtility.ToJson(row);
        if(lastRows.TryGetValue(key,out var prior) && prior==state)return;
        lastRows[key]=state;
        row.frame=Time.frameCount;row.timeScale=Time.timeScale;
        row.locale=UiShellRuntimeGateway.Localization.CurrentLocaleCode;
        File.AppendAllText(Evidence,JsonUtility.ToJson(row)+"\n");
    }
    static string PathOf(Transform t) => t==null ? "" : t.parent==null ? t.name : PathOf(t.parent)+"/"+t.name;
    static bool Presented(Button b)
    {
        if (b==null) return false;
        return (bool)ReadPoint.Invoke(null,new object[]{b,default(Vector2)});
    }
    static bool CopyMatches(TMP_Text t,string localized)
    {
        if(t==null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(localized))return false;
        string expected=UiLocalizedText.CatalogLabel(localized);
        string raw=t is RTLTMPro.RTLTextMeshPro rtl ? rtl.OriginalText : t.text;
        return string.Equals(raw,expected,StringComparison.Ordinal) || string.Equals(raw,V3LocalizedTextBindingView.ShapeForRendering(expected),StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }
    static string AuthoredSetupCopy(string catalog,int seed) {
        if(!SkirmishSetupBriefing.TryLoad(catalog,seed,out var setup))throw new InvalidOperationException("Exact production briefing setup unresolved");
        return new SkirmishSetupBriefing(setup,UiShellRuntimeGateway.Localization.CurrentLocaleCode.StartsWith("fa",StringComparison.Ordinal)).Objective;
    }
    static string AuthoredHelpCopy() {
        var w=World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;if(w==null||!w.IsCreated)throw new InvalidOperationException("Native help setup absent");
        using var q=w.EntityManager.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishResolvedSetupRecord));if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Native resolved help setup not unique");
        return SkirmishSetupBriefing.MatchHelp(w.EntityManager.GetComponentObject<SkirmishResolvedSetupRecord>(q.GetSingletonEntity()).Setup,UiShellRuntimeGateway.Localization.CurrentLocaleCode.StartsWith("fa",StringComparison.Ordinal));
    }
    static string ClockNumber(string source)
    {
        if(string.IsNullOrEmpty(source))return "";
        int separator=source.LastIndexOf("  ",StringComparison.Ordinal);
        string value=separator>=0 ? source.Substring(separator+2).Trim() : source.Trim();
        var parts=value.Split(':');
        if(parts.Length!=2 || !int.TryParse(parts[0],out int minutes) || !int.TryParse(parts[1],out int seconds) || minutes<0 || seconds<0 || seconds>=60)return "";
        return value;
    }
    static string Text(TMP_Text t) => t==null ? "" : t.text ?? "";
    static bool Rendered(TMP_Text t)
    {
        if(t==null || !t.isActiveAndEnabled || !t.gameObject.activeInHierarchy || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(t.text) || t.color.a<=.01f || t.canvasRenderer.cull) return false;
        foreach(var group in t.GetComponentsInParent<CanvasGroup>()) if(group.alpha<=.01f)return false;
        bool glyph=false;
        if(t.textInfo!=null)for(int i=0;i<t.textInfo.characterCount;i++)if(t.textInfo.characterInfo[i].isVisible){glyph=true;break;}
        if(!glyph)return false;
        var canvas=t.GetComponentInParent<Canvas>();
        var camera=canvas!=null && canvas.renderMode!=RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay ? canvas.worldCamera : null;
        var corners=new Vector3[4];t.rectTransform.GetWorldCorners(corners);
        var low=RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(camera,corners[0]);var high=RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(camera,corners[2]);
        return new Rect(Mathf.Min(low.x,high.x),Mathf.Min(low.y,high.y),Mathf.Abs(high.x-low.x),Mathf.Abs(high.y-low.y)).Overlaps(Screen.safeArea);
    }
    static void BoundText(string role,TMP_Text text,Transform owner)
    {
        if(text==null){Append(new Row{stage="NativeBoundText",role=role,ownerPath=PathOf(owner),samplingPhase="CanvasWillRenderCanvases",reason="MissingExactNativeBoundLabel"});return;}
        var canvas=text.GetComponentInParent<Canvas>();
        var camera=canvas!=null && canvas.renderMode!=RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay ? canvas.worldCamera : null;
        var corners=new Vector3[4];text.rectTransform.GetWorldCorners(corners);
        var lo=RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(camera,corners[0]);var hi=RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(camera,corners[2]);
        var rect=new Rect(Mathf.Min(lo.x,hi.x),Mathf.Min(lo.y,hi.y),Mathf.Abs(hi.x-lo.x),Mathf.Abs(hi.y-lo.y));
        float alpha=1;foreach(var group in text.GetComponentsInParent<CanvasGroup>())alpha=Mathf.Min(alpha,group.alpha);
        int visible=0;if(text.textInfo!=null)for(int i=0;i<text.textInfo.characterCount;i++)if(text.textInfo.characterInfo[i].isVisible)visible++;
        Rect safe=Screen.safeArea,viewport=canvas!=null?canvas.pixelRect:default;
        Append(new Row{stage="NativeBoundText",role=role,controlPath=PathOf(text.transform),textPath=PathOf(text.transform),ownerPath=PathOf(owner),exactBoundReference=owner!=null && text.transform.IsChildOf(owner),samplingPhase="CanvasWillRenderCanvases",body=Text(text),activeText=text.isActiveAndEnabled && text.gameObject.activeInHierarchy,culledText=text.canvasRenderer.cull,textAlpha=text.color.a,ancestorAlpha=alpha,characterCount=text.textInfo?.characterCount??0,visibleGlyphs=visible,safeAreaOverlap=rect.Overlaps(safe),textX=rect.x,textY=rect.y,textWidth=rect.width,textHeight=rect.height,screenWidth=Screen.width,screenHeight=Screen.height,safeX=safe.x,safeY=safe.y,safeWidth=safe.width,safeHeight=safe.height,canvasX=viewport.x,canvasY=viewport.y,canvasWidth=viewport.width,canvasHeight=viewport.height,renderedBody=Rendered(text)});
    }
    static TMP_Text Label(Transform parent,string path) => parent.Find(path)?.GetComponent<TMP_Text>();
    static void Observe()
    {
        try
        {
            if(AnyCompanionFailed()){CloseAfterCompanionFailure();return;}
            S005PassengerCarrierIdentity1619.Observe("EditorUpdate");
            if(EditorApplication.isPlaying)everPlaying=true;
            if(everPlaying && !EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode)
            {
                StopAllExternalCompanions("NativeClosed");
                Append(new Row{stage="ClosedObserver",setupSeen=setupSeen,bindingSeen=bindingSeen,purposeSeen=purposeSeen,activeControlSeen=activeControlSeen,manualPlaySeen=manualPlaySeen,resultSeen=resultSeen,returnSeen=returnSeen,tracePath=(string)TracePath.Invoke(null,null)});
                Debug.Log("[S005NormalSouthFlank1619] observerClosed=1 normalJourneyEvidence=PendingOfflineChecks playerReady=0");
                return;
            }
            if(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-began>1740d)
            {
                Append(new Row{stage="ObserverFailure",reason="ReadOnlyObserverDeadline; native harness/ownership preserved"});StopAllExternalCompanions("ObserverDeadline");SkirmishS002AriaRunHarness.AbortFailedValidationAndExit();return;
            }
        }
        catch(Exception error) { ObserverFailure(error); }
    }
    static bool companionFailureClosed;
    static bool AnyCompanionFailed()=>S005ManualFogFallback1619.HasFailed||S005ManualHomeDefense1619.HasFailed||S005FreshRocketeerPlanner1619.HasFailed||S005CatalogTouch1619.HasFailed||S005EarlyRoute1619.HasFailed;
    static string CompanionFailureReason()
    {
        if(S005ManualFogFallback1619.HasFailed)return "ManualFogFallback: "+S005ManualFogFallback1619.FailureReason;
        if(S005ManualHomeDefense1619.HasFailed)return "ManualHomeDefense: "+S005ManualHomeDefense1619.FailureReason;
        if(S005FreshRocketeerPlanner1619.HasFailed)return "FreshRocketeerPlanner: "+S005FreshRocketeerPlanner1619.FailureReason;
        if(S005CatalogTouch1619.HasFailed)return "CatalogTouch failed; preserve its own failure row";
        if(S005EarlyRoute1619.HasFailed)return "EarlyRoute failed; preserve its own failure row";
        return "Unknown external companion failure";
    }
    static void EnsureNoPriorExternalCompanionActive()
    {
        var owners=new[]{(typeof(EditorApplication),"update"),(typeof(EditorApplication),"delayCall"),(typeof(Canvas),"willRenderCanvases"),(typeof(Application),"s_LogCallbackHandler"),(typeof(AriaCommandEvidence),"Recorded")};
        foreach(var owner in owners)
        {
            var field=owner.Item1.GetField(owner.Item2,BindingFlags.Static|BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.NonPublic);
            if(field==null||!typeof(Delegate).IsAssignableFrom(field.FieldType))throw new InvalidOperationException("Prior external S005 callback field cannot be audited: "+owner.Item1.FullName+"."+owner.Item2);
            var callbacks=(field.GetValue(null) as MulticastDelegate)?.GetInvocationList();
            if(callbacks==null)continue;
            foreach(var callback in callbacks)
            {
                string declaring=callback.Method.DeclaringType?.FullName??"";
                if(!declaring.StartsWith("S005",StringComparison.Ordinal))continue;
                for(int previous=1601;previous<=1619;previous++)
                    if(declaring.Contains(previous.ToString()))throw new InvalidOperationException("Prior external S005 callback still attached; clear stale in-memory delegate before new native launch: "+owner.Item1.Name+"."+owner.Item2+" -> "+declaring+"."+callback.Method.Name);
            }
        }
    }
    static void StopAllExternalCompanions(string reason)
    {
        if(teardownStarted)return;
        teardownStarted=true;
        EditorApplication.update-=Observe;Canvas.willRenderCanvases-=ObserveRendered;
        Action<string,Action> stop=(name,action)=>{try{action();}catch(Exception error){Debug.LogWarning("[S005NormalSouthFlank1619] teardown="+name+" reason="+reason+" error="+error.Message);}};
        stop("FreshRocketeerPlanner",()=>S005FreshRocketeerPlanner1619.Stop(reason));
        stop("CatalogTouch",()=>S005CatalogTouch1619.Stop(reason));
        stop("ManualHomeDefense",()=>S005ManualHomeDefense1619.Stop(reason));
        stop("ManualFogFallback",()=>S005ManualFogFallback1619.Stop(reason));
        stop("GroundTouchDiagnostic",()=>S005GroundTouchDiagnostic1619.Stop(reason));
        stop("EarlyRoute",()=>S005EarlyRoute1619.Stop(reason));
        try{DetachOwnCallbacks();}
        catch(Exception error){Debug.LogError("[S005NormalSouthFlank1619] own callback teardown failed; original reason="+reason+" error="+error);}
    }
    static void DetachOwnCallbacks()
    {
        var owners=new[]{(typeof(EditorApplication),"update"),(typeof(EditorApplication),"delayCall"),(typeof(Canvas),"willRenderCanvases"),(typeof(Application),"s_LogCallbackHandler"),(typeof(AriaCommandEvidence),"Recorded")};
        foreach(var owner in owners)
        {
            var field=owner.Item1.GetField(owner.Item2,BindingFlags.Static|BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.NonPublic);
            if(field==null||!typeof(Delegate).IsAssignableFrom(field.FieldType))throw new InvalidOperationException("Own external S005 callback field cannot be audited: "+owner.Item1.FullName+"."+owner.Item2);
            var source=field.GetValue(null) as MulticastDelegate;
            if(source==null)continue;
            Delegate reduced=source;int detached=0;
            foreach(var callback in source.GetInvocationList())
            {
                string declaring=callback.Method.DeclaringType?.FullName??"";
                if(!declaring.StartsWith("S005",StringComparison.Ordinal)||!declaring.Contains("1619"))continue;
                reduced=Delegate.Remove(reduced,callback);detached++;
            }
            if(detached>0)field.SetValue(null,reduced);
        }
    }
    static void CloseAfterCompanionFailure()
    {
        if(companionFailureClosed)return;
        companionFailureClosed=true;
        string originalFailure=CompanionFailureReason();
        try{Append(new Row{stage="CompanionFailureObserved",reason=originalFailure});}
        catch(Exception evidenceError){Debug.LogWarning("[S005NormalSouthFlank1619] companion failure row unavailable after teardown: "+evidenceError.Message);}
        StopAllExternalCompanions("CompanionFailure");
        if(EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode)SkirmishS002AriaRunHarness.AbortFailedValidationAndExit();
        Debug.LogError("[S005NormalSouthFlank1619] companionFailure=1 originalFailurePreserved=1");
    }
    static void ObserverFailure(Exception error)
    {
        try{Append(new Row{stage="ObserverFailure",reason=error.ToString()});}
        catch(Exception evidenceError){Debug.LogWarning("[S005NormalSouthFlank1619] observer failure row unavailable: "+evidenceError.Message);}
        StopAllExternalCompanions("ObserverFailure");
        if(EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode)SkirmishS002AriaRunHarness.AbortFailedValidationAndExit();
        Debug.LogError("[S005NormalSouthFlank1619] observerFailure=1 gameplayUnmodified=1");
    }

    static string Canonical(string path)
    {string full=Path.GetFullPath(path);return full.StartsWith("/var/",StringComparison.Ordinal)?"/private"+full:full;}
    static void EnsureInheritedProfile()
    {
        string inheritedOperand=(string)ProfileRoot.Invoke(null,null);
        string environmentOperand=Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("WARLINE_VALIDATION_SAVE_ROOT");
        if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(inheritedOperand))throw new InvalidOperationException("Active inherited profile operand missing: ValidationProfileRoot");
        if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(environmentOperand))throw new InvalidOperationException("Active inherited profile operand missing: WARLINE_VALIDATION_SAVE_ROOT");
        string inherited=Canonical(inheritedOperand);
        string parent=Canonical(Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(),"aria-play-validation-profile"));
        if(Canonical(Path.GetDirectoryName(inherited))!=parent || !System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(Path.GetFileName(inherited),"^[0-9a-f]{32}$") || Canonical(environmentOperand)!=inherited)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Normal manual save is not owned initialized inherited QA profile.");
        var property=typeof(SkirmishNativePlayerSaveService).GetProperty("SavePath",BindingFlags.Static|BindingFlags.NonPublic)??throw new MissingMemberException("Native SavePath missing.");
        string pathOperand=(string)property.GetValue(null);
        if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(pathOperand))throw new InvalidOperationException("Active inherited profile operand missing: NativeSavePath");
        string path=Canonical(pathOperand);
        if(path!=Canonical(Path.Combine(inherited,"skirmish-s005-native.json")))throw new InvalidOperationException("Native inherited SavePath differs.");
        if(saveRoot.Length==0)
        {
            if(File.Exists(path))throw new InvalidOperationException("Refuse prior native checkpoint in unique normal profile.");
            saveRoot=inherited;nativeSavePath=path;
            Append(new Row{stage="InheritedSaveProfile",saveProfileRoot=saveRoot,nativeSavePath=nativeSavePath,inheritedProfileVerified=true,reason="Existing native initialized unique GUID QA profile; environment unchanged"});
        }
        else if(saveRoot!=inherited || nativeSavePath!=path)throw new InvalidOperationException("Native initialized SavePath ownership changed.");
    }
    static object Field(object value,string name)=>value.GetType().GetField(name,BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(value);
    static object Managed(EntityManager em,Entity entity,System.Type type)
    {
        foreach(var method in typeof(EntityManager).GetMethods(BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.Instance))
            if(method.Name=="GetComponentObject" && method.IsGenericMethodDefinition && method.GetParameters().Length==1 && method.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType==typeof(Entity))
                return method.MakeGenericMethod(type).Invoke(em,new object[]{entity});
        throw new MissingMethodException("Native managed read-only component accessor missing.");
    }
    static T SnapshotComponent<T>(object snapshot) where T:unmanaged,IComponentData
    {
        var type=typeof(SkirmishNativePlayerSaveService).Assembly.GetType("Game.Runtime.SkirmishNativeCheckpointParts",true);
        foreach(var method in type.GetMethods(BindingFlags.Static|BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.NonPublic))
            if(method.Name=="TryReadComponent" && method.IsGenericMethodDefinition)
            {
                object[] args={Field(snapshot,"SessionParts"),null};
                if(!(bool)method.MakeGenericMethod(typeof(T)).Invoke(null,args))throw new InvalidOperationException("Native checkpoint missing "+typeof(T).Name);
                return (T)args[1];
            }
        throw new MissingMethodException("Native checkpoint component decoder missing.");
    }
    static T[] SnapshotBuffer<T>(object snapshot) where T:unmanaged,IBufferElementData
    {
        var type=typeof(SkirmishNativePlayerSaveService).Assembly.GetType("Game.Runtime.SkirmishNativeCheckpointParts",true);
        foreach(var method in type.GetMethods(BindingFlags.Static|BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.NonPublic))
            if(method.Name=="TryReadBuffer" && method.IsGenericMethodDefinition)
            {object[] args={Field(snapshot,"SessionParts"),null};if(!(bool)method.MakeGenericMethod(typeof(T)).Invoke(null,args))throw new InvalidOperationException("Native checkpoint buffer missing "+typeof(T).Name);return (T[])args[1];}
        throw new MissingMethodException("Native checkpoint buffer decoder missing.");
    }
    static void ReceiptRow(string stage,SkirmishProductionReservation receipt,string attempt,float elapsed,bool paused,bool recovered)
    {Append(new Row{stage=stage,attempt=attempt,reservation=receipt.ReservationId,faction=receipt.FactionId,role=receipt.Role.ToString(),phase=receipt.Phase.ToString(),producer=receipt.ProducerRuntimeId,materialsPaid=receipt.MaterialsPaid,members=receipt.MemberCount,deliveredMembers=receipt.DeliveredMembers,remainingMembers=receipt.RemainingMembers,supplyCost=receipt.SupplyCost,elapsed=elapsed,paused=paused,recoveredNativeComplete=recovered,actorStableId=trackedPaidStable});}
    static void ObserveCheckpointFile()
    {
        if(saveRoot.Length==0 || checkpointArchived || !File.Exists(nativeSavePath))return;
        EnsureInheritedProfile();
        var method=typeof(SkirmishNativePlayerSaveService).GetMethod("TryRead",BindingFlags.Static|BindingFlags.NonPublic)??throw new MissingMethodException("Native checkpoint TryRead missing.");
        object[] args={"S005",null,null};
        if(!(bool)method.Invoke(null,args) || args[1]==null || (string)Field(args[1],"SessionId")!=initialAttempt)throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual saved native checkpoint attempt invalid.");
        var clock=SnapshotComponent<SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent>(args[1]);
        var research=SnapshotComponent<SkirmishResearchStateComponent>(args[1]);
        if(clock.Paused==0 || Math.Abs(clock.ElapsedSeconds-lastPausedElapsed)>.05f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Saved native paused clock differs from observed pause.");
        if(trackedPaidReceipt==0||trackedPaidStable.Length==0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual paid actor identity never observed before Save.");
        int savedReceiptCount=0;foreach(var receipt in SnapshotBuffer<SkirmishProductionReservation>(args[1]))if(receipt.ReservationId==trackedPaidReceipt){savedReceiptCount++;ReceiptRow("NativeSavedPaidReceipt",receipt,initialAttempt,clock.ElapsedSeconds,true,false);}
        if(savedReceiptCount!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Unique actual paid receipt absent from native saved buffer.");
        File.Copy(nativeSavePath,CheckpointArchive);checkpointArchived=true;
        Append(new Row{stage="ActualNativeCheckpoint",attempt=initialAttempt,nativeSavePath=nativeSavePath,saveProfileRoot=saveRoot,checkpointArchive=CheckpointArchive,inheritedProfileVerified=true,checkpointNativeValidated=true,savedElapsed=clock.ElapsedSeconds,paused=clock.Paused!=0,runtimeReadiness=research.Readiness.ToString(),readinessValue=(int)research.Readiness,infantryUpgrade=research.InfantryWeapons,vehicleUpgrade=research.VehicleProtection,aircraftUpgrade=research.AircraftEfficiency});
    }
    static bool postTerminalProfileReported;
    static void ObserveResearchRecovery(EntityManager em,Entity session,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state)
    {
        if(state.SpawnComplete==0 || !em.HasComponent<SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent>(session) || !em.HasComponent<SkirmishResearchStateComponent>(session))return;
        if(em.HasComponent<SkirmishMatchState>(session))
        {
            var terminal=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishMatchState>(session);
            if(terminal.Phase==SkirmishPhase.Finished)
            {
                if(!postTerminalProfileReported){postTerminalProfileReported=true;Append(new Row{stage="PostTerminalProfileReleased",attempt=state.SessionId.ToString(),outcome=terminal.Outcome.ToString(),reason="Preserve natural terminal; inherited QA profile may be released after result; no readiness inferred"});}
                return;
            }
        }
        EnsureInheritedProfile();
        var clock=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent>(session);
        S005PaidProducerCensus1619.Observe(em,session,state,trackedPaidReceipt,clock);
        bool recoveryPresent=em.HasComponent(session,ComponentType.ReadOnly(RecoveryType));
        bool recovered=recoveryPresent && (bool)Field(Managed(em,session,RecoveryType),"Complete");
        if(clock.Paused!=0)lastPausedElapsed=clock.ElapsedSeconds;
        var research=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishResearchStateComponent>(session);
        bool simulationActive=false;
        using(var runtime=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(RuntimeGameplayStateComponent)))
        {if(runtime.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Unique native gameplay state missing.");simulationActive=runtime.GetSingleton<RuntimeGameplayStateComponent>().SimulationActive!=0;}
        int actorCount=0,hp=0,entityIndex=0,entityVersion=0;string stable="";uint receiptId=0;
        using(var actors=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(SkirmishUnitRoleComponent),typeof(UnitHealth)))
        using(var entities=actors.ToEntityArray(Unity.Collections.Allocator.Temp))foreach(var actor in entities)
        {
            var owner=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);
            if(owner.SessionId!=state.SessionId || owner.FactionId!=1 || owner.CapacityReleased!=0 || owner.ReservationId==0 || em.GetComponentData<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor).Role!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.ApcHeavy || em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(actor).Current<=0)continue;
            actorCount++;hp=em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(actor).Current;stable=owner.StableObjectId.ToString();receiptId=owner.ReservationId;entityIndex=actor.Index;entityVersion=actor.Version;
        }
        if(trackedPaidReceipt==0 && actorCount==1){trackedPaidReceipt=receiptId;trackedPaidStable=stable;}
        if(trackedPaidReceipt!=0)
        {
            if(em.HasBuffer<SkirmishProductionReservation>(session))foreach(var item in em.GetBuffer<SkirmishProductionReservation>(session,true))if(item.ReservationId==trackedPaidReceipt)ReceiptRow("NativePaidReceiptState",item,state.SessionId.ToString(),clock.ElapsedSeconds,clock.Paused!=0,recovered);
            using var allPaid=em.CreateEntityQuery(new EntityQueryDesc{All=new[]{ComponentType.ReadOnly<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(),ComponentType.ReadOnly<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(),ComponentType.ReadOnly<UnitHealth>()},Options=EntityQueryOptions.IncludeDisabledEntities});using var allActors=allPaid.ToEntityArray(Unity.Collections.Allocator.Temp);
            foreach(var actor in allActors){var own=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);if(own.SessionId!=state.SessionId||own.FactionId!=1||own.ReservationId!=trackedPaidReceipt)continue;Append(new Row{stage="NativePaidActorState",attempt=state.SessionId.ToString(),actorStableId=own.StableObjectId.ToString(),reservation=own.ReservationId,actorHealth=em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(actor).Current,capacityReleased=own.CapacityReleased,role=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor).Role.ToString(),elapsed=clock.ElapsedSeconds,paused=clock.Paused!=0,recoveredNativeComplete=recovered,actorEntityIndex=actor.Index,actorEntityVersion=actor.Version});}
        }
        var row=new Row{stage="NativeManualRuntime",attempt=state.SessionId.ToString(),elapsed=clock.ElapsedSeconds,paused=clock.Paused!=0,simulationActive=simulationActive,runtimeReadiness=research.Readiness.ToString(),readinessValue=(int)research.Readiness,faction=research.FactionId,infantryUpgrade=research.InfantryWeapons,vehicleUpgrade=research.VehicleProtection,aircraftUpgrade=research.AircraftEfficiency,recoveredNativeComplete=recovered,actorCount=actorCount,actorHealth=hp,actorStableId=stable,reservation=receiptId,actorEntityIndex=entityIndex,actorEntityVersion=entityVersion};
        var bank=SkirmishMaterialsService.FindEconomy(em,1);
        if(bank!=Entity.Null && em.HasComponent<FactionTacticalMaterialsComponent>(bank))
        {var stock=em.GetComponentData<FactionTacticalMaterialsComponent>(bank);row.bankCurrent=stock.Current;row.bankCapacity=stock.Capacity;row.lifetimeSpent=stock.LifetimeSpent;row.lifetimeFabricated=stock.LifetimeFabricated;row.lifetimeImported=stock.LifetimeImported;row.lifetimeRewarded=stock.LifetimeRewarded;row.lifetimeExported=stock.LifetimeExported;}
        Append(row);
        if(recovered && !restoredSeen){restoredSeen=true;Append(new Row{stage="NativeRecoveredSameAttempt",attempt=state.SessionId.ToString(),elapsed=clock.ElapsedSeconds,readinessValue=(int)research.Readiness,runtimeReadiness=research.Readiness.ToString(),recoveredNativeComplete=true,actorStableId=stable,reservation=receiptId,actorCount=actorCount,actorHealth=hp,actorEntityIndex=entityIndex,actorEntityVersion=entityVersion,infantryUpgrade=research.InfantryWeapons,vehicleUpgrade=research.VehicleProtection,aircraftUpgrade=research.AircraftEfficiency});}
        if(em.HasBuffer<SkirmishResearchQueueItem>(session))foreach(var item in em.GetBuffer<SkirmishResearchQueueItem>(session,true))
            if(item.FactionId==1 && item.Kind==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishResearchKind.Readiness)
                Append(new Row{stage="NativePaidResearch",attempt=state.SessionId.ToString(),faction=item.FactionId,researchId=item.ResearchId,researchKind=item.Kind.ToString(),researchPhase=item.Phase.ToString(),researchProducer=item.Producer.ToString(),materialsPaid=item.MaterialsPaid,researchRemainingSeconds=item.RemainingSeconds,elapsed=clock.ElapsedSeconds,readinessValue=(int)research.Readiness,runtimeReadiness=research.Readiness.ToString(),expectedResearchSeconds=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishResearchCosts.ReadinessEstablishedSeconds,expectedCost=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishResearchCosts.ReadinessEstablishedMaterials,recoveredNativeComplete=recovered});
    }
    static void ObserveMainMenuDestination()
    {
        if(!resultSeen || !finishedVictorySeen || witnessedAttempt.Length==0 || !UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadShellState(out var shell) || shell.ActiveRoute!=Game.UI.Contracts.UIRoute.MainMenu || shell.IsTransitionRunning)return;
        var world=World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;if(world==null || !world.IsCreated)return;
        using(var query=world.EntityManager.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent)))if(query.CalculateEntityCount()!=0)return;
        var shellView=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<UIShellView>();
        bool obscured=shellView!=null && shellView.TryGetRegion(UIShellRegionId.LoadingLayer,out var loading) && loading.CanvasGroup!=null && loading.CanvasGroup.alpha>.01f;
        if(obscured)return;
        foreach(var route in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<UIShellRouteButtonView>(FindObjectsInactive.Exclude,FindObjectsSortMode.None))
        {
            var button=route.GetComponent<Button>();var card=button?.transform.parent;
            if(button==null || card==null || card.name!="Card_Skirmish" || button.name!="Hotspot" || card.Find("Hotspot")!=button.transform ||
                route.Intent!=Game.UI.Contracts.UiShellRouteIntent.OpenMenuRoute || route.Route!=Game.UI.Contracts.UIRoute.QuickCustomSetup || !route.PushHistory || !Presented(button))continue;
            var text=card.Find("Title")?.GetComponent<TMP_Text>();
            if(text==null || text.transform.parent!=card || !Rendered(text) || !CopyMatches(text,"SKIRMISH"))continue;
            BoundText("MainMenuSkirmishControl",text,card);
            Append(new Row{stage="NativeMainMenuDestination",attempt=witnessedAttempt,finished=true,frozenVictory=true,renderedVictoryWitnessed=true,outcome="Victory",shellRoute=shell.ActiveRoute.ToString(),presentedControl=true,renderedBody=true,controlPath=PathOf(button.transform),controlText=Text(text),unobscuredDestination=true,destinationSessionAbsent=true,exactMainMenuOwner=true,exactMainMenuTitle=true,mainMenuIntent=route.Intent.ToString(),mainMenuRoute=route.Route.ToString(),mainMenuTitlePath=PathOf(text.transform),expectedMainMenuCaption=UiLocalizedText.CatalogLabel("SKIRMISH"),actualMainMenuCaption=text is RTLTMPro.RTLTextMeshPro rtl?rtl.OriginalText:text.text});returnSeen=true;
        }
    }

    static void ObserveRendered()
    {
        try
        {
            if(AnyCompanionFailed()){CloseAfterCompanionFailure();return;}
            S005PassengerCarrierIdentity1619.Observe("CanvasWillRenderCanvases");

            S005PackedRenderCensus1619.Observe();
            if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying)return;
            Canvas nativeCanvas=null;

                if(candidate.name=="GameUICanvas" && candidate.isRootCanvas){nativeCanvas=candidate;break;}
            if(nativeCanvas==null)return;
            Rect viewport=nativeCanvas.pixelRect,safe=Screen.safeArea;
            bool matched=Mathf.Abs(viewport.width-Screen.width)<=1f && Mathf.Abs(viewport.height-Screen.height)<=1f && safe.width>0 && safe.height>0;
            Append(new Row{stage="RenderedViewportContext",samplingPhase="CanvasWillRenderCanvases",ownerPath=PathOf(nativeCanvas.transform),nativeViewportMatched=matched,screenWidth=Screen.width,screenHeight=Screen.height,safeX=safe.x,safeY=safe.y,safeWidth=safe.width,safeHeight=safe.height,canvasX=viewport.x,canvasY=viewport.y,canvasWidth=viewport.width,canvasHeight=viewport.height});
            if(!matched)return; // Fail closed on foreign Editor-window coordinates; require a real native render viewport sample.
            if(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup<next)return;
            next=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup+.25d;
            foreach(var view in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<QuickCustomScreenView>(FindObjectsInactive.Exclude,FindObjectsSortMode.None))
            {
                if(!view.IsBaseAssaultSetup || view.SelectedScenarioId!="S005")continue;

                if(Rendered(title) && Rendered(objective) && !bindingSeen)setupSeen=true;
            }
            ObserveCheckpointFile();
            ObserveMainMenuDestination();
            var world=World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;
            if(world==null || !world.IsCreated)return;
            var em=world.EntityManager;
            bool nativeFrozenVictory=false; string nativeAttempt="";
            using(var query=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishResolvedSetupRecord),typeof(SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent)))
            {
                if(query.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)return;
                var session=query.GetSingletonEntity();var state=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(session);var setup=em.GetComponentObject<SkirmishResolvedSetupRecord>(session).Setup;
                if(setup.CatalogId!="S005")throw new InvalidOperationException("Observed foreign normal session");

                else if(initialAttempt!=nativeAttempt)throw new InvalidOperationException("Native Continue changed normal manual attempt identity.");
                ObserveResearchRecovery(em,session,state);
                if(em.HasComponent<SkirmishMatchState>(session) && em.HasComponent<SkirmishResultComponent>(session))
                {

                    nativeFrozenVictory=match.Phase==SkirmishPhase.Finished && match.Outcome==SkirmishOutcome.Victory && result.Frozen!=0 && result.Outcome==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishOutcomeKind.Victory && result.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId);
                }
                if(!bindingSeen)
                {

                {

                    if(!receiptStates.Add(key))continue;
                    int cost=0;foreach(var overlay in setup.RoleOverlays)if(overlay.RoleKind==receipt.Role){cost=overlay.MaterialsCost;break;}
                    Append(new Row{stage="NativePaidReservation",attempt=state.SessionId.ToString(),reservation=receipt.ReservationId,faction=receipt.FactionId,role=receipt.Role.ToString(),phase=receipt.Phase.ToString(),producer=receipt.ProducerRuntimeId,materialsPaid=receipt.MaterialsPaid,expectedCost=cost,members=receipt.MemberCount,deliveredMembers=receipt.DeliveredMembers});
                }
            }
            if(!UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadSkirmish(out var model))return;
            bool active=UiShellRuntimeGateway.ReadAriaPlay().Active;
            if(active)throw new InvalidOperationException("ARIA active during manual-only qualification.");
            foreach(var briefing in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<AriaTutorialBriefingView>(FindObjectsInactive.Exclude,FindObjectsSortMode.None))
            {

                bool title=Rendered(briefing.TitleText),body=Rendered(briefing.BodyText);

                if(title&&body&&!active)purposeSeen=true;
            }
            foreach(var button in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<Button>(FindObjectsInactive.Exclude,FindObjectsSortMode.None))
            {

                if(!active && presented && Rendered(label))manualPlaySeen=true;
            }
            foreach(var view in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<SkirmishMatchView>(FindObjectsInactive.Exclude,FindObjectsSortMode.None))
            {
                var strip=view.transform.Find("BaseObjectives");bool own=false,enemy=false,clock=false;
                if(strip!=null)foreach(var t in strip.GetComponentsInChildren<TMP_Text>()) BoundText("ObjectiveStrip",t,strip);
                var ownLabel=NativePlayerLabel.GetValue(view) as TMP_Text;
                var enemyLabel=NativeEnemyLabel.GetValue(view) as TMP_Text;
                var clockLabel=NativeClockLabel.GetValue(view) as TMP_Text;
                bool ownBinding=ownLabel!=null && view.PlayerFocusButton!=null && ownLabel.GetComponentInParent<Button>()==view.PlayerFocusButton;
                bool enemyBinding=enemyLabel!=null && view.EnemyFocusButton!=null && enemyLabel.GetComponentInParent<Button>()==view.EnemyFocusButton;
                bool clockBinding=clockLabel!=null && strip!=null && clockLabel.transform.parent==strip;
                string ownCaption=UiShellRuntimeGateway.Localization.IsRightToLeft ? "پایگاه شما" : "YOUR BASE";

                bool clockCurrent=modelNumeric.Length>0 && nativeNumeric==modelNumeric && CopyMatches(clockLabel,model.Clock);
                own=ownBinding && Rendered(ownLabel) && CopyMatches(ownLabel,ownCaption);
                enemy=enemyBinding && Rendered(enemyLabel) && CopyMatches(enemyLabel,enemyCaption);
                clock=clockBinding && Rendered(clockLabel) && clockCurrent;
                BoundText("PlayerBaseCaption",ownLabel,view.PlayerFocusButton?.transform);
                BoundText("EnemyBaseCaption",enemyLabel,view.EnemyFocusButton?.transform);
                BoundText("CurrentMatchClock",clockLabel,strip);
                Append(new Row{stage="NativeObjectiveStrip",ariaActive=active,finished=model.Finished,renderedPlayerBase=own,renderedEnemyBase=enemy,renderedClock=clock,playerBase=model.PlayerBase,enemyBase=model.EnemyBase,clock=model.Clock,modelObjective=model.Objective,exactPlayerBinding=ownBinding,exactEnemyBinding=enemyBinding,exactClockBinding=clockBinding,clockNumberPresentAndCurrent=clockCurrent,expectedPlayerCaption=ownCaption,expectedEnemyCaption=enemyCaption,actualPlayerCaption=Text(ownLabel),actualEnemyCaption=Text(enemyLabel),actualClockText=Text(clockLabel),nativeClockSource=nativeClockSource,modelClockNumber=modelNumeric,nativeClockNumber=nativeNumeric});
                if(model.Finished)
                {
                    var result=view.transform.Find("SkirmishResult/CampaignStyleResult");
                    if(result!=null)foreach(var text in result.GetComponentsInChildren<TMP_Text>())if(Rendered(text))
                    {BoundText("ResultText",text,result);bool victoryCopy=CopyMatches(text,"پیروزی");Append(new Row{stage="NativeRenderedResultText",localizedVictoryCopy=victoryCopy,title=Text(text),renderedTitle=true,finished=model.Finished,frozenVictory=nativeFrozenVictory,outcome=nativeFrozenVictory?"Victory":"",attempt=nativeAttempt,renderedVictoryWitnessed=nativeFrozenVictory && victoryCopy});if(model.Finished && nativeFrozenVictory && victoryCopy){resultSeen=finishedVictorySeen=true;witnessedAttempt=nativeAttempt;}}
                }
            }
        }
        catch(Exception error)
        {
            ObserverFailure(error);
        }
    }
}

    static void OnReceipt(string operation,uint id,bool verified)
    {
        if(!OwnsLease)return;
        if(phase==5)
        {
            Write(new Row{stage="TacticNativeReceipt",operation=operation,receipt=id,verified=verified,tacticStep=tacticStep});
            if(!verified||id==0){failure="Unverified native scout/direct command receipt: "+operation;return;}
            if((tacticStep<=3||tacticStep==98||tacticStep==99||tacticStep==100||tacticStep==101)&&operation=="SelectionOrder")return;
            if(tacticStep>=6&&tacticStep<=8&&(operation=="Scan"||operation=="ArmyScan")){tacticScanReceipt=id;return;}
            if(tacticStep>=13&&UnityEngine.Time.frameCount>=tacticTouchFrame)
            {
                int frame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount;
                if(tacticAttackEvents>=8){failure="Extra direct Barracks receipt after four exact pairs: "+operation;return;}
                if(tacticAttackEvents==0){tacticAttackFrame=frame;tacticAttackReceipt=id;}
                if(frame!=tacticAttackFrame||id!=tacticAttackReceipt){failure="Direct Barracks receipt frame/id diverged";return;}
                string expected=tacticAttackPending?"ArmyAttack":"AttackMove";
                if(operation!=expected){failure="Direct Barracks receipt order differs from four alternating native pairs: "+operation;return;}
                tacticAttackEvents++;
                if(tacticAttackPending)tacticAttackPairs++;
                tacticAttackPending=!tacticAttackPending;
                return;
            }
            failure="Unexpected native scout/direct receipt: "+operation;return;
        }
        if(operation=="ArmyAttack" && verified && id!=0 && id==receipt && UnityEngine.Time.frameCount==lastMoveReceiptFrame)
        {Write(new Row{stage="NativeReceiptAlias",operation=operation,receipt=id,verified=true,reason="PairedSameFrameAttackMoveScope"});return;}
        Write(new Row{stage="NativeReceipt",operation=operation,receipt=id,verified=verified});
        if(!verified||id==0||operation!="AttackMove"){failure="Unexpected/unverified native fallback receipt:"+operation;return;}
        acceptCount++;receipt=id;lastMoveReceiptFrame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount;
    }
    static void Restore(string reason)
    {
        if(!lease)return;
        if(reselectPending){var aborted=ReadCommandAdmission(false);WriteCommandAdmission(aborted,"PublicReselectionAborted",reason+";priorExpectedDispatch="+reselectDispatched+";priorAcceptedCommands="+reselectAccepted);reselectPending=false;}
        bool same=object.ReferenceEquals(Driver.GetValue(null),touch);Due.SetValue(null,originalDue);
        Write(new Row{stage="LeaseRestored",reason=reason,sameDriver=same,restoredExact=(double)Due.GetValue(null)==originalDue,originalDue=originalDue,receipt=receipt});
        lease=false;suspendedForDefense=routeReselectRequired=false;flankLease=reinforcementFlankLease=false;reinforcementLeg=0;phase=cameraStep=tacticStep=0;next=UnityEditor.EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup+20;
        if(!same)failure="Input owner changed during fallback";
    }

    }

    static bool Tap(UnityEngine.UI.Button b)
    {

    static bool Ground(out UnityEngine.Vector2 point)
    {
        point=default;var c=UnityEngine.Camera.main;if(c==null)return false;var p=c.WorldToScreenPoint(site);if(p.z<=0)return false;point=p;
        var popup=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudFullMapPopupView>(UnityEngine.FindObjectsInactive.Include);if(popup!=null&&popup.IsOpen)return false;
        var es=UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current;if(es==null)return false;var hits=new System.Collections.Generic.List<UnityEngine.EventSystems.RaycastResult>();
        es.RaycastAll(new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(es){position=point},hits);return UnityEngine.Screen.safeArea.Contains(point)&&hits.Count==0;
    }
    static bool PublicScanPoint(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em,out UnityEngine.Vector2 point)
    {
        point=default;using var grids=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.GridConfig));if(grids.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)return false;
        float c=grids.GetSingleton<Game.Components.GridConfig>().CellSize;if(c<=0||float.IsNaN(c)||float.IsInfinity(c))return false;
        // Each public center remains within two grid cells of the visible objective position;
        // the actual selected combat-unit scan radius is six cells.
        var offsets=new[]{UnityEngine.Vector2.zero,new UnityEngine.Vector2(0,-c),new UnityEngine.Vector2(-c,0),new UnityEngine.Vector2(c,0),new UnityEngine.Vector2(0,c),new UnityEngine.Vector2(-2*c,0),new UnityEngine.Vector2(0,-2*c)};
        foreach(var offset in offsets){site=objective+new UnityEngine.Vector3(offset.x,0,offset.y);if(Ground(out point))return true;}
        return false;
    }
    static bool ExactSelection(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em,System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<Unity.Entities.Entity> expected)
    {
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SelectedUnitTag));using var all=q.ToEntityArray(Unity.Collections.Allocator.Temp);
        if(all.Length!=expected.Count||expected.Count==0)return false;
        foreach(var e in expected)if(!em.Exists(e)||!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SelectedUnitTag>(e))return false;
        foreach(var e in all){bool found=false;for(int i=0;i<expected.Count;i++)if(expected[i]==e){found=true;break;}if(!found)return false;}
        return true;
    }
    static int LivingOriginal(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em,bool requireStopped)
    {

        {

            if(em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(e).Current<=0)continue;
            living++;if(requireStopped&&(!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.ManualMoveOrderTag>(e)||em.HasComponent<Game.Components.HoldPositionOrderTag>(e)||em.HasComponent<Game.Components.EngageTarget>(e)||em.HasComponent<Game.Components.AttackMoveOrder>(e)||!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitCombat>(e)||em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitCombat>(e).AutoEngage!=0))return -1;
        }
        return living;
    }
    static bool FindPaidScout(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em,Unity.Entities.Entity session,Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state)
    {
        if(!em.HasBuffer<Game.Components.SkirmishProductionReservation>(session))return false;
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishUnitRoleComponent),typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent),typeof(Game.Components.UnitHealth));using var es=q.ToEntityArray(Unity.Collections.Allocator.Temp);
        Unity.Entities.Entity found=Unity.Entities.Entity.Null;uint foundGroup=0,foundReceipt=0;
        foreach(var e in es)
        {
            var own=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(e);
            if(!own.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId)||own.FactionId!=1||own.IsStructure!=0||own.CapacityReleased!=0||own.ReservationId==0||em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(e).Current<=0||em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(e).Role!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.ApcHeavy)continue;
            uint group=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(e).GroupId;bool paid=false;
            foreach(var row in em.GetBuffer<Game.Components.SkirmishProductionReservation>(session,true))if(row.ReservationId==own.ReservationId&&row.ProductionGroupId==group&&row.FactionId==1&&row.Role==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.ApcHeavy&&row.Phase==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishReservationPhase.Live&&row.MaterialsPaid==280&&row.DeliveredMembers>0)paid=true;
            if(!paid)continue;if(found!=Unity.Entities.Entity.Null)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Ambiguous paid Heavy APC scout");found=e;foundGroup=group;foundReceipt=own.ReservationId;
        }
        if(found==Unity.Entities.Entity.Null)return false;tacticScout=found;tacticScoutGroup=foundGroup;tacticScoutReservation=foundReceipt;return true;
    }
    static bool ScoutCanScan(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em)
    {
        if(tacticScout==Unity.Entities.Entity.Null||!em.Exists(tacticScout)||!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitMove>(tacticScout)||!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitCombat>(tacticScout)||em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitCombat>(tacticScout).CanAttack==0)return false;
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.FocusedUnitUiReadModelComponent));
        if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)return false;
        var model=q.GetSingleton<Game.Components.FocusedUnitUiReadModelComponent>();
        return model.HasFocusedUnit!=0&&model.FocusedUnit==tacticScout&&model.OwnedByPlayer!=0&&model.CanScan!=0;
    }
    static bool FindCard(uint group,out UnityEngine.UI.Button button)
    {
        button=null;if(!Game.UI.Runtime.UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadMatchHudSquadTray(out var model))return false;
        var tray=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudSquadTrayView>();if(tray==null)return false;
        for(int i=0;i<5;i++){var c=model.GetCard(i);if(!c.Visible||c.GroupId!=group||c.AliveCount<=0)continue;if(button!=null)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Ambiguous public squad card");button=tray.VisibleCardButton(i);}
        return button!=null;
    }
    static bool PageForCard()
    {
        if(!Game.UI.Runtime.UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadExpandedSquadPage(out var page))return false;
        var tray=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudSquadTrayView>();if(tray==null)return false;
        if(page.PageCount<1||page.PageCount>12)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Public roster page count outside bounded scout tactic");
        if(tacticPagingBack&&page.PreviousPage){if(++tacticPages>page.PageCount*2)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Public roster page traversal exceeded bound");return Tap(tray.PreviousPageButton);}
        tacticPagingBack=false;
        if(page.NextPage){if(++tacticPages>page.PageCount*2)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Public roster page traversal exceeded bound");return Tap(tray.NextPageButton);}
        throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Paid scout/original Rocketeer card absent from all public roster pages");
    }
    static bool PointClear(UnityEngine.Vector2 point)
    {
        if(!UnityEngine.Screen.safeArea.Contains(point)||UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current==null)return false;
        var hits=new System.Collections.Generic.List<UnityEngine.EventSystems.RaycastResult>();var es=UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current;es.RaycastAll(new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(es){position=point},hits);
        var popup=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudFullMapPopupView>(UnityEngine.FindObjectsInactive.Include);
        return hits.Count==0&&(popup==null||!popup.IsOpen);
    }
    // Diagnostic-only observation. This never writes native gameplay state or sends another order.
    // Bits: 1 fresh accepted UI result, 2 wheel closed, 4 all four alive,
    // 8 ManualMove, 16 no Hold, 32 no EngageTarget, 64 no AttackMove,
    // 128 AutoEngage off, 256 near-stationary, 512 paired public Touch,
    // 1024 exact original selection, 2048 original actor identity/role.
    static void SampleStopDiagnostic(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em,Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState match)
    {
        if(!started||!lease||stopDiagnosticFinished||phase!=5||tacticStep!=1||tacticStopTouchFrame<=0||lastStopDiagnosticFrame==UnityEngine.Time.frameCount)return;
        int offset=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount-tacticStopTouchFrame;
        if(offset<0||offset>90)return;
        lastStopDiagnosticFrame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount;
        var row=new StopDiagnosticFrame{attempt=attempt,frame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount,offset=offset,actorCount=cohort.Count,acceptedCommands=Game.Runtime.AriaCommandEvidence.AcceptedCommands,dispatched=touch?.DispatchedGestures??0,completed=touch?.CompletedGestures??0,stopExpectedDispatched=stopExpectedDispatched,touchBusy=touch!=null&&touch.IsBusy,touchRunning=touch!=null&&touch.IsRunning,touchInterrupted=touch!=null&&touch.PlayerInterrupted,preTouchMessage=tacticStopFeedbackBefore,expectedMessage=Game.Configs.GameText.Get("tactical.feedback.stopped_selected_units","Stopped selected units.")};
        row.pairedTouch=touch!=null&&!touch.IsBusy&&stopExpectedDispatched>0&&touch.CompletedGestures==stopExpectedDispatched&&touch.DispatchedGestures==stopExpectedDispatched;
        var feedback=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.BattleHudRuntimeFeedbackView>();
        row.feedbackPresent=feedback!=null&&feedback.HasLastCommandResult;
        if(row.feedbackPresent){var result=feedback.LastCommandResult;row.feedbackAccepted=result.Accepted;row.feedbackMessage=result.Message??"";row.feedbackReason=result.ReasonCode.ToString();}
        row.feedbackFresh=row.feedbackPresent&&row.feedbackAccepted&&row.feedbackMessage==row.expectedMessage&&row.preTouchMessage!=row.expectedMessage;
        var controls=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchOverlayCommandControlsView>();
        var wheel=controls?.CommandWheelPanel;var stop=controls?.CommandWheelStopButton;
        row.wheelOpen=wheel!=null&&wheel.IsOpen;row.wheelListenersBound=wheel!=null&&wheel.HasBoundListeners;
        row.wheelPath=wheel!=null?CameraButtonPath(wheel.transform):"none";row.stopButtonPath=stop!=null?CameraButtonPath(stop.transform):"none";
        row.stopButtonActive=stop!=null&&stop.gameObject.activeInHierarchy;row.stopButtonInteractable=stop!=null&&stop.IsInteractable();
        if(row.stopButtonActive){var args=new object[]{stop,default(UnityEngine.Vector2)};row.stopButtonPresented=(bool)ReadPoint.Invoke(null,args);}
        var eventSystem=UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current;
        row.selectedObjectPath=eventSystem!=null&&eventSystem.currentSelectedGameObject!=null?CameraButtonPath(eventSystem.currentSelectedGameObject.transform):"none";
        row.allOriginalSelected=cohort.Count==4&&ExactSelection(em,cohort);
        row.allActorsNearStill=offset>=3;
        for(int ordinal=0;ordinal<cohort.Count;ordinal++)
        {
            var actor=cohort[ordinal];
            var a=new StopActorFrame{attempt=attempt,frame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount,offset=offset,actorOrdinal=ordinal,entityIndex=actor.Index,entityVersion=actor.Version,autoEngage=-1,stable=ids.TryGetValue(actor,out var originalId)?originalId:""};
            if(em.Exists(actor))
            {
                a.entityExists=true;
                a.ownerPresent=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);
                if(a.ownerPresent){var owner=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);a.ownerAttempt=owner.SessionId.ToString();a.ownerStable=owner.StableObjectId.ToString();a.ownerFaction=owner.FactionId;a.ownerMatches=a.ownerAttempt==attempt&&a.ownerStable==a.stable&&a.ownerFaction==1;}
                if(em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor))a.role=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor).Role.ToString();
                a.roleMatches=a.role=="Rocketeer";
                if(em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(actor)){a.health=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(actor).Current;a.alive=a.health>0;}
                a.selected=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SelectedUnitTag>(actor);
                a.manualMove=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.ManualMoveOrderTag>(actor);
                a.hold=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.HoldPositionOrderTag>(actor);
                a.engageTarget=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.EngageTarget>(actor);
                a.attackMove=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.AttackMoveOrder>(actor);
                a.unitCombat=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitCombat>(actor);
                if(a.unitCombat)a.autoEngage=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitCombat>(actor).AutoEngage;
                if(em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor))
                {
                    a.positionPresent=true;
                    var p=(UnityEngine.Vector3)em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor).Position;a.x=p.x;a.y=p.y;a.z=p.z;
                    if(stopPreviousPositions.TryGetValue(actor,out var previous))a.frameTravel=UnityEngine.Vector2.Distance(new UnityEngine.Vector2(p.x,p.z),new UnityEngine.Vector2(previous.x,previous.z));
                    stopPreviousPositions[actor]=p;
                }
            }
            System.IO.File.AppendAllText(StopDiagnosticEvidence,UnityEngine.JsonUtility.ToJson(a)+"\n");
            if(a.alive)row.liveCount++;
            if(a.selected)row.selectedCount++;
            if(a.alive&&a.manualMove&&!a.hold&&!a.engageTarget&&!a.attackMove&&a.unitCombat&&a.autoEngage==0)row.stoppedCount++;
            if(!a.alive||!a.positionPresent||!a.manualMove||a.hold||a.engageTarget||a.attackMove||!a.unitCombat||a.autoEngage!=0||a.frameTravel>.1f)row.allActorsNearStill=false;
            if(!a.ownerMatches||!a.roleMatches||!a.positionPresent)row.failedBitMask|=2048;
            if(!a.manualMove)row.failedBitMask|=8;
            if(a.hold)row.failedBitMask|=16;
            if(a.engageTarget)row.failedBitMask|=32;
            if(a.attackMove)row.failedBitMask|=64;
            if(!a.unitCombat||a.autoEngage!=0)row.failedBitMask|=128;
        }
        row.allOriginalAlive=cohort.Count==4&&row.liveCount==4;
        if(!row.feedbackFresh)row.failedBitMask|=1;
        if(wheel==null||row.wheelOpen)row.failedBitMask|=2;
        if(!row.allOriginalAlive)row.failedBitMask|=4;
        if(!row.allActorsNearStill)row.failedBitMask|=256;
        if(!row.pairedTouch)row.failedBitMask|=512;
        if(!row.allOriginalSelected)row.failedBitMask|=1024;
        System.IO.File.AppendAllText(StopDiagnosticEvidence,UnityEngine.JsonUtility.ToJson(row)+"\n");
        if(offset<90&&(row.failedBitMask!=0||offset<5))return;
        if(row.failedBitMask!=0)
        {
            row.stage="StopGateFailed";
            System.IO.File.AppendAllText(StopDiagnosticEvidence,UnityEngine.JsonUtility.ToJson(row)+"\n");
            throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Public Cease Fire did not satisfy bounded flat Stop gate; failedBitMask="+row.failedBitMask);
        }
        row.stage="StopAcceptedComplete";
        System.IO.File.AppendAllText(StopDiagnosticEvidence,UnityEngine.JsonUtility.ToJson(row)+"\n");
        stopDiagnosticFinished=true;
        if(feedback==null||!feedback.HasLastCommandResult)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Fresh Stop HUD feedback disappeared at flat gate completion");
        tacticStopFeedbackConfirmed=true;tacticStopFeedbackMessage=feedback.LastCommandResult.Message;
        tacticStopPositions.Clear();foreach(var actor in cohort){if(!em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor))throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Stopped Rocketeer lacks native position");tacticStopPositions[actor]=(UnityEngine.Vector3)em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor).Position;}
        TacticRow("OriginalRouteCohortStopped",em,match,"FlatStopGatePassed;offset="+offset);
        using(var sessions=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent),typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState)))
        {
            if(sessions.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Unique actual S005 session absent after Stop");
            var session=sessions.GetSingletonEntity();var state=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(session);
            if(state.SessionId.ToString()!=attempt||!FindPaidScout(em,session,state))throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Genuine paid Heavy APC scout unavailable under fixed budget");
        }
        tacticPages=0;tacticPagingBack=true;tacticStep=2;
    }
    static void RenderStopDiagnostic()
    {
        if(!started||!lease||phase!=5||tacticStep!=1||stopDiagnosticFinished||!UnityEditor.EditorApplication.isPlaying)return;
        try{var world=Unity.Entities.World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;if(world==null||!world.IsCreated)return;var em=world.EntityManager;using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState));if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)return;SampleStopDiagnostic(em,q.GetSingleton<Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState>());}
        catch(System.Exception error){failure="Stop frame observation failed: "+error;}
    }
    static bool TryCaptureDirectActorProof(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em,Unity.Entities.Entity target,Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state,Game.UI.Runtime.SkirmishMatchView hud)
    {
        tacticAttackIntentActors=tacticAttackPathActors=0;
        if(cohort.Count!=4||!em.Exists(target)||!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(target)||!em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(target)||hud==null||!hud.EnemyBaseTargetVisible)return false;
        var targetHealth=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(target).Current;
        if(targetHealth<=0)return false;
        var targetPosition=em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(target).Position;
        var rows=new System.Collections.Generic.List<DirectAttackActorRow>();
        for(int ordinal=0;ordinal<cohort.Count;ordinal++)
        {
            var actor=cohort[ordinal];
            if(!em.Exists(actor)||!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor)||!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor)||!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(actor)||!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SkirmishMoveIntentComponent>(actor))return false;
            var own=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);
            var role=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor).Role;
            var intent=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishMoveIntentComponent>(actor);
            var hp=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(actor).Current;
            string stable=own.StableObjectId.ToString();
            bool current=own.FactionId==1&&own.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId)&&ids.TryGetValue(actor,out var boundStable)&&boundStable==stable;
            bool selected=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SelectedUnitTag>(actor);
            bool exact=intent.Active!=0&&intent.Order==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishGroupOrderKind.Attack&&intent.AttackTarget==target&&UnityEngine.Vector2.Distance(new UnityEngine.Vector2(intent.DestinationX,intent.DestinationZ),new UnityEngine.Vector2(targetPosition.x,targetPosition.z))<.1f;
            if(!current||!selected||hp<=0||role!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.Rocketeer||!exact)return false;
            tacticAttackIntentActors++;
            if(!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.AttackMoveOrder>(actor))return false;
            var move=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.AttackMoveOrder>(actor);
            bool hasTarget=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitTarget>(actor),hasRequest=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitPathRequest>(actor),hasFollow=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitPathFollow>(actor);
            var cell=hasTarget?em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitTarget>(actor).Cell:default;
            var request=hasRequest?em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitPathRequest>(actor).Goal:default;
            float pathDistance=UnityEngine.Vector2.Distance(new UnityEngine.Vector2(move.Position.x,move.Position.z),new UnityEngine.Vector2(targetPosition.x,targetPosition.z));
            bool pathMatches=pathDistance<=32f&&((hasTarget&&cell.Equals(move.Destination))||(hasRequest&&request.Equals(move.Destination))||(hasFollow&&hasTarget));
            if(!pathMatches)return false;
            tacticAttackPathActors++;
            rows.Add(new DirectAttackActorRow{attempt=attempt,stable=stable,role=role.ToString(),ownerAttempt=own.SessionId.ToString(),ownerFaction=own.FactionId,frame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount,actorOrdinal=ordinal,entityIndex=actor.Index,entityVersion=actor.Version,health=hp,selected=selected,ownerCurrent=current,intentOrder=intent.Order.ToString(),intentActive=intent.Active,attackTargetExact=exact,intentX=intent.DestinationX,intentZ=intent.DestinationZ,targetStable=tacticBarracks.targetStable,targetIndex=target.Index,targetVersion=target.Version,targetHp=targetHealth,targetX=targetPosition.x,targetZ=targetPosition.z,visible=hud.EnemyBaseTargetVisible,attackMovePresent=true,pathX=move.Position.x,pathZ=move.Position.z,pathCellX=move.Destination.x,pathCellY=move.Destination.y,pathDistance=pathDistance,unitTargetPresent=hasTarget,targetCellX=cell.x,targetCellY=cell.y,pathRequestPresent=hasRequest,requestCellX=request.x,requestCellY=request.y,pathFollowPresent=hasFollow,pathMatches=pathMatches,receipt=tacticAttackReceipt,receiptFrame=tacticAttackFrame});
        }
        if(rows.Count!=4)return false;
        foreach(var row in rows)System.IO.File.AppendAllText(DirectAttackActorEvidence,UnityEngine.JsonUtility.ToJson(row)+"\n");
        tacticAttackTargetHp=targetHealth;
        return true;
    }
    static void TacticRow(string stage,Unity.Entities.EntityManager em,Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState match,string reason="")
    {
        int hp=tacticScout!=Unity.Entities.Entity.Null&&em.Exists(tacticScout)&&em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(tacticScout)?em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(tacticScout).Current:0;
        float range=0,distance=0,x=0,z=0;if(cohort.Count>0&&em.Exists(cohort[0])){var actor=cohort[0];if(em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitAttack>(actor))range=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitAttack>(actor).Range;if(em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor)){var p=em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor).Position;distance=UnityEngine.Vector2.Distance(new UnityEngine.Vector2(p.x,p.z),new UnityEngine.Vector2(objective.x,objective.z));}}
        if(hp>0&&em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(tacticScout)){var p=em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(tacticScout).Position;x=p.x;z=p.z;}
        var hud=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.SkirmishMatchView>();
        var targetPoint=new UnityEngine.Vector2(tacticBarracks.targetX,tacticBarracks.targetY);
        var controls=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchOverlayCommandControlsView>();
        var wheel=controls?.CommandWheelPanel;var stopButton=controls?.CommandWheelStopButton;var openButton=wheel?.OpenButton;
        var selectionPanel=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudSelectionPanelView>();
        var openArgs=new object[]{openButton,default(UnityEngine.Vector2)};
        bool openPresented=openButton!=null&&(bool)ReadPoint.Invoke(null,openArgs);
        var stopArgs=new object[]{stopButton,default(UnityEngine.Vector2)};
        bool stopPresented=stopButton!=null&&(bool)ReadPoint.Invoke(null,stopArgs);
        bool modeRead=Game.UI.Runtime.UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadMatchHudCommandState(out var mode);
        var selectedRoot=selectionPanel!=null&&SelectedSquadPanelField!=null?SelectedSquadPanelField.GetValue(selectionPanel) as UnityEngine.GameObject:null;
        using var selectedQuery=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SelectedUnitTag));int selectedTags=selectedQuery.CalculateEntityCount();
        string closeInference=tacticWheelWasOpen&&wheel!=null&&!wheel.IsOpen?(selectionPanel==null||!selectionPanel.HasWheelSelection?"SelectionPanelHiddenWhenWheelClosed":"WheelClosedWhileSelectionPresented"):"";
        Write(new Row{stage=stage,reason=reason,tacticStep=tacticStep,scoutIndex=tacticScout.Index,scoutVersion=tacticScout.Version,scoutHp=hp,scoutX=x,scoutZ=z,rocketeerLiving=LivingOriginal(em,false),rocketeerStopped=LivingOriginal(em,true),rocketeerRange=range,baseDistance=distance,goalX=site.x,goalZ=site.z,scoutCanScan=ScoutCanScan(em),scoutSelected=tacticScout!=Unity.Entities.Entity.Null&&ExactSelection(em,new[]{tacticScout}),selected=ExactSelection(em,cohort),visibleAtImpact=hud!=null&&hud.EnemyBaseTargetVisible,safeArea=tacticBarracks.targetIndex>0&&UnityEngine.Screen.safeArea.Contains(targetPoint),noUiHit=tacticBarracks.targetIndex>0&&PointClear(targetPoint),scoutGroup=tacticScoutGroup,scoutReservation=tacticScoutReservation,scanReceipt=tacticScanReceipt,attackReceipt=tacticAttackReceipt,attackEvents=tacticAttackEvents,attackPairs=tacticAttackPairs,attackFrame=tacticAttackFrame,attackIntentActors=tacticAttackIntentActors,attackPathActors=tacticAttackPathActors,attackTargetHp=tacticAttackTargetHp,stopFeedbackAccepted=tacticStopFeedbackConfirmed,stopFeedbackMessage=tacticStopFeedbackMessage,stopFeedbackBefore=tacticStopFeedbackBefore,wheelOpen=wheel!=null&&wheel.IsOpen,wheelWasOpen=tacticWheelWasOpen,wheelListenersBound=wheel!=null&&wheel.HasBoundListeners,uiHasWheelSelection=selectionPanel!=null&&selectionPanel.HasWheelSelection,selectedTagCount=selectedTags,selectionPanelActiveSelf=selectionPanel!=null&&selectionPanel.gameObject.activeSelf,selectionPanelActiveInHierarchy=selectionPanel!=null&&selectionPanel.gameObject.activeInHierarchy,selectedSquadPanelActiveSelf=selectedRoot!=null&&selectedRoot.activeSelf,selectedSquadPanelActiveInHierarchy=selectedRoot!=null&&selectedRoot.activeInHierarchy,selectedSquadPanelParentActive=selectedRoot!=null&&selectedRoot.transform.parent!=null&&selectedRoot.transform.parent.gameObject.activeInHierarchy,selectionPanelParent=selectionPanel!=null?CameraButtonPath(selectionPanel.transform.parent):"none",selectedSquadPanelParent=selectedRoot!=null?CameraButtonPath(selectedRoot.transform.parent):"none",wheelCloseInference=closeInference,openButtonActive=openButton!=null&&openButton.gameObject.activeInHierarchy,openButtonInteractable=openButton!=null&&openButton.IsInteractable(),openButtonPresented=openPresented,stopButtonActive=stopButton!=null&&stopButton.gameObject.activeInHierarchy,stopButtonInteractable=stopButton!=null&&stopButton.IsInteractable(),stopButtonPresented=stopPresented,modeRead=modeRead,commandMode=modeRead?mode.ActiveCommandMode.ToString():"Unavailable",targetStable=tacticBarracks.targetStable,targetIndex=tacticBarracks.targetIndex,targetVersion=tacticBarracks.targetVersion,targetX=tacticBarracks.targetX,targetY=tacticBarracks.targetY,elapsed=match.ElapsedSeconds});
    }
    static void RenderWheel()
    {
        if(!started||failed||!lease||phase!=5||tacticStep!=100||tacticWheelRenderSamples>=64)return;
        try
        {
            if(tacticWheelRenderFrame!=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount){tacticWheelRenderFrame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount;tacticWheelRenderFrameCount=0;}
            if(tacticWheelRenderFrameCount++>=2)return;
            var world=Unity.Entities.World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;if(world==null||!world.IsCreated)return;
            var em=world.EntityManager;using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState));if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)return;
            var match=q.GetSingleton<Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState>();
            var controls=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchOverlayCommandControlsView>();
            if(controls?.CommandWheelPanel?.IsOpen==true)tacticWheelWasOpen=true;
            tacticWheelRenderSamples++;TacticRow("CommandWheelCanvasSample",em,match);
        }
        catch(System.Exception error){failure="Rendered Commands-wheel observation failed: "+error;}
    }
    static void Tactic(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em,Unity.Entities.Entity session,Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state,Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState match,Game.UI.Runtime.SkirmishMatchView hud,double now)
    {
        if(now-tacticStartedAt>290)throw new System.TimeoutException("Bounded paid scout/direct Barracks tactic exceeded290s");
        if(tacticStep==1)return; // Flat per-actor Stop gate owns this bounded wait; then paid Scan resumes.
        if(LivingOriginal(em,false)!=cohort.Count)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Original route Rocketeer cohort lost before direct Barracks effect");
        if(tacticStep>=2&&tacticStep<=12)
        {
            if(LivingOriginal(em,true)!=cohort.Count)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Publicly stopped south cohort acquired an unrequested order before direct Barracks Attack");
            foreach(var actor in cohort){if(!tacticStopPositions.TryGetValue(actor,out var stopped)||!em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor))throw new System.InvalidOperationException("South cohort stop-position evidence lost");var p=(UnityEngine.Vector3)em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor).Position;if(UnityEngine.Vector2.Distance(new UnityEngine.Vector2(p.x,p.z),new UnityEngine.Vector2(stopped.x,stopped.z))>2f)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Publicly stopped south Rocketeer drifted before direct Barracks Attack");}
        }
        if(!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SkirmishBaseAssaultFactComponent>(session)||em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishBaseAssaultFactComponent>(session).PlayerDesignatedAlive==0)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Home base lost during scout/direct tactic");
        var controls=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchOverlayCommandControlsView>();var tray=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudSquadTrayView>();if(controls==null||tray==null)return;
        if(tacticStep==0)
        {
            if(!ExactSelection(em,cohort))throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Original route cohort not exclusively selected before Cease Fire");
            if(Tap(controls.SelectButton)){tacticSelectTouchFrame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount;tacticStep=98;TacticRow("PublicSelectModeTouchBeforeWheel",em,match);}
            else if(UnityEngine.Time.frameCount-tacticSelectTouchFrame>=10)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Public Select button unavailable before Commands wheel");
            return;
        }
        if(tacticStep==98)
        {
            if(!ExactSelection(em,cohort))throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Original Rocketeer selection changed during public Select-mode Touch");
            if(touch.CompletedGestures!=touch.DispatchedGestures||touch.IsBusy)return;
