    static bool FiniteClose(float n)=>!float.IsNaN(n)&&!float.IsInfinity(n);
    static bool CloseViewportMatches(UnityEngine.Canvas canvas)
    {
        if(canvas==null)return false;var bounds=canvas.rootCanvas.pixelRect;
        return FiniteClose(bounds.width)&&FiniteClose(bounds.height)&&bounds.width>0&&bounds.height>0&&UnityEngine.Screen.width>0&&UnityEngine.Screen.height>0&&UnityEngine.Mathf.Abs(bounds.width-UnityEngine.Screen.width)<=1&&UnityEngine.Mathf.Abs(bounds.height-UnityEngine.Screen.height)<=1;
    }
    static bool PresentedClosePoint(UnityEngine.UI.Button button,out UnityEngine.Vector2 point,out string firstHit,out bool nativeCenter)
    {
        point=default;firstHit="";nativeCenter=false;
        var es=UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current;var rect=button!=null?button.transform as UnityEngine.RectTransform:null;var canvas=button!=null?button.GetComponentInParent<UnityEngine.Canvas>():null;var root=canvas!=null?canvas.rootCanvas:null;var camera=canvas!=null&&canvas.renderMode!=UnityEngine.RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay?canvas.worldCamera:null;
        double now=UnityEditor.EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;bool census=now>=(renderedCloseContext?nextRenderedGeometry:nextEditorGeometry);if(census){if(renderedCloseContext)nextRenderedGeometry=now+1;else nextEditorGeometry=now+1;}
        bool usable=button!=null&&button.IsActive()&&button.IsInteractable()&&rect!=null&&es!=null&&es.isActiveAndEnabled;
        if(usable){object[] native={button,default(UnityEngine.Vector2)};nativeCenter=(bool)ReadPoint.Invoke(null,native);}
        var uvs=new[]{new UnityEngine.Vector2(.5f,.5f),new UnityEngine.Vector2(.25f,.5f),new UnityEngine.Vector2(.75f,.5f),new UnityEngine.Vector2(.5f,.25f),new UnityEngine.Vector2(.5f,.75f),new UnityEngine.Vector2(.25f,.25f),new UnityEngine.Vector2(.75f,.25f),new UnityEngine.Vector2(.25f,.75f),new UnityEngine.Vector2(.75f,.75f)};
        for(int i=0;i<(rect!=null?uvs.Length:1);i++)
        {
            var world=rect!=null?rect.TransformPoint(new UnityEngine.Vector3(UnityEngine.Mathf.Lerp(rect.rect.xMin,rect.rect.xMax,uvs[i].x),UnityEngine.Mathf.Lerp(rect.rect.yMin,rect.rect.yMax,uvs[i].y),0)):default;
            var candidate=rect!=null?UnityEngine.RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(camera,world):default;
            var safe=UnityEngine.Screen.safeArea;var cb=root!=null?root.pixelRect:default;bool finite=FiniteClose(candidate.x)&&FiniteClose(candidate.y);bool positive=rect!=null&&FiniteClose(rect.rect.width)&&FiniteClose(rect.rect.height)&&rect.rect.width>0&&rect.rect.height>0;bool viewport=CloseViewportMatches(canvas);
            bool inSafe=finite&&safe.Contains(candidate),inside=finite&&rect!=null&&UnityEngine.RectTransformUtility.RectangleContainsScreenPoint(rect,candidate,camera);
            var hits=new System.Collections.Generic.List<UnityEngine.EventSystems.RaycastResult>();bool ray=usable&&finite&&positive&&inSafe&&inside&&(!renderedCloseContext||viewport);
            if(ray)es.RaycastAll(new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(es){position=candidate},hits);
            bool owned=hits.Count>0&&(hits[0].gameObject==button.gameObject||hits[0].gameObject.transform.IsChildOf(button.transform));string hit=hits.Count>0?CameraButtonPath(hits[0].gameObject.transform):ray?"none":"";
            if(census||owned)System.IO.File.AppendAllText(GeometryEvidence,UnityEngine.JsonUtility.ToJson(new CloseGeometryRow{attempt=attempt,samplingPhase=renderedCloseContext?"CanvasWillRenderCanvases":"EditorUpdate",frame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount,candidateIndex=rect!=null?i:-1,buttonPath=button!=null?CameraButtonPath(button.transform):"none",canvasPath=root!=null?CameraButtonPath(root.transform):"none",renderMode=canvas!=null?canvas.renderMode.ToString():"none",cameraPath=camera!=null?CameraButtonPath(camera.transform):"none",eventSystemPath=es!=null?CameraButtonPath(es.transform):"none",buttonExists=button!=null,buttonActive=button!=null&&button.IsActive(),buttonInteractable=button!=null&&button.IsInteractable(),rectExists=rect!=null,canvasExists=canvas!=null,cameraExists=camera!=null,eventSystemExists=es!=null,eventSystemActive=es!=null&&es.isActiveAndEnabled,screenWidth=UnityEngine.Screen.width,screenHeight=UnityEngine.Screen.height,safeX=safe.x,safeY=safe.y,safeWidth=safe.width,safeHeight=safe.height,canvasX=cb.x,canvasY=cb.y,canvasWidth=cb.width,canvasHeight=cb.height,rectX=rect!=null?rect.rect.x:0,rectY=rect!=null?rect.rect.y:0,rectWidth=rect!=null?rect.rect.width:0,rectHeight=rect!=null?rect.rect.height:0,pointX=candidate.x,pointY=candidate.y,worldX=world.x,worldY=world.y,worldZ=world.z,finitePoint=finite,positiveRect=positive,positiveCanvas=cb.width>0&&cb.height>0,viewportMatched=viewport,inSafeArea=inSafe,insideButton=inside,raycastAttempted=ray,hitCount=hits.Count,frontOwned=owned,firstHit=hit})+"\n");
            if(ray)firstHit=hit;if(!owned)continue;point=candidate;return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    static void RenderClose()
    {
        try
        {
            if(!started||failed||!lease||cameraStep!=3||lastCloseRenderFrame==UnityEngine.Time.frameCount||!UnityEditor.EditorApplication.isPlaying||!S005EarlyRoute1619.PaidReady)return;
            lastCloseRenderFrame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount;double now=UnityEditor.EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;
            if(now>deadline||now<next||touch==null||touch.IsBusy)return;
            if(!object.ReferenceEquals(Driver.GetValue(null),touch)||!touch.IsRunning||touch.PlayerInterrupted||touch.UnexpectedSamples!=0||touch.PhysicalInterventions!=0||!UnityEngine.Mathf.Approximately(UnityEngine.Time.timeScale,1)||Game.UI.Runtime.UiShellRuntimeGateway.ReadAriaPlay().Active)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Rendered Close input ownership/normal speed changed");
            var w=Unity.Entities.World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;if(w==null||!w.IsCreated)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Rendered Close world absent");var em=w.EntityManager;
            using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent),typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState));if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Rendered Close unique session absent");var e=q.GetSingletonEntity();var state=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(e);var match=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState>(e);
            if(state.CatalogId.ToString()!="S005"||state.SessionId.ToString()!=attempt)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Rendered Close attempt changed");if(match.Phase!=Game.Components.SkirmishPhase.Playing)return;
            Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudFullMapPopupView popup=null;int count=0;foreach(var p in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudFullMapPopupView>(UnityEngine.FindObjectsInactive.Include))if(p.IsOpen){popup=p;count++;}if(count!=1)return;
            var button=popup.CloseAction;renderedCloseContext=true;bool admitted=PresentedClosePoint(button,out var point,out var hit,out var nativeCenter);ObserveClose("RenderedCloseAdmission",popup,button,point,admitted,hit,nativeCenter);
            if(admitted&&Gesture(point,point))ObserveClose("PublicCloseTouch",popup,button,point,true,hit,nativeCenter);
        }
        catch(System.Exception ex){failure="Rendered Close failure: "+ex;}
        finally{renderedCloseContext=false;}
    }

        {
            var p=camera.WorldToScreenPoint(site);r.groundX=p.x;r.groundY=p.y;r.groundDepth=p.z;r.groundFinite=FiniteClose(p.x)&&FiniteClose(p.y)&&FiniteClose(p.z);r.groundInFront=r.groundFinite&&p.z>0;r.groundInSafeArea=r.groundFinite&&safe.Contains(new UnityEngine.Vector2(p.x,p.y));r.groundRaycastAttempted=r.eventSystemActive&&r.groundInFront&&r.groundInSafeArea&&!r.popupOpen;
            if(r.groundRaycastAttempted){var hits=new System.Collections.Generic.List<UnityEngine.EventSystems.RaycastResult>();es.RaycastAll(new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(es){position=new UnityEngine.Vector2(p.x,p.y)},hits);r.groundHitCount=hits.Count;r.groundNoUiHit=hits.Count==0;r.groundFirstHit=hits.Count>0?CameraButtonPath(hits[0].gameObject.transform):"none";}
        }
        r.groundAdmitted=r.viewportMatched&&r.cameraExists&&r.groundFinite&&r.groundInFront&&r.groundInSafeArea&&!r.popupOpen&&r.groundRaycastAttempted&&r.groundNoUiHit;
        return r;
    }
    static void WriteCommandAdmission(CommandAdmissionRow row,string stage,string reason="")
    {row.stage=stage;row.reason=reason;System.IO.File.AppendAllText(CommandEvidence,UnityEngine.JsonUtility.ToJson(row)+"\n");}
    static void ObserveCommands(bool rendered)
    {
        if(UnityEditor.EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup<nextCommandCensus)return;nextCommandCensus=UnityEditor.EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup+1;WriteCommandAdmission(ReadCommandAdmission(rendered),"Admission");
    }
    static bool reselectPending;
    static uint reselectDispatched;
    static int reselectAccepted;
    static void ReadCohortSelection(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em,CommandAdmissionRow r)
    {
        r.acceptedCommands=Game.Runtime.AriaCommandEvidence.AcceptedCommands;var stable=new System.Collections.Generic.List<string>();
        foreach(var actor in cohort)
        {
            if(!em.Exists(actor))continue;
            if(!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor)||!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(actor))throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Tracked route actor ownership/health lost");
            var o=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);
            if(o.SessionId.ToString()!=attempt||o.FactionId!=1||o.StableObjectId.ToString()!=ids[actor])throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Tracked route actor identity changed");
            if(em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(actor).Current<=0)continue;
            if(o.CapacityReleased!=0||!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(actor))throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Living route actor released or membership absent");
            uint group=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(actor).GroupId;
            if(group==0||(r.cohortGroup!=0&&r.cohortGroup!=group))throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Original route cohort group identity changed");r.cohortGroup=group;var actualRole=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor)?em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor).Role.ToString():"Missing";if(r.cohortRole.Length==0)r.cohortRole=actualRole;else if(r.cohortRole!=actualRole)r.cohortRole="Mixed";r.cohortLiving++;stable.Add(o.StableObjectId.ToString());if(em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SelectedUnitTag>(actor))r.cohortSelected++;
        }
        stable.Sort(System.StringComparer.Ordinal);r.cohortIds=string.Join("|",stable);
        using var query=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SelectedUnitTag),typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(Game.Components.UnitHealth));using var selected=query.ToEntityArray(Unity.Collections.Allocator.Temp);
        foreach(var actor in selected){var o=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);if(o.SessionId.ToString()==attempt&&o.FactionId==1&&em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(actor).Current>0&&!cohort.Contains(actor))r.foreignSelected++;}
        using var membersQuery=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent),typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(Game.Components.UnitHealth));using var members=membersQuery.ToEntityArray(Unity.Collections.Allocator.Temp);
        foreach(var actor in members){var o=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);if(o.SessionId.ToString()==attempt&&o.FactionId==1&&em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(actor).Current>0&&em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(actor).GroupId==r.cohortGroup&&!cohort.Contains(actor))throw new System.InvalidOperationException("New living member joined original route group");}
        r.cohortExact=r.cohortLiving>0&&r.cohortSelected==r.cohortLiving&&r.foreignSelected==0;
    }
    static bool EnsurePublicCohortSelection(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em,CommandAdmissionRow r)
    {
        ReadCohortSelection(em,r);
        if(r.foreignSelected!=0)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Foreign live selected cohort before route command");
        if(reselectPending)
        {
            if(touch.CompletedGestures!=reselectDispatched||touch.DispatchedGestures!=reselectDispatched||r.acceptedCommands!=reselectAccepted)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Public reselection touch/order continuity failed");
            if(!r.cohortExact)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Completed public card reselection did not select exact original live cohort");
            WriteCommandAdmission(r,"PublicReselectionObserved","ExactOriginalLivingRocketeerCohort; no troop-order receipt generated");reselectPending=false;
        }
        if(r.cohortExact)return true;
        if(r.cohortLiving==0)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("No original living route cohort to reselect");
        foreach(var actor in cohort)if(em.Exists(actor)&&em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(actor)&&em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(actor).Current>0&&(!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor)||em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor).Role!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.Rocketeer))throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Public route reselection authorized only for original Rocketeer cohort");
        var tray=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudSquadTrayView>();if(tray==null||!Game.UI.Runtime.UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadMatchHudSquadTray(out var model))return false;
        UnityEngine.UI.Button button=null;for(int i=0;i<5;i++){var card=model.GetCard(i);if(!card.Visible||card.GroupId!=r.cohortGroup)continue;if(button!=null)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Ambiguous original group public card");button=tray.VisibleCardButton(i);r.cardSlot=i;r.cardGroup=card.GroupId;r.cardAlive=card.AliveCount;}
        if(button==null)return false;if(r.cardAlive!=r.cohortLiving)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Presented card living count differs from original cohort");
        var rect=button.transform as UnityEngine.RectTransform;var canvas=button.GetComponentInParent<UnityEngine.Canvas>();var cam=canvas!=null&&canvas.renderMode!=UnityEngine.RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay?canvas.worldCamera:null;
        r.selectionButtonPath=CameraButtonPath(button.transform);r.selectionActive=button.IsActive();r.selectionInteractable=button.IsInteractable();r.selectionViewportMatched=CloseViewportMatches(canvas);
        if(rect!=null){var point=UnityEngine.RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(cam,rect.TransformPoint(rect.rect.center));r.selectionX=point.x;r.selectionY=point.y;r.selectionFinite=FiniteClose(point.x)&&FiniteClose(point.y);r.selectionPositive=FiniteClose(rect.rect.width)&&FiniteClose(rect.rect.height)&&rect.rect.width>0&&rect.rect.height>0;r.selectionInSafeArea=r.selectionFinite&&UnityEngine.Screen.safeArea.Contains(point);r.selectionInside=r.selectionFinite&&UnityEngine.RectTransformUtility.RectangleContainsScreenPoint(rect,point,cam);var es=UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current;if(es!=null&&es.isActiveAndEnabled&&r.selectionFinite&&r.selectionPositive&&r.selectionInSafeArea&&r.selectionInside){var hits=new System.Collections.Generic.List<UnityEngine.EventSystems.RaycastResult>();es.RaycastAll(new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(es){position=point},hits);r.selectionFirstHit=hits.Count>0?CameraButtonPath(hits[0].gameObject.transform):"none";r.selectionFrontOwned=hits.Count>0&&(hits[0].gameObject==button.gameObject||hits[0].gameObject.transform.IsChildOf(button.transform));}}
        WriteCommandAdmission(r,"PublicReselectionAdmission");
        if(!r.selectionActive||!r.selectionInteractable||!r.selectionViewportMatched||!r.selectionFinite||!r.selectionPositive||!r.selectionInSafeArea||!r.selectionInside||!r.selectionFrontOwned)return false;
        if(Gesture(new UnityEngine.Vector2(r.selectionX,r.selectionY),new UnityEngine.Vector2(r.selectionX,r.selectionY))){reselectPending=true;reselectDispatched=touch.DispatchedGestures;reselectAccepted=r.acceptedCommands;WriteCommandAdmission(r,"PublicReselectTouch","Same original stable cohort/group; public visible card; native Touch only");}return false;
    }
    static void RenderCommands()
    {
        try
        {
            if(!started||failed||!lease||cameraStep!=0||(phase!=1&&phase!=2)||lastCommandRenderFrame==UnityEngine.Time.frameCount||!UnityEditor.EditorApplication.isPlaying||!S005EarlyRoute1619.PaidReady)return;
            lastCommandRenderFrame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount;double now=UnityEditor.EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;if(now>deadline||now<next||touch==null||touch.IsBusy)return;
            if(!object.ReferenceEquals(Driver.GetValue(null),touch)||!touch.IsRunning||touch.PlayerInterrupted||touch.UnexpectedSamples!=0||touch.PhysicalInterventions!=0||touch.DispatchedGestures!=touch.CompletedGestures||!UnityEngine.Mathf.Approximately(UnityEngine.Time.timeScale,1)||Game.UI.Runtime.UiShellRuntimeGateway.ReadAriaPlay().Active)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Rendered route input ownership/normal speed changed");
            var w=Unity.Entities.World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;if(w==null||!w.IsCreated)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Rendered route world absent");var em=w.EntityManager;using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent),typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState));if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Rendered route unique session absent");var e=q.GetSingletonEntity();var state=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(e);var match=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState>(e);
            if(state.CatalogId.ToString()!="S005"||state.SessionId.ToString()!=attempt)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Rendered route attempt changed");if(match.Phase!=Game.Components.SkirmishPhase.Playing)return;
            var r=ReadCommandAdmission(true);r.elapsed=match.ElapsedSeconds;ObserveCommands(true);
            if(!r.groundAdmitted){renderNeedsCamera=true;return;}renderNeedsCamera=false;
            if(!EnsurePublicCohortSelection(em,r))return;
            if(phase==1)
            {
                if(!r.modeRead||r.commandMode!=Game.Tactical.Contracts.TacticalCommandMode.Attack.ToString())
                {
                    if(!r.viewportMatched||!r.buttonActive||!r.buttonInteractable||!r.buttonPositive||!r.buttonFinite||!r.buttonInSafeArea||!r.buttonInside||!r.buttonFrontOwned)return;
                    if(Gesture(new UnityEngine.Vector2(r.buttonX,r.buttonY),new UnityEngine.Vector2(r.buttonX,r.buttonY)))WriteCommandAdmission(r,"PublicAttackModeTouch");return;
                }
                phase=2;return;
            }
            if(!r.modeRead||r.commandMode!=Game.Tactical.Contracts.TacticalCommandMode.Attack.ToString()){phase=1;return;}
            acceptBefore=acceptCount;contactFrame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount;var point=new UnityEngine.Vector2(r.groundX,r.groundY);
            if(Gesture(point,point)){S005GroundTouchDiagnostic1619.Track(touch,attempt,cohort,site);WriteCommandAdmission(r,"PublicGroundTouch");Write(new Row{stage="GroundTouch",goalX=site.x,goalZ=site.z,safeArea=true,noUiHit=true,members=cohort.Count});phase=3;}
        }
        catch(System.Exception ex){failure="Rendered route input failure: "+ex;}
    }
    static void ObserveClose(string stage,Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudFullMapPopupView popup,UnityEngine.UI.Button button,UnityEngine.Vector2 point,bool admitted,string firstHit,bool nativeCenter)
    {
        if(stage!="PublicCloseTouch"&&UnityEditor.EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup<cameraObservationNext)return;cameraObservationNext=UnityEditor.EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup+1;
        var rect=button!=null?button.transform as UnityEngine.RectTransform:null;var canvas=button!=null?button.GetComponentInParent<UnityEngine.Canvas>():null;var camera=canvas!=null&&canvas.renderMode!=UnityEngine.RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay?canvas.worldCamera:null;int open=0;foreach(var map in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudFullMapPopupView>(UnityEngine.FindObjectsInactive.Include))if(map.IsOpen)open++;
        System.IO.File.AppendAllText(CameraEvidence,UnityEngine.JsonUtility.ToJson(new CameraCloseRow{stage=stage,attempt=attempt,samplingPhase=renderedCloseContext?"CanvasWillRenderCanvases":"EditorUpdate",viewportMatched=CloseViewportMatches(canvas),frame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount,cameraStep=cameraStep,busy=touch!=null&&touch.IsBusy,running=touch!=null&&touch.IsRunning,nextDue=next,acceptedBefore=cameraCommandsBefore,acceptedNow=Game.Runtime.AriaCommandEvidence.AcceptedCommands,openPopups=open,dispatched=touch?.DispatchedGestures??0,completed=touch?.CompletedGestures??0,popupExists=popup!=null,popupOpen=popup!=null&&popup.IsOpen,buttonExists=button!=null,buttonActive=button!=null&&button.IsActive(),buttonInteractable=button!=null&&button.IsInteractable(),nativeCenterPresented=nativeCenter,pointAdmitted=admitted,frontOwned=admitted,firstHit=firstHit,buttonPath=button!=null?CameraButtonPath(button.transform):"none",safeArea=UnityEngine.Screen.safeArea.Contains(point),insideButton=rect!=null&&UnityEngine.RectTransformUtility.RectangleContainsScreenPoint(rect,point,camera),pointX=point.x,pointY=point.y,editorTime=UnityEditor.EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup})+"\n");
    }
    // Editor update schedules only; actual camera inputs use the current rendered Canvas coordinates.
    static bool CameraOnly()=>false;
    static readonly string MapEvidence=Root+"native1619-rendered-map-positioning.jsonl";
    static int lastCameraRenderFrame=-1,mapAttempts;
    static UnityEngine.Vector3 mapAttemptSite,cameraBefore;
    [System.Serializable] sealed class MapRow
    {
        public string stage="",attempt="",samplingPhase="CanvasWillRenderCanvases",mapPath="",fromHit="",goalHit="",reason="",cohortIds="";
        public int frame,phaseValue,cameraStepValue,routeLeg=-1,attemptNumber,acceptedBefore,acceptedNow,cohortLiving,cohortSelected,foreignSelected;
        public uint dispatched,completed,cohortGroup;
        public bool routeLease,sameDriver,inputRunning,normalSpeed,ariaInactive,viewportMatched,eventSystemActive,mapActive,positiveMap,positiveViewport,fromSafe,goalSafe,fromInsideMap,goalInsideMap,fromInsideViewport,fromFrontOwned,goalFrontOwned,finitePoints,admitted,groundAdmitted,cohortExact;
        public float screenWidth,screenHeight,canvasWidth,canvasHeight,safeX,safeY,safeWidth,safeHeight,fromX,fromY,goalX,goalY,siteX,siteZ,groundX,groundY,groundDepth,cameraX,cameraY,cameraZ,cameraBeforeX,cameraBeforeY,cameraBeforeZ,cameraDisplacement,travelSeconds;
    }
    static void WriteMap(MapRow r,string stage,string reason="")
    {
        r.stage=stage;r.reason=reason;r.attempt=attempt;r.frame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount;r.phaseValue=phase;r.cameraStepValue=cameraStep;r.routeLease=flankLease;r.routeLeg=flankLease?S005SouthFlank1619.CurrentLeg:reinforcementFlankLease?reinforcementLeg:-1;r.attemptNumber=mapAttempts;r.dispatched=touch?.DispatchedGestures??0;r.completed=touch?.CompletedGestures??0;r.acceptedBefore=cameraCommandsBefore;r.acceptedNow=Game.Runtime.AriaCommandEvidence.AcceptedCommands;r.siteX=site.x;r.siteZ=site.z;
        System.IO.File.AppendAllText(MapEvidence,UnityEngine.JsonUtility.ToJson(r)+"\n");
    }
    static bool MapFront(UnityEngine.RectTransform owner,UnityEngine.Vector2 point,out string hit)
    {
        hit="";var es=UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current;if(es==null||!es.isActiveAndEnabled)return false;
        var hits=new System.Collections.Generic.List<UnityEngine.EventSystems.RaycastResult>();es.RaycastAll(new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(es){position=point},hits);hit=hits.Count>0?CameraButtonPath(hits[0].gameObject.transform):"none";
        return hits.Count>0&&(hits[0].gameObject.transform==owner||hits[0].gameObject.transform.IsChildOf(owner));
    }
    static bool RenderMapPoint(Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudMinimapView map,UnityEngine.Vector2 point,out string hit)
    {
        hit="";if(map==null||!map.isActiveAndEnabled||map.MapRect==null)return false;var cv=map.GetComponentInParent<UnityEngine.Canvas>();var cam=cv!=null&&cv.renderMode!=UnityEngine.RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay?cv.worldCamera:null;var rect=map.MapRect;
        return CloseViewportMatches(cv)&&FiniteClose(point.x)&&FiniteClose(point.y)&&rect.rect.width>0&&rect.rect.height>0&&UnityEngine.Screen.safeArea.Contains(point)&&UnityEngine.RectTransformUtility.RectangleContainsScreenPoint(rect,point,cam)&&MapFront(rect,point,out hit);
    }
    static void RenderCamera()
    {
        try
        {
            if(!started||failed||!lease||(!renderNeedsCamera&&cameraStep==0)||(phase!=1&&phase!=2)||lastCameraRenderFrame==UnityEngine.Time.frameCount||!UnityEditor.EditorApplication.isPlaying||!S005EarlyRoute1619.PaidReady)return;
            lastCameraRenderFrame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount;double now=UnityEditor.EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;if(now>deadline||now<next||touch==null||touch.IsBusy)return;
            if(!object.ReferenceEquals(Driver.GetValue(null),touch)||!touch.IsRunning||touch.PlayerInterrupted||touch.UnexpectedSamples!=0||touch.PhysicalInterventions!=0||touch.DispatchedGestures!=touch.CompletedGestures||!UnityEngine.Mathf.Approximately(UnityEngine.Time.timeScale,1)||Game.UI.Runtime.UiShellRuntimeGateway.ReadAriaPlay().Active)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Rendered map input ownership changed");
            var world=Unity.Entities.World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;if(world==null||!world.IsCreated)return;var em=world.EntityManager;using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent),typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState));if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Rendered map unique session absent");var session=q.GetSingletonEntity();var state=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(session);if(state.CatalogId.ToString()!="S005"||state.SessionId.ToString()!=attempt)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Rendered map attempt changed");if(em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState>(session).Phase!=Game.Components.SkirmishPhase.Playing)return;
            var admission=ReadCommandAdmission(true);ReadCohortSelection(em,admission);
            var r=new MapRow{sameDriver=admission.sameDriver,inputRunning=admission.inputRunning,normalSpeed=admission.normalSpeed,ariaInactive=admission.ariaInactive,eventSystemActive=admission.eventSystemActive,screenWidth=UnityEngine.Screen.width,screenHeight=UnityEngine.Screen.height,cohortIds=admission.cohortIds,cohortGroup=admission.cohortGroup,cohortLiving=admission.cohortLiving,cohortSelected=admission.cohortSelected,foreignSelected=admission.foreignSelected,cohortExact=admission.cohortExact,groundAdmitted=admission.groundAdmitted,groundX=admission.groundX,groundY=admission.groundY,groundDepth=admission.groundDepth};
            var safe=UnityEngine.Screen.safeArea;r.safeX=safe.x;r.safeY=safe.y;r.safeWidth=safe.width;r.safeHeight=safe.height;var camera=UnityEngine.Camera.main;if(camera==null)return;var position=camera.transform.position;r.cameraX=position.x;r.cameraY=position.y;r.cameraZ=position.z;r.cameraBeforeX=cameraBefore.x;r.cameraBeforeY=cameraBefore.y;r.cameraBeforeZ=cameraBefore.z;r.cameraDisplacement=UnityEngine.Vector3.Distance(cameraBefore,position);
            Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudFullMapPopupView popup=null;int open=0;foreach(var p in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudFullMapPopupView>(UnityEngine.FindObjectsInactive.Include))if(p.IsOpen){popup=p;open++;}if(open>1)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Ambiguous rendered full map");
            if(cameraStep==3)
            {
                if(popup!=null)return;
                WriteMap(r,"CameraPositionObserved","Read-only camera and ground projection after completed map drag/Close");int after=Game.Runtime.AriaCommandEvidence.AcceptedCommands;Write(new Row{stage="CameraClosed",commandsBefore=cameraCommandsBefore,commandsAfter=after,cameraOnly=after==cameraCommandsBefore});if(after!=cameraCommandsBefore)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Camera issued troop order");cameraStep=0;return;
            }
            if(admission.groundAdmitted&&popup==null){renderNeedsCamera=false;cameraStep=0;return;}
            if(cameraStep==0)
            {
                if(admission.modeRead&&admission.commandMode!=Game.Tactical.Contracts.TacticalCommandMode.Select.ToString())
                {
                    var controls=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchOverlayCommandControlsView>();renderedCloseContext=true;bool ok=PresentedClosePoint(controls?.SelectButton,out var point,out var hit,out var native);renderedCloseContext=false;
                    if(ok&&Gesture(point,point)){r.fromX=r.goalX=point.x;r.fromY=r.goalY=point.y;r.fromHit=r.goalHit=hit;WriteMap(r,"PublicCameraSelectTouch");}return;
                }
                if(!admission.modeRead)return;cameraCommandsBefore=Game.Runtime.AriaCommandEvidence.AcceptedCommands;cameraStep=1;
            }
            if(popup==null)
            {
                foreach(var map in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudMinimapView>(UnityEngine.FindObjectsSortMode.None))
                {
                    if(map.GetComponentInParent<Game.UI.Runtime.MatchHudFullMapPopupView>()!=null||map.MapRect==null)continue;var cv=map.GetComponentInParent<UnityEngine.Canvas>();var cam=cv!=null&&cv.renderMode!=UnityEngine.RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay?cv.worldCamera:null;var point=UnityEngine.RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(cam,map.MapRect.TransformPoint(map.MapRect.rect.center));if(!RenderMapPoint(map,point,out var hit))continue;
                    if(Gesture(point,point)){r.fromX=r.goalX=point.x;r.fromY=r.goalY=point.y;r.mapPath=CameraButtonPath(map.MapRect);r.fromHit=r.goalHit=hit;WriteMap(r,"PublicOpenMapTouch");}return;
                }
                return;
            }
            var full=popup.Minimap;if(full==null||full.MapRect==null||full.ViewportRect==null)return;var canvas=full.GetComponentInParent<UnityEngine.Canvas>();var eventCamera=canvas!=null&&canvas.renderMode!=UnityEngine.RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay?canvas.worldCamera:null;var cb=canvas!=null?canvas.rootCanvas.pixelRect:default;r.canvasWidth=cb.width;r.canvasHeight=cb.height;r.viewportMatched=CloseViewportMatches(canvas);r.mapActive=full.isActiveAndEnabled;r.mapPath=CameraButtonPath(full.MapRect);r.positiveMap=FiniteClose(full.MapRect.rect.width)&&FiniteClose(full.MapRect.rect.height)&&full.MapRect.rect.width>0&&full.MapRect.rect.height>0;r.positiveViewport=full.ViewportRect.gameObject.activeInHierarchy&&FiniteClose(full.ViewportRect.rect.width)&&FiniteClose(full.ViewportRect.rect.height)&&full.ViewportRect.rect.width>0&&full.ViewportRect.rect.height>0;
            if(!full.TryGetPresentedMapPoint(site,eventCamera,out var goal))return;var from=UnityEngine.RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(eventCamera,full.ViewportRect.TransformPoint(full.ViewportRect.rect.center));r.fromX=from.x;r.fromY=from.y;r.goalX=goal.x;r.goalY=goal.y;r.finitePoints=FiniteClose(from.x)&&FiniteClose(from.y)&&FiniteClose(goal.x)&&FiniteClose(goal.y);r.fromSafe=safe.Contains(from);r.goalSafe=safe.Contains(goal);r.fromInsideMap=UnityEngine.RectTransformUtility.RectangleContainsScreenPoint(full.MapRect,from,eventCamera);r.goalInsideMap=UnityEngine.RectTransformUtility.RectangleContainsScreenPoint(full.MapRect,goal,eventCamera);r.fromInsideViewport=UnityEngine.RectTransformUtility.RectangleContainsScreenPoint(full.ViewportRect,from,eventCamera);r.fromFrontOwned=RenderMapPoint(full,from,out r.fromHit);r.goalFrontOwned=RenderMapPoint(full,goal,out r.goalHit);r.travelSeconds=.45f;
            r.admitted=r.viewportMatched&&r.mapActive&&r.positiveMap&&r.positiveViewport&&r.finitePoints&&r.fromSafe&&r.goalSafe&&r.fromInsideMap&&r.goalInsideMap&&r.fromInsideViewport&&r.fromFrontOwned&&r.goalFrontOwned&&(from-goal).sqrMagnitude>25;
            WriteMap(r,"RenderedMapDragAdmission");if(!r.admitted)return;
            if((mapAttemptSite-site).sqrMagnitude>.01f){mapAttemptSite=site;mapAttempts=0;}if(mapAttempts>=3)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Three actual rendered public map drags failed safe ground admission for unchanged goal");
            cameraBefore=position;if(Gesture(from,goal,.45f)){mapAttempts++;cameraStep=3;WriteMap(r,"PublicViewportDrag","Actual native viewport-center drag to public authored world goal; no camera API writes");Write(new Row{stage="CameraTouch",goalX=site.x,goalZ=site.z,cameraOnly=true,commandsBefore=cameraCommandsBefore});}
        }
        catch(System.Exception ex){failure="Rendered map input failure: "+ex;}
        finally{renderedCloseContext=false;}
    }
    static void CandidateSite()
    {
        if(flankLease){site=S005SouthFlank1619.Destination;return;}
        if(reinforcementFlankLease){site=S005SouthFlank1619.DestinationAt(reinforcementLeg);return;}
        var forward=objective-player;forward.y=0;if(forward.sqrMagnitude<1)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Public objective geometry absent");forward.Normalize();
        // The existing manual journey already escorts toward the public enemy-base marker from 40m out.
        // The old 12m/+6m landing point lay beside ground_staging.2 and let AttackMove auto-acquire
        // that non-objective structure. Keep the destination derived only from the public HUD geometry.
        site=objective-forward*(40+candidate*6);site.y=0;
    }
    static void BindCohort(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em,Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent session)
    {
        cohort.Clear();ids.Clear();starts.Clear();
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SelectedUnitTag),typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(Game.Components.UnitHealth),typeof(Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform),typeof(Game.Components.UnitMove),typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishUnitRoleComponent));using var list=q.ToEntityArray(Unity.Collections.Allocator.Temp);

            cohort.Add(e);ids.Add(e,o.StableObjectId.ToString());starts.Add(e,em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(e).Position);
        }
        if(cohort.Count==0)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Actual public selection cohort absent");
    }
    static bool Sample(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em,float elapsed,bool visible,bool targetVisible)
    {
        int live=0;foreach(var e in cohort)
        {
            if(!em.Exists(e)||!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(e)||!em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(e))continue;
            var o=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(e);if(o.FactionId!=1||o.SessionId.ToString()!=attempt||o.StableObjectId.ToString()!=ids[e])throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Fallback actor identity changed");
            var p=(UnityEngine.Vector3)em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(e).Position;var s=starts[e];bool alive=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(e).Current>0;if(alive)live++;
            var role=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(e).Role;
            Write(new Row{stage="Actor",id=ids[e],role=role.ToString(),x=p.x,z=p.z,startX=s.x,startZ=s.z,displacement=UnityEngine.Vector2.Distance(new UnityEngine.Vector2(p.x,p.z),new UnityEngine.Vector2(s.x,s.z)),speed=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitMove>(e).Speed,elapsed=elapsed,markerVisible=visible,targetVisible=targetVisible,selected=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SelectedUnitTag>(e),alive=alive,receipt=receipt});
        }
        return live>0;
    }
    static bool TryReselectRouteCohort(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em)
    {
        if(ExactSelection(em,cohort)){routeReselectRequired=false;Write(new Row{stage="OriginalRouteReselectedAfterDefense",reason="ExactOriginalFourPublicCardSelectionRestored",members=cohort.Count,receipt=receipt});return true;}

        if(FindCard(group,out var card)){if(Tap(card))Write(new Row{stage="PublicOriginalRouteReselectTouch",reason="CurrentPresentedOriginalRocketeerCardAfterDefense",members=cohort.Count,receipt=receipt});return false;}
        PageForCard();return false;
    }
    static void Tick()
    {
        try
        {
            if(!started||failed||!UnityEditor.EditorApplication.isPlaying||!S005EarlyRoute1619.PaidReady)return;double now=UnityEditor.EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;
            if(failure.Length>0)throw new System.InvalidOperationException(failure);
            var w=Unity.Entities.World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;if(w==null||!w.IsCreated){if(lease)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("World lost during fallback lease");return;}var em=w.EntityManager;

            // Native lethal Barracks damage may finish the match in this same update.
            S005SouthFlank1619.ObserveDamage(em,session,state);
            if(match.Phase!=Game.Components.SkirmishPhase.Playing){if(lease&&!TryReleaseVerifiedTerminal(em,session,state,match))throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Unverified native phase change during fallback lease");return;}
            if(state.CatalogId.ToString()!="S005")throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Foreign actual session");var hud=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<Game.UI.Runtime.SkirmishMatchView>();if(hud==null)return;
            if(!lease&&(S005ManualHomeDefense1619.OwnsLease||!S005NativeIntroReady1619.Ready(em)))return;
            if(lease)
            {
                if(suspendedForDefense)
                {
                    if(S005ManualHomeDefense1619.HasFailed)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Public home-defense companion failed during safe route yield");
                    if(now-defenseSuspendAt>130)throw new System.TimeoutException("Bounded public home-defense route yield exceeded130s");

                    if(S005ManualHomeDefense1619.OwnsLease||touch.IsBusy||touch.CompletedGestures!=touch.DispatchedGestures||!object.ReferenceEquals(Driver.GetValue(null),touch)||System.Math.Abs((double)Due.GetValue(null)-(deadline+5))>.000001)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Home-defense route yield returned without exact settled Touch/manualDue ownership");

                if(phase==4&&flankLease&&!S005SouthFlank1619.RouteComplete&&!routeReselectRequired&&receipt!=0&&defenseYieldCount<2&&S005ManualHomeDefense1619.WantsVisibleHomeDefense(em,state))

                    return;

                {

                        // Public Stop disables auto-engage; Hold would reacquire ground_staging.2.
                        if(LivingOriginal(em,false)!=cohort.Count||S005SouthFlank1619.QualifiedDamageSeen)
                            throw new System.InvalidOperationException("South-route cohort/damage baseline differs before scout tactic");
                        flankLease=false;tacticStep=tacticPages=tacticTouchFrame=tacticDamageFrame=tacticSelectionFrame=tacticWheelTouchFrame=tacticStopTouchFrame=0;tacticSelectTouchFrame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount;tacticWheelSampleFrame=-1;tacticWheelRenderSamples=0;tacticWheelRenderFrame=-1;tacticWheelRenderFrameCount=0;tacticWheelWasOpen=false;tacticPagingBack=true;tacticStopPositions.Clear();tacticScanReceipt=tacticAttackReceipt=0;tacticAttackEvents=tacticAttackPairs=tacticAttackIntentActors=tacticAttackPathActors=tacticAttackTargetHp=0;tacticAttackFrame=-1;tacticAttackPending=false;tacticStopFeedbackConfirmed=false;tacticStopFeedbackBefore=tacticStopFeedbackMessage="";tacticScout=Unity.Entities.Entity.Null;tacticScoutGroup=tacticScoutReservation=0;tacticBarracks=default;tacticStartedAt=now;phase=5;

                    }

                    {

// Read-only authoritative readiness; no layout, timing or gameplay writes.
public static class S005NativeIntroReady1619
{
    public static bool Ready(Unity.Entities.EntityManager em)
    {
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.UI.Shell.Contracts.Ecs.MatchIntroTransitionComponent));
        if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)return false;var e=q.GetSingletonEntity();
        if(!em.HasComponent<Game.UI.Shell.Contracts.Ecs.UiShellStateComponent>(e))return false;
        var state=em.GetComponentData<Game.UI.Shell.Contracts.Ecs.MatchIntroTransitionComponent>(e);
        if(state.State!=Game.UI.Contracts.MatchIntroTransitionStateKind.Complete||state.InputLocked!=0)return false;
        using var finale=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.CampaignMissionFinalePresentationComponent));
        if(finale.CalculateEntityCount()==1){var f=finale.GetSingleton<Game.Components.CampaignMissionFinalePresentationComponent>();if(f.Required!=0&&f.Stage is >=1 and <=3)return false;}
        return true;
    }
}


// External, read-only QA. Call from the existing observer, never from gameplay.
// This census matches native snapshot owned-query inclusion of Disabled entities.
public static class S005PassengerCarrierIdentity1619
{
    static string output;
    static int lastFrame = -1;
    [Serializable] public sealed class Row
    {
        public string stage = "", samplingPhase = "", attempt = "", passengerStable = "", passengerAttempt = "", carrierStable = "", carrierAttempt = "", carrierPrefab = "", reason = "";
        public int frame, passengerIndex, passengerVersion, passengerFaction, passengerRole, passengerHp, passengerReleased, carrierIndex, carrierVersion, carrierFaction, carrierRole, carrierHp, carrierReleased;
        public int carrierBufferCount, reciprocalEntries, nullEntries, missingEntries, foreignEntries, brokenBacklinks, passengerCount, ownedCount, sessionCount;
        public uint passengerReservation, carrierReservation;
        public float elapsed;
        public bool paused, passengerExists, passengerDisabled, passengerHasHealth, passengerHasHiddenScale, carrierExists, carrierOwned, carrierSameAttempt, carrierHasHealth, carrierDisabled, carrierHasBuffer, carrierInSnapshotOwnedSet;
    }
    public static void Run() => Debug.Log("[S005PassengerCarrierIdentity1619] loadedOnly=1 gameplayStarted=0");
    public static void Initialize(string path)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path) || !Path.IsPathFullyQualified(path)) throw new ArgumentException("Absolute diagnostic path required.");
        using (File.Open(path, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.Read)) { }
        output = path; lastFrame = -1;
        Write(new Row { stage = "Initialized", samplingPhase = "ExternalObserver", reason = "ReadOnly; no actor/order/health/resource/clock/knowledge writes" });
    }
    public static void Observe(string phase)
    {
        if (output == null || lastFrame == Time.frameCount) return;
        var world = World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;
        if (world == null || !world.IsCreated) return;
        var em = world.EntityManager;
        using var sessions = em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent));
        int count = sessions.CalculateEntityCount();
        if (count != 1) return;
        Entity session = sessions.GetSingletonEntity();
        var state = em.GetComponentData<SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(session);
        if (state.CatalogId.ToString() != "S005") return;
        lastFrame = Time.frameCount;
        var clock = em.HasComponent<SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent>(session) ? em.GetComponentData<SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent>(session) : default;
        using var ownedQuery = em.CreateEntityQuery(new EntityQueryDesc {
            All = new[] { ComponentType.ReadOnly<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>() },
            Options = EntityQueryOptions.IncludeDisabledEntities | EntityQueryOptions.IgnoreComponentEnabledState });
        using var owned = ownedQuery.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);
        int ownCount = 0, passengers = 0;
        foreach (Entity actor in owned)
        {
            var owner = em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);
            if (!owner.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId)) continue;
            ownCount++;
            if (!em.HasComponent<UnitTransportPassenger>(actor)) continue;
            passengers++;
            var transport = em.GetComponentData<UnitTransportPassenger>(actor).Transport;
            var row = new Row {
                stage = "PassengerCarrierIdentity", samplingPhase = phase, attempt = state.SessionId.ToString(),
                frame = Time.frameCount, elapsed = clock.ElapsedSeconds, paused = clock.Paused != 0,
                passengerIndex = actor.Index, passengerVersion = actor.Version, passengerExists = em.Exists(actor),
                passengerStable = owner.StableObjectId.ToString(), passengerAttempt = owner.SessionId.ToString(), passengerFaction = owner.FactionId,
                passengerReleased = owner.CapacityReleased, passengerReservation = owner.ReservationId,
                passengerDisabled = em.HasComponent<Disabled>(actor), passengerHasHealth = em.HasComponent<UnitHealth>(actor),
                passengerHasHiddenScale = em.HasBuffer<UnitTransportHiddenVisualScale>(actor),
                carrierIndex = transport.Index, carrierVersion = transport.Version, carrierExists = transport != Entity.Null && em.Exists(transport) };
            if (row.passengerHasHealth) row.passengerHp = em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(actor).Current;
            if (em.HasComponent<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor)) row.passengerRole = (int)em.GetComponentData<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor).Role;
            if (row.carrierExists)
            {
                row.carrierOwned = em.HasComponent<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(transport);
                row.carrierHasHealth = em.HasComponent<UnitHealth>(transport);
                row.carrierDisabled = em.HasComponent<Disabled>(transport);
                row.carrierHasBuffer = em.HasBuffer<UnitTransportPassengerElement>(transport);
                if (row.carrierOwned)
                {
                    var carrierOwner = em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(transport);
                    row.carrierStable = carrierOwner.StableObjectId.ToString(); row.carrierAttempt = carrierOwner.SessionId.ToString();
                    row.carrierFaction = carrierOwner.FactionId; row.carrierReleased = carrierOwner.CapacityReleased; row.carrierReservation = carrierOwner.ReservationId;
                    row.carrierSameAttempt = carrierOwner.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId);
                    // Exact native snapshot adds ALL current-attempt owned roots or fails before reference capture.
                    row.carrierInSnapshotOwnedSet = row.carrierSameAttempt && row.carrierStable.Length != 0 &&
                        em.HasComponent<SkirmishSharedActorTag>(transport) && em.HasComponent<UnitSourcePrefabKey>(transport);
                }
                if (row.carrierHasHealth) row.carrierHp = em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(transport).Current;
                if (em.HasComponent<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(transport)) row.carrierRole = (int)em.GetComponentData<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(transport).Role;
                if (em.HasComponent<UnitSourcePrefabKey>(transport)) row.carrierPrefab = em.GetComponentData<UnitSourcePrefabKey>(transport).Value.ToString();
                if (row.carrierHasBuffer)
                {
                    var entries = em.GetBuffer<UnitTransportPassengerElement>(transport, true); row.carrierBufferCount = entries.Length;
                    foreach (var entry in entries)
                    {
                        if (entry.Passenger == actor) row.reciprocalEntries++;
                        if (entry.Passenger == Entity.Null) { row.nullEntries++; continue; }
                        if (!em.Exists(entry.Passenger)) { row.missingEntries++; continue; }
                        if (!em.HasComponent<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(entry.Passenger) ||
                            !em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(entry.Passenger).SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId)) row.foreignEntries++;
                        if (!em.HasComponent<UnitTransportPassenger>(entry.Passenger) ||
                            em.GetComponentData<UnitTransportPassenger>(entry.Passenger).Transport != transport) row.brokenBacklinks++;
                    }
                }
            }
            row.reason = !row.carrierExists ? "MissingNativeCarrierEntity" :
                !row.carrierInSnapshotOwnedSet ? "CarrierOutsideCurrentOwnedSnapshotSet" :
                row.reciprocalEntries != 1 ? "NonReciprocalPassengerLink" : "CurrentOwnedReciprocalLink";
            Write(row);
        }
        Write(new Row { stage = "PassengerCensus", samplingPhase = phase, frame = Time.frameCount, attempt = state.SessionId.ToString(),
            elapsed = clock.ElapsedSeconds, paused = clock.Paused != 0, passengerCount = passengers, ownedCount = ownCount, sessionCount = count });
    }
    static void Write(Row row) => File.AppendAllText(output, JsonUtility.ToJson(row) + "\n");
}

    static bool Gesture(Vector2 point){if(!touch.TryGesture(point,point,.15f,0,Time.unscaledTime))return false;F("manualActions").SetValue(null,(int)F("manualActions").GetValue(null)+1);next=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup+.9;return true;}

    static bool GroundPoint(Vector3 site,out Vector2 point){point=default;if(Camera.main==null)return false;var p=Camera.main.WorldToScreenPoint(site);if(float.IsNaN(p.x)||float.IsNaN(p.y)||float.IsNaN(p.z)||float.IsInfinity(p.x)||float.IsInfinity(p.y)||float.IsInfinity(p.z)||p.z<=0||!Screen.safeArea.Contains(p))return false;var popup=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<MatchHudFullMapPopupView>(FindObjectsInactive.Include);if(popup!=null&&popup.IsOpen)return false;var es=UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current;if(es==null||!es.isActiveAndEnabled)return false;var hits=new List<UnityEngine.EventSystems.RaycastResult>();es.RaycastAll(new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(es){position=p},hits);if(hits.Count!=0)return false;point=p;return true;}
    static bool Ground(Vector3 site)=>GroundPoint(site,out var point)&&Gesture(point);
    static bool Finite(float value)=>!float.IsNaN(value)&&!float.IsInfinity(value);
    static bool FrontOwned(RectTransform owner,Vector2 point,Camera eventCamera){if(owner==null||!Finite(point.x)||!Finite(point.y)||!Screen.safeArea.Contains(point)||!RectTransformUtility.RectangleContainsScreenPoint(owner,point,eventCamera))return false;var es=UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current;if(es==null||!es.isActiveAndEnabled)return false;var hits=new List<UnityEngine.EventSystems.RaycastResult>();es.RaycastAll(new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(es){position=point},hits);return hits.Count>0&&(hits[0].gameObject.transform==owner||hits[0].gameObject.transform.IsChildOf(owner));}
    static Rect PresentedScreenRect(RectTransform rect,Camera eventCamera){var corners=new Vector3[4];rect.GetWorldCorners(corners);Vector2 lo=RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(eventCamera,corners[0]);Vector2 hi=RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(eventCamera,corners[2]);return new Rect(lo,hi-lo);}
    static bool StablePresentedMap(MatchHudMinimapView map,Canvas canvas,Camera eventCamera)
    {
        if(map==null||!map.isActiveAndEnabled||map.MapRect==null||map.ViewportRect==null||canvas==null||!map.MapRect.gameObject.activeInHierarchy||!map.ViewportRect.gameObject.activeInHierarchy)return false;
        var canvasBounds=canvas.rootCanvas.pixelRect;if(!Finite(canvasBounds.width)||!Finite(canvasBounds.height)||canvasBounds.width<=0||canvasBounds.height<=0||Mathf.Abs(canvasBounds.width-Screen.width)>1||Mathf.Abs(canvasBounds.height-Screen.height)>1)return false;
        Rect bounds=PresentedScreenRect(map.MapRect,eventCamera),viewport=PresentedScreenRect(map.ViewportRect,eventCamera);
        bool usable=Finite(bounds.x)&&Finite(bounds.y)&&Finite(bounds.width)&&Finite(bounds.height)&&bounds.width>1&&bounds.height>1&&Finite(viewport.x)&&Finite(viewport.y)&&Finite(viewport.width)&&Finite(viewport.height)&&viewport.width>1&&viewport.height>1;
        if(!usable){mapLayoutFrame=-1;mapLayoutStable=false;return false;}
        bool stable=mapLayoutOwner==map&&mapLayoutFrame>=0&&mapLayoutFrame<Time.frameCount&&(bounds.position-mapLayoutBounds.position).sqrMagnitude<=.25f&&(bounds.size-mapLayoutBounds.size).sqrMagnitude<=.25f&&(viewport.position-mapLayoutViewport.position).sqrMagnitude<=.25f&&(viewport.size-mapLayoutViewport.size).sqrMagnitude<=.25f;
        mapLayoutOwner=map;mapLayoutFrame=Time.frameCount;mapLayoutBounds=bounds;mapLayoutViewport=viewport;mapLayoutStable=stable;return stable;
    }
    static bool DragMap(Vector2 from,Vector2 goal){if(!touch.TryGesture(from,goal,.15f,.45f,Time.unscaledTime))return false;F("manualActions").SetValue(null,(int)F("manualActions").GetValue(null)+1);next=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup+.9;return true;}
    static void AppendCameraCallbacks(System.Text.StringBuilder text,Type owner,string fieldName){var field=owner.GetField(fieldName,BindingFlags.Static|BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.NonPublic);if(field==null||!typeof(Delegate).IsAssignableFrom(field.FieldType))throw new MissingFieldException(owner.FullName,fieldName);var callbacks=(field.GetValue(null) as MulticastDelegate)?.GetInvocationList();if(callbacks==null)return;foreach(var callback in callbacks){var type=callback.Method.DeclaringType?.FullName??"";if(type.StartsWith("S005",StringComparison.Ordinal)||type.IndexOf(".S005",StringComparison.Ordinal)>=0||type.IndexOf("SkirmishS002AriaRunHarness",StringComparison.Ordinal)>=0)text.Append(owner.Name).Append('.').Append(fieldName).Append(':').Append(type).Append('.').Append(callback.Method.Name).Append(';');}}
    static string FocusCallbackSignature(){var text=new System.Text.StringBuilder();AppendCameraCallbacks(text,typeof(EditorApplication),"update");AppendCameraCallbacks(text,typeof(EditorApplication),"delayCall");AppendCameraCallbacks(text,typeof(Canvas),"willRenderCanvases");AppendCameraCallbacks(text,typeof(Application),"s_LogCallbackHandler");AppendCameraCallbacks(text,typeof(AriaCommandEvidence),"Recorded");return text.ToString();}
    static void CaptureCameraWitness(EntityManager em,Entity session){var state=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(session);var members=Members(em,state.SessionId);if(members.Count!=1||!ExclusiveSelected(em,members)||ExactDefender(em,state.SessionId)!=members[0])throw new InvalidOperationException("Public home camera requires exact living selected defender");cameraActor=members[0];cameraDriverIdentityBefore=System.Runtime.CompilerServices.RuntimeHelpers.GetHashCode(touch);cameraDeviceBefore=touch.DeviceId;cameraGenerationBefore=touch.Generation;cameraStartedRunsBefore=touch.StartedRuns;cameraProbeTouchBefore=F("helipadProbeTouch").GetValue(null) as AriaTouchInputUiSystemHelper;cameraProbeStageBefore=(int)F("helipadProbeStage").GetValue(null);cameraProbeMapStepBefore=(int)F("airProbeDefenseMapStep").GetValue(null);cameraProbeMapAttemptsBefore=(int)F("airProbeDefenseMapAttempts").GetValue(null);cameraCallbacksBefore=FocusCallbackSignature();if(cameraDeviceBefore<=0||cameraGenerationBefore==0||cameraProbeTouchBefore!=null||cameraProbeStageBefore!=0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Public home camera began with foreign probe/input owner");}
    static void EnsureCameraWitness(){if(touch==null||!ReferenceEquals(Current,touch)||!touch.IsRunning||System.Runtime.CompilerServices.RuntimeHelpers.GetHashCode(touch)!=cameraDriverIdentityBefore||touch.DeviceId!=cameraDeviceBefore||touch.Generation!=cameraGenerationBefore||touch.StartedRuns!=cameraStartedRunsBefore||!ReferenceEquals(F("helipadProbeTouch").GetValue(null),cameraProbeTouchBefore)||(int)F("helipadProbeStage").GetValue(null)!=cameraProbeStageBefore||(int)F("airProbeDefenseMapStep").GetValue(null)!=cameraProbeMapStepBefore||(int)F("airProbeDefenseMapAttempts").GetValue(null)!=cameraProbeMapAttemptsBefore||FocusCallbackSignature()!=cameraCallbacksBefore)throw new InvalidOperationException("Public home camera changed Touch generation or unrelated probe/callback state");}
    // Home-defense-owned public UI path; no aircraft-probe callbacks or driver aliasing.
    static bool Focus(Vector3 site,double now,EntityManager em,Entity session)
    {
        if(touch==null||touch.IsBusy||touch.CompletedGestures!=touch.DispatchedGestures||!ReferenceEquals(Current,touch)||!touch.IsRunning)throw new InvalidOperationException("Public home camera Touch ownership changed");
        if(cameraStep!=0)EnsureCameraWitness();
        var popup=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<MatchHudFullMapPopupView>(FindObjectsInactive.Include);bool open=popup!=null&&popup.IsOpen;
        if(cameraStep==0&&!open&&GroundPoint(site,out _))return true;
        if(cameraStep==0&&open){if(Tap(popup.CloseAction))Write("PublicHomeMapCloseResidualTouch",em,session,"Close preexisting rendered map before home camera input");return false;}
        if(cameraStep==0)
        {
            if(UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadMatchHudCommandState(out var command)&&command.ActiveCommandMode.ToString()!="Select")
            {var controls=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<MatchOverlayCommandControlsView>(FindObjectsInactive.Include);if(Tap(controls?.SelectButton))Write("PublicHomeCameraSelectTouch",em,session,"Public Select control prevents map camera input from ordering troops");return false;}
            cameraCommandsBefore=AriaCommandEvidence.AcceptedCommands;cameraAttackMaskBefore=UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadExpandedSquadPage(out var page)?page.AttackOrderMask:-1;
            CaptureCameraWitness(em,session);cameraBefore=Camera.main!=null?Camera.main.transform.position:default;cameraStep=1;mapLayoutFrame=-1;mapLayoutOwner=null;
            Write("PublicHomeCameraLeaseStarted",em,session,"Exact living defender bound; camera input through presented public map only");
        }
        if(cameraStep==2)
        {
            if(open){if(Tap(popup.CloseAction)){cameraStep=3;cameraCloseAttempts=1;Write("PublicHomeMapCloseTouch",em,session,"Presented full-map Close action after public viewport drag");}return false;}
            cameraStep=3;
        }
        if(cameraStep==3)
        {
            if(open){if(cameraCloseAttempts>=3)throw new InvalidOperationException("Bounded public full-map Close touches failed");if(Tap(popup.CloseAction)){cameraCloseAttempts++;Write("PublicHomeMapCloseTouch",em,session,"Bounded retry of presented full-map Close action");}return false;}
            bool admitted=GroundPoint(site,out _);bool moved=Camera.main!=null&&Vector3.Distance(cameraBefore,Camera.main.transform.position)>.5f;
            if(AriaCommandEvidence.AcceptedCommands!=cameraCommandsBefore||!UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadExpandedSquadPage(out var after)||after.AttackOrderMask!=cameraAttackMaskBefore)throw new InvalidOperationException("Public home map camera gesture issued or changed troop orders");
            Write("PublicHomeCameraObserved",em,session,"Rendered popup closed; camera movement and ground admission checked without gameplay writes");
            cameraStep=0;mapLayoutFrame=-1;mapLayoutOwner=null;
            if(admitted&&moved)return true;
            if(cameraAttempts>=3)throw new InvalidOperationException("Three public home map drags did not yield camera movement and safe ground admission");
            return false;
        }
        if(!open)
        {
            foreach(var map in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<MatchHudMinimapView>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
            {
                if(map.GetComponentInParent<MatchHudFullMapPopupView>()!=null||map.MapRect==null)continue;var canvas=map.GetComponentInParent<Canvas>();var eventCamera=canvas!=null&&canvas.renderMode!=RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay?canvas.worldCamera:null;var point=RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(eventCamera,map.MapRect.TransformPoint(map.MapRect.rect.center));
                if(FrontOwned(map.MapRect,point,eventCamera)&&Gesture(point)){Write("PublicHomeMapOpenTouch",em,session,"Actual presented compact map center Touch");}return false;
            }
            return false;
        }
        var full=popup.Minimap;if(full==null||full.MapRect==null||full.ViewportRect==null)return false;var cv=full.GetComponentInParent<Canvas>();var cam=cv!=null&&cv.renderMode!=RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay?cv.worldCamera:null;if(!StablePresentedMap(full,cv,cam))return false;
        if(!full.TryGetPresentedMapPoint(site,cam,out var goal))return false;var from=RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(cam,full.ViewportRect.TransformPoint(full.ViewportRect.rect.center));if((from-goal).sqrMagnitude<=25f)from+=Vector2.up*24f;
        mapFrom=from;mapGoal=goal;mapFromFront=FrontOwned(full.MapRect,from,cam);mapGoalFront=FrontOwned(full.MapRect,goal,cam);
        if(!mapFromFront||!mapGoalFront||!RectTransformUtility.RectangleContainsScreenPoint(full.ViewportRect,from,cam))return false;
        if(cameraAttempts>=3)throw new InvalidOperationException("Bounded public home camera drag attempts exhausted");
        if(DragMap(from,goal)){cameraAttempts++;cameraCloseAttempts=0;cameraStep=2;Write("PublicHomeViewportDrag",em,session,"Two-frame-stable rendered full map; public viewport-to-target drag with front-owned safe coordinates");}
        return false;
    }
    static List<Entity> Members(EntityManager em,FixedString64Bytes id){var a=new List<Entity>();using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent),typeof(UnitHealth));using var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);foreach(var e in es){var o=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(e);if(o.SessionId.Equals(id)&&S005NativeOwnedActorBridge1619.IsLivingOwnedActor(em,e,1)&&em.GetComponentData<SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(e).GroupId==group)a.Add(e);}return a;}

    static bool ExclusiveSelected(EntityManager em,List<Entity> members){if(members.Count==0)return false;using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SelectedUnitTag));using var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);foreach(var e in es)if(!members.Contains(e))return false;foreach(var e in members)if(!em.HasComponent<SelectedUnitTag>(e))return false;return true;}
    static bool Held(EntityManager em,List<Entity> members){if(members.Count==0)return false;foreach(var e in members)if(!em.HasComponent<HoldPositionOrderTag>(e)||em.HasComponent<EngageTarget>(e))return false;return true;}
    static Entity InitialHeldCar(EntityManager em,FixedString64Bytes id){if(initialCarGroup==0||initialCarId.Length==0)return Entity.Null;using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent),typeof(UnitHealth),typeof(Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform));using var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);foreach(var e in es){var o=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(e);if(o.FactionId==1&&o.SessionId.Equals(id)&&o.StableObjectId.ToString()==initialCarId&&em.GetComponentData<SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(e).GroupId==initialCarGroup&&S005NativeOwnedActorBridge1619.IsLivingOwnedActor(em,e,1)&&em.HasComponent<HoldPositionOrderTag>(e)&&!em.HasComponent<EngageTarget>(e))return e;}return Entity.Null;}
    static float NearRadius => defenderRole=="ApcArmored"?26f:defenderRole=="Car"?24f:0f; // Native ranges 22m/20m plus 4m near-range margin.
    static bool NearDefender(EntityManager em,FixedString64Bytes id,Entity enemy){var actor=ExactDefender(em,id);return actor!=Entity.Null&&enemy!=Entity.Null&&em.Exists(enemy)&&em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor)&&em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(enemy)&&NearRadius>0f&&Vector3.Distance(em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor).Position,em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(enemy).Position)<=NearRadius;}
    static Entity VisibleThreat(EntityManager em,FixedString64Bytes id,Entity exclude=default){var actor=ExactDefender(em,id);if(actor==Entity.Null||NearRadius<=0f||!em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor))return Entity.Null;Vector3 defenderPosition=em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor).Position;using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(UnitHealth),typeof(Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform));using var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);Entity nearest=Entity.Null;float best=NearRadius*NearRadius;foreach(var e in es){var o=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(e);if(e==exclude||o.FactionId!=2||!o.SessionId.Equals(id)||!S005NativeOwnedActorBridge1619.IsLivingOwnedActor(em,e,2)||!SkirmishFogService.IsVisibleToFaction(em,e,1)||em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(e).Current<=0||em.HasComponent<RuntimeBuildingCombatInfo>(e)||em.HasComponent<UnitAirComponent>(e))continue;Vector3 p=em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(e).Position;float d=(p-defenderPosition).sqrMagnitude;if((p-home).sqrMagnitude>65f*65f||d>best)continue;best=d;nearest=e;}return nearest;}
