    static Entity AvailableArmored(EntityManager em,FixedString64Bytes id)
    {
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent),typeof(SkirmishUnitRoleComponent),typeof(UnitHealth),typeof(Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform));using var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);
        Entity best=Entity.Null;float distance=float.PositiveInfinity;
        foreach(var e in es){var o=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(e);if(o.FactionId!=1||!o.SessionId.Equals(id)||o.IsStructure!=0||o.CapacityReleased!=0||!S005NativeOwnedActorBridge1619.IsLivingOwnedActor(em,e,1)||em.GetComponentData<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(e).Role!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.ApcArmored)continue;float d=((Vector3)em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(e).Position-home).sqrMagnitude;if(d<distance){distance=d;best=e;}}
        return best;
    }
    public static bool WantsVisibleHomeDefense(EntityManager em,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state)
    {
        if(!started||failed||lease||awaitingRouteHandoff||!initialDefenseReady||!armoredStaged||attempt!=state.SessionId.ToString()||VisibleThreat(em,state.SessionId)==Entity.Null)return false;
        return AvailableArmored(em,state.SessionId)!=Entity.Null||Members(em,state.SessionId).Count>0;
    }
    static void BindArmoredCandidate(EntityManager em,Entity actor)
    {
        var own=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);
        uint nextGroup=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(actor).GroupId;
        if(nextGroup==0)return;
        if(group==nextGroup)return;
        group=nextGroup;defender=own.StableObjectId.ToString();defenderRole="ApcArmored";placed=searchBackwards=false;homePageTouches=0;
        if(!armoredHomeChosen)
        {
            Vector3 start=em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor).Position;
            Vector3 towardCar=carHome-start;towardCar.y=0;
            if(towardCar.sqrMagnitude<20f*20f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Starting Armored APC lacks a distinct frontage approach from publicly held Car");
            armoredHome=carHome-towardCar.normalized*12f;
            armoredHome.y=carHome.y;
            armoredHomeChosen=true;
        }
        home=armoredHome;
        if(Vector3.Distance(home,carHome)<11.5f||Vector3.Distance(home,carHome)>12.5f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Distinct Armored frontage point is not safely separated from Car Hold point");
        Write("ExistingArmoredReserveCandidate",reason:"Current attempt living existing APC; distinct public frontage Move/Hold point selected 12m before Car Hold");
    }
    static void CaptureBaseline(EntityManager em,FixedString64Bytes id){var actor=ExactDefender(em,id);baselineShot=actor!=Entity.Null&&em.HasComponent<UnitAttackTraceComponent>(actor)?em.GetComponentData<UnitAttackTraceComponent>(actor).ShotCounter:0;using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(CombatDamageObservationQueueComponent),typeof(CombatDamageObservationElement));if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Native unique combat observations unavailable");baselineEvent=q.GetSingleton<CombatDamageObservationQueueComponent>().LastEventId;}
    static void ObserveCombat(EntityManager em,Entity session,FixedString64Bytes id){
        if(target==Entity.Null)return;var actor=ExactDefender(em,id);if(actor==Entity.Null)return;
        bool visible=em.Exists(target)&&SkirmishFogService.IsVisibleToFaction(em,target,1),exact=false,commanded=false;
        if(em.HasComponent<EngageTarget>(actor)){var g=em.GetComponentData<EngageTarget>(actor);exact=g.Target==target;commanded=g.IsCommanded!=0;}
        var intent=em.HasComponent<SkirmishMoveIntentComponent>(actor)?em.GetComponentData<SkirmishMoveIntentComponent>(actor):default;
        bool path=em.HasComponent<AttackMoveOrder>(actor)||em.HasComponent<UnitPathRequest>(actor);
        var f=new DefenseFrame{frame=Time.frameCount,sourceIndex=actor.Index,sourceVersion=actor.Version,targetIndex=target.Index,targetVersion=target.Version,visible=visible,exactEngaged=exact,commanded=commanded,attackMove=em.HasComponent<AttackMoveOrder>(actor),intentActive=intent.Active!=0,intentTargetExact=intent.AttackTarget==target,intentOrderAttack=intent.Order==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishGroupOrderKind.Attack,movementPath=path,shotCounter=em.HasComponent<UnitAttackTraceComponent>(actor)?em.GetComponentData<UnitAttackTraceComponent>(actor).ShotCounter:0,targetHealth=visible&&em.HasComponent<UnitHealth>(target)?em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(target).Current:-1};
        if(combatFrames.Count>4096)throw new InvalidOperationException("Bounded defense frame census exceeded");combatFrames[f.frame]=f;
        bool legal=receipt!=0&&f.intentActive&&f.intentTargetExact&&f.intentOrderAttack&&f.movementPath&&visible&&f.frame>queuedFrame&&attackMoveAccepted;
        if(legal&&!bindingSeen){bindingSeen=true;WriteCombat("NativeLaterVisibleTargetBinding",em,session,f,null,f.frame);}
        WriteCombat("DefenseFrame",em,session,f,null,0);
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(CombatDamageObservationQueueComponent),typeof(CombatDamageObservationElement));if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)return;
        foreach(var hit in em.GetBuffer<CombatDamageObservationElement>(q.GetSingletonEntity(),true)){
            if(hit.EventId<=baselineEvent||hit.Frame<queuedFrame||hit.TargetEntity!=target||hit.DamageApplied<=0)continue;
            DefenseFrame before=null;bool eventSnapshot=combatFrames.TryGetValue(hit.Frame,out var impact)&&combatFrames.TryGetValue(hit.Frame-1,out before);
            bool qualify=receipt!=0&&bindingSeen&&eventSnapshot&&before.intentActive&&before.intentTargetExact&&before.intentOrderAttack&&before.movementPath&&before.visible&&hit.SourceEntity==actor&&impact.shotCounter>baselineShot&&hit.SourceKind==CombatDamageSourceKind.DirectFire&&impact.sourceIndex==actor.Index&&impact.sourceVersion==actor.Version&&before.targetIndex==target.Index&&before.targetVersion==target.Version;
            WriteCombat("DefenseTargetHitCensus",em,session,impact??f,hit,before?.frame??0,false);
            WriteCombat(qualify?"NativeQualifiedDefenderEffect":"NativeUnqualifiedDefenderEffect",em,session,impact??f,hit,before?.frame??0,qualify);
            baselineEvent=hit.EventId;if(qualify)effectSeen=true;
        }
    }
    static void WriteCombat(string stage,EntityManager em,Entity session,DefenseFrame f,CombatDamageObservationElement? hit,int bindingFrame,bool qualified=false){
        File.AppendAllText(Evidence,JsonUtility.ToJson(new Row{stage=stage,attempt=attempt,defender=defender,target=targetId,group=group,receipt=receipt,frame=Time.frameCount,phase=phase,queuedFrame=queuedFrame,elapsed=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent>(session).ElapsedSeconds,timeScale=Time.timeScale,visible=f.visible,exactEngaged=f.exactEngaged,commanded=f.commanded,attackMove=f.attackMove,intentActive=f.intentActive,intentTargetExact=f.intentTargetExact,intentOrderAttack=f.intentOrderAttack,movementPath=f.movementPath,sourceIndex=f.sourceIndex,sourceVersion=f.sourceVersion,targetIndex=f.targetIndex,targetVersion=f.targetVersion,eventSourceIndex=hit.HasValue?hit.Value.SourceEntity.Index:0,eventSourceVersion=hit.HasValue?hit.Value.SourceEntity.Version:0,shotCounter=f.shotCounter,baselineShotCounter=baselineShot,eventId=hit?.EventId??0,eventFrame=hit?.Frame??0,damageApplied=hit?.DamageApplied??0,targetHealthAfter=hit?.TargetHealthAfter??0,targetHealth=f.targetHealth,bindingFrame=bindingFrame,sourceKind=hit?.SourceKind.ToString()??"",qualified=qualified,completed=(int)(touch?.CompletedGestures??0),dispatched=(int)(touch?.DispatchedGestures??0),unexpected=(int)(touch?.UnexpectedSamples??0),interventions=(int)(touch?.PhysicalInterventions??0),violations=AriaCommandEvidence.Violations,selected=ExclusiveSelected(em,Members(em,em.GetComponentData<SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(session).SessionId)),members=Members(em,em.GetComponentData<SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(session).SessionId).Count,passed=true})+"\n");
    }
    // Read-only native public-input census. No command-buffer mutation or hidden target-pose reads.
    [Serializable] sealed class CommandCensus {
        public string stage="",attempt="",defender="",target="",reason="",commandMode="",uiRaycasts="",physicsRaycasts="",nativeKind="",message="",hudMessage="";
        public uint group,receipt;public int frame,phase,queuedFrame,attackAttempt,members,completed,dispatched,unexpected,interventions,violations,pendingSelection,pendingAttack,resultCount,acceptedResultCount,targetHealth,requestId,resultFrame,resultSourceIndex,resultSourceVersion,resultTargetIndex,resultTargetVersion,resultReason,hudResultCount,hudReason;
        public float elapsed,timeScale,screenX,screenY,targetX,targetZ;public bool passed=true,selected,visible,held,busy,running,safeArea,projectionValid,commandResultPresent,commandResultAccepted,hudPresent,hudAccepted;
    }
    static int PendingCommands(EntityManager em) {
        int count=0;using(var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(RtsSelectionCommandIntentRequestElement)))using(var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp))foreach(var e in es)foreach(var r in em.GetBuffer<RtsSelectionCommandIntentRequestElement>(e,true))if(r.Frame>=queuedFrame&&(r.Kind is RtsSelectionCommandIntentKind.Attack or RtsSelectionCommandIntentKind.Move))count++;
        using(var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(UnitAttackOrderRequestElement)))using(var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp))foreach(var e in es)foreach(var r in em.GetBuffer<UnitAttackOrderRequestElement>(e,true))if(r.FactionId==1)count++;
        return count;
    }
    static bool HasAcceptedNativeResult(EntityManager em) {
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(RtsSelectionCommandResultElement));using var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);foreach(var e in es)foreach(var r in em.GetBuffer<RtsSelectionCommandResultElement>(e,true))if(r.Frame>=queuedFrame&&r.Frame<=Time.frameCount&&r.HasCommandResult!=0&&r.Accepted!=0&&(r.Kind is RtsSelectionCommandIntentKind.Attack or RtsSelectionCommandIntentKind.Move))return true;return false;
    }
    static CommandCensus CensusBase(string stage,EntityManager em,Entity session) {
        var state=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(session);var members=Members(em,state.SessionId);bool visible=target!=Entity.Null&&em.Exists(target)&&SkirmishFogService.IsVisibleToFaction(em,target,1);
        var c=new CommandCensus{stage=stage,attempt=attempt,defender=defender,target=targetId,group=group,receipt=receipt,frame=Time.frameCount,phase=phase,queuedFrame=queuedFrame,attackAttempt=attackAttempts,elapsed=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent>(session).ElapsedSeconds,timeScale=Time.timeScale,members=members.Count,selected=ExclusiveSelected(em,members),visible=visible,held=Held(em,members),completed=(int)(touch?.CompletedGestures??0),dispatched=(int)(touch?.DispatchedGestures??0),unexpected=(int)(touch?.UnexpectedSamples??0),interventions=(int)(touch?.PhysicalInterventions??0),violations=AriaCommandEvidence.Violations,busy=touch?.IsBusy??false,running=touch?.IsRunning??false};
        if(stage=="BeforePublicAttackTouch")c.attackAttempt=attackAttempts+1;
        c.targetHealth=visible&&em.HasComponent<UnitHealth>(target)?em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(target).Current:-1;
        var hudFeedback=UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<BattleHudRuntimeFeedbackView>(FindObjectsInactive.Exclude,FindObjectsSortMode.None);c.hudResultCount=hudFeedback.Length;if(hudFeedback.Length==1){c.hudPresent=hudFeedback[0].HasLastCommandResult;var result=hudFeedback[0].LastCommandResult;c.hudAccepted=result.Accepted;c.hudReason=(int)result.ReasonCode;c.hudMessage=result.Message??"";} // Timeless last-result snapshot only; never inferred as this Touch's acceptance.

        c.commandMode=UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadMatchHudCommandState(out var mode)?mode.ActiveCommandMode.ToString():"Unavailable";
        if(visible&&em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(target)){var p=em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(target).Position;c.targetX=p.x;c.targetZ=p.z;}
        var camera=Camera.main;if(camera!=null&&visible){Vector3 p=new(c.targetX,targetPosition.y,c.targetZ);var screen=camera.WorldToScreenPoint(p);c.screenX=screen.x;c.screenY=screen.y;c.projectionValid=screen.z>0;c.safeArea=c.projectionValid&&Screen.safeArea.Contains(screen);if(c.projectionValid){
            var hits=new List<UnityEngine.EventSystems.RaycastResult>();if(UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current!=null)UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current.RaycastAll(new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current){position=screen},hits);for(int i=0;i<Math.Min(hits.Count,8);i++)c.uiRaycasts+=(i==0?"":"|")+hits[i].gameObject.name;
            var physical=Physics.RaycastAll(camera.ScreenPointToRay(screen));Array.Sort(physical,(a,b)=>a.distance.CompareTo(b.distance));for(int i=0;i<Math.Min(physical.Length,8);i++)c.physicsRaycasts+=(i==0?"":"|")+physical[i].collider.name;
        }}return c;
    }
    static void WriteCensus(CommandCensus c)=>File.AppendAllText(Evidence,JsonUtility.ToJson(c)+"\n");
    static void CaptureCommandCensus(string stage,EntityManager em,Entity session) {
        var c=CensusBase(stage,em,session);using(var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(RtsSelectionCommandIntentRequestElement)))using(var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp))foreach(var e in es)foreach(var r in em.GetBuffer<RtsSelectionCommandIntentRequestElement>(e,true))if(r.Frame>=queuedFrame&&(r.Kind is RtsSelectionCommandIntentKind.Attack or RtsSelectionCommandIntentKind.Move))c.pendingSelection++;
        using(var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(UnitAttackOrderRequestElement)))using(var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp))foreach(var e in es)foreach(var r in em.GetBuffer<UnitAttackOrderRequestElement>(e,true))if(r.FactionId==1)c.pendingAttack++;
        using(var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(RtsSelectionCommandResultElement)))using(var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp))foreach(var e in es)foreach(var r in em.GetBuffer<RtsSelectionCommandResultElement>(e,true)){if(r.Frame<queuedFrame||r.Frame>Time.frameCount)continue;c.resultCount++;if(r.HasCommandResult!=0&&r.Accepted!=0&&(r.Kind is RtsSelectionCommandIntentKind.Attack or RtsSelectionCommandIntentKind.Move))c.acceptedResultCount++;if(c.resultCount<=16){var result=CensusBase("NativeSelectionResult",em,session);result.nativeKind=r.Kind.ToString();result.requestId=r.RequestId;result.resultFrame=r.Frame;result.resultSourceIndex=r.SourceEntity.Index;result.resultSourceVersion=r.SourceEntity.Version;result.resultTargetIndex=r.TargetEntity.Index;result.resultTargetVersion=r.TargetEntity.Version;result.commandResultPresent=r.HasCommandResult!=0;result.commandResultAccepted=r.Accepted!=0;result.resultReason=r.ReasonCode;result.message=r.Message.ToString();WriteCensus(result);}}
        WriteCensus(c);
    }
    static void Restore(string reason){if(!lease)return;F("manualDue").SetValue(null,originalDue);lease=false;Write("ScheduleRestored",reason:reason);if(!failed&&(reason=="PublicDefenseCompletedResumeAssault"||reason=="PublicVisibleThreatAttackEffect"||reason=="ThreatVanishedBeforeTouch"||reason=="ThreatOutsideNearRangeBeforeTouch")){completedCycleCount++;if(S005ManualFogFallback1619.SuspendedForDefense)awaitingRouteHandoff=true;}phase=0;touch=null;next=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup+1;}
    static void Begin(AriaTouchInputUiSystemHelper actual,double now,EntityManager em,Entity session,string reason){touch=actual;originalDue=(double)F("manualDue").GetValue(null);F("manualDue").SetValue(null,now+125);lease=true;phase=1;deadline=now+120;receipt=0;attackMoveAccepted=effectSeen=bindingSeen=searchBackwards=neutralizedRecorded=cameraSelectionLost=cameraReselectTouchIssued=false;homePageTouches=preTouchRetargets=cameraStep=cameraAttempts=cameraCloseAttempts=cameraReselectCount=0;cameraActor=Entity.Null;mapLayoutFrame=-1;mapLayoutOwner=null;mapLayoutBounds=mapLayoutViewport=default;mapFrom=mapGoal=default;mapFromFront=mapGoalFront=mapLayoutStable=false;cameraDriverIdentityBefore=cameraDeviceBefore=cameraProbeStageBefore=cameraProbeMapStepBefore=cameraProbeMapAttemptsBefore=0;cameraGenerationBefore=cameraStartedRunsBefore=0;cameraProbeTouchBefore=null;cameraCallbacksBefore="";previousTargetId="";previousTargetLiving=previousTargetVisible=previousTargetNear=false;movementNext=0;nativeMoveGoalCaptured=observedMovePath=nativeProjectedSettlement=false;nativeMoveGoal=default;moveOrigin=projectedSettledPosition=Vector3.zero;projectedSettledFrame=-1;combatFrames.Clear();attackAttempts=0;lastPairedCensus=-1;Write("LeaseStarted",em,session,reason,visible:target!=Entity.Null&&em.Exists(target)&&SkirmishFogService.IsVisibleToFaction(em,target,1));}
    static void PageTowardDefender(MatchHudSquadTrayView tray,Game.UI.Contracts.UiExpandedSquadPage page)
    {
        if(page.PageCount<1||page.PageCount>12||homePageTouches>=page.PageCount*2)throw new InvalidOperationException("Bounded public home-defender page search exhausted");
        if(!searchBackwards&&page.NextPage){if(Tap(tray.NextPageButton)){homePageTouches++;Write("PublicDefenderNextPageTouch",reason:"Monotone forward search for living current-attempt defender card");}return;}
        searchBackwards=true;
        if(page.PreviousPage){if(Tap(tray.PreviousPageButton)){homePageTouches++;Write("PublicDefenderPreviousPageTouch",reason:"Monotone backward search after public right edge");}return;}
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Living reserved home-defender card absent from all presented roster pages");
    }
    static void Tick(){try{
        if(!started||failed||!EditorApplication.isPlaying||Time.frameCount==lastFrame)return;lastFrame=Time.frameCount;
        var w=World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;if(w==null||!w.IsCreated)return;var em=w.EntityManager;
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent),typeof(SkirmishMatchState),typeof(SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent));if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)return;var session=q.GetSingletonEntity();var state=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(session);var match=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishMatchState>(session);
        if(lease&&phase is 5 or 6)ObserveCombat(em,session,state.SessionId);
        if(attempt.Length>0&&attempt!=state.SessionId.ToString())throw new InvalidOperationException("Home defender foreign attempt");
        if(match.Phase!=SkirmishPhase.Playing){if(lease){if(touch.IsBusy||touch.CompletedGestures!=touch.DispatchedGestures)throw new InvalidOperationException("Unclosed defender Touch at native terminal");Restore("NativeFinished:"+match.Outcome);}return;}
        if(!S005EarlyRoute1619.PaidReady)return;
        var actual=Current;if(actual==null){if(lease){bool save=(bool)F("playerResumeProbeActive").GetValue(null)&&(int)F("playerResumeStep").GetValue(null)==1;var nextTouch=F("playerResumeTouch").GetValue(null) as AriaTouchInputUiSystemHelper;if(!save||touch.IsBusy||touch.IsRunning||touch.CompletedGestures!=touch.DispatchedGestures||nextTouch==null||!nextTouch.IsRunning)throw new InvalidOperationException("Unverified defender input owner change");Restore("VerifiedNativeSaveHandoff");}return;}
        foreach(var frame in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<MonoBehaviour>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))if(frame.GetType().FullName=="Game.Editor.SkirmishS004ManualInputFrame"){if(!frames.ContainsKey(frame))frames.Add(frame,frame.enabled);frame.enabled=false;}
        actual.Tick(Time.unscaledTime);if(actual.PlayerInterrupted||actual.UnexpectedSamples!=0||actual.PhysicalInterventions!=0||UiShellRuntimeGateway.ReadAriaPlay().Active||!Mathf.Approximately(Time.timeScale,1))throw new InvalidOperationException("Defender native input/normal time invariant violated");
        if(S005ManualFogFallback1619.OwnsLease||!S005NativeIntroReady1619.Ready(em))return;double now=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;
        var existingDrawer=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<BuildDrawerView>(FindObjectsInactive.Include);
        if(!lease&&!S005EarlyRoute1619.OwnsSelection&&!S005ManualFogFallback1619.SuspendedForDefense&&((bool)F("manualCameraFocusPending").GetValue(null)||(existingDrawer!=null&&existingDrawer.IsOpen))){M("TickS004ManualPlayerFrame").Invoke(null,null);return;}
        var hud=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<SkirmishMatchView>(FindObjectsInactive.Include);var tray=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<MatchHudSquadTrayView>(FindObjectsInactive.Include);var controls=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<MatchOverlayCommandControlsView>(FindObjectsInactive.Include);if(hud==null||tray==null||controls==null){if(!lease)M("TickS004ManualPlayerFrame").Invoke(null,null);return;}
        if(!UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadMatchHudSquadTray(out var model)||!UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadExpandedSquadPage(out var page))return;
        if(attempt.Length==0){attempt=state.SessionId.ToString();Vector3 f=hud.EnemyBaseObjectivePosition-hud.PlayerBaseObjectivePosition;f.y=0;carHome=hud.PlayerBaseObjectivePosition+(f.sqrMagnitude>1?f.normalized*18f:Vector3.zero);home=carHome;}
        var members=Members(em,state.SessionId);
        if(group!=0&&members.Count==0){Write("DefenderLost",em,session,"Actual native casualty; no revive/replacement injection");if(lease)Restore("DefenderLost");group=0;placed=searchBackwards=false;homePageTouches=0;}
        if(group==0)
        {
            for(int i=0;i<5;i++)
            {
                var c=model.GetCard(i);if(!c.Visible||c.GroupId==0||c.AliveCount<=0)continue;
                using var aq=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent),typeof(SkirmishUnitRoleComponent),typeof(UnitHealth));using var es=aq.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);
                foreach(var e in es){var o=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(e);if(o.FactionId==1&&o.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId)&&S005NativeOwnedActorBridge1619.IsLivingOwnedActor(em,e,1)&&em.GetComponentData<SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(e).GroupId==c.GroupId&&em.GetComponentData<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(e).Role==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.Car){group=c.GroupId;defender=o.StableObjectId.ToString();defenderRole="Car";home=carHome;if(initialCarGroup==group&&initialCarId==defender&&Held(em,Members(em,state.SessionId)))placed=true;Write("BoundPublicHomeDefender",em,session,"Actual presented card "+c.Title);if(placed)Write("PreviouslyHeldCarReserve",em,session,"Same living initial publicly held Car re-bound without a duplicate Move");break;}}
                if(group!=0)break;
            }
            if(group==0){var armored=AvailableArmored(em,state.SessionId);if(armored!=Entity.Null)BindArmoredCandidate(em,armored);}
        }
        if(initialDefenseReady&&!armoredStaged&&!lease&&!awaitingRouteHandoff){var armored=AvailableArmored(em,state.SessionId);if(armored==Entity.Null)throw new InvalidOperationException("Existing living Armored APC unavailable for public pre-route home staging");BindArmoredCandidate(em,armored);}
        if(!lease&&!awaitingRouteHandoff&&group!=0&&(!initialDefenseReady||!armoredStaged||!S005EarlyRoute1619.OwnsSelection)&&!actual.IsBusy&&now>=next)
        {
            var threat=VisibleThreat(em,state.SessionId);
            if(armoredStaged&&threat!=Entity.Null){var armored=AvailableArmored(em,state.SessionId);if(armored!=Entity.Null)BindArmoredCandidate(em,armored);}
            target=threat;
            if(!armoredStaged)target=Entity.Null;
            targetId=target!=Entity.Null?em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(target).StableObjectId.ToString():"";
            if(!placed||target!=Entity.Null)Begin(actual,now,em,session,target==Entity.Null?(initialDefenseReady?"PublicArmoredFrontageMoveHold":"PublicHomeFrontageMoveHold"):"CurrentVisibleHomeThreat");
        }
        if(!lease)
        {
            if(S005EarlyRoute1619.OwnsSelection||S005ManualFogFallback1619.SuspendedForDefense)return;
            int slot=(int)F("manualSlot").GetValue(null),step=(int)F("manualStep").GetValue(null);if(slot>=0&&slot<5&&step>=0&&step<=3&&model.GetCard(slot).GroupId==group&&!actual.IsBusy){F("manualSlot").SetValue(null,slot+1);F("manualStep").SetValue(null,0);Write("ReservedDefenderSkippedOffensiveSweep",em,session,"QA slot only; native Hold/order unchanged");}M("TickS004ManualPlayerFrame").Invoke(null,null);return;
        }
        if(!ReferenceEquals(actual,touch)||!touch.IsRunning)throw new InvalidOperationException("Defender driver changed or stopped during lease");if(cameraStep!=0)EnsureCameraWitness();if(now>deadline)throw new TimeoutException("Bounded public home-defense sequence timed out");if(touch.IsBusy||now<next)return;members=Members(em,state.SessionId);
        if(phase==1){
            if(cameraSelectionLost)EnsureCameraActor(em,state.SessionId);
            if(ExclusiveSelected(em,members)){
                if(cameraSelectionLost){
                    if(!cameraReselectTouchIssued){if(Tap(tray.ClearSelectionButton))Write("PublicHomeCameraClearSelectionTouch",em,session,"Public clear before required exact card reselection");return;}
                    if(cameraReselectCount>=3)throw new InvalidOperationException("Bounded public home camera reselection exhausted");
                    cameraReselectCount++;cameraSelectionLost=cameraReselectTouchIssued=false;
                    Write("PublicHomeCameraReselected",em,session,"Same living current-attempt actor selected exclusively after public card Touch and map Close");
                }else Write("BoundPublicHomeDefender",em,session,"Exact current living card-selected defender cohort");
                phase=2;return;
            }
            if(page.SelectedGroups>0){if(Tap(tray.ClearSelectionButton))Write(cameraSelectionLost?"PublicHomeCameraClearSelectionTouch":"PublicDefenderClearSelectionTouch",em,session,"Public clear of nonexclusive selection");return;}
            for(int i=0;i<5;i++)if(model.GetCard(i).Visible&&model.GetCard(i).AliveCount>0&&model.GetCard(i).GroupId==group){if(Tap(tray.VisibleCardButton(i))){if(cameraSelectionLost){cameraReselectTouchIssued=true;Write("PublicHomeCameraReselectTouch",em,session,"Actual presented exact living defender card Touch after camera selection loss");}else Write("PublicDefenderCardTouch",em,session,"Actual presented living defender card");}return;}
            PageTowardDefender(tray,page);return;
        }
        if(phase==2&&cameraStep!=0){
            if(!ExclusiveSelected(em,members)&&!cameraSelectionLost){EnsureCameraActor(em,state.SessionId);cameraSelectionLost=true;cameraReselectTouchIssued=false;Write("PublicHomeCameraSelectionLost",em,session,"Public full-map gesture cleared selection; exact living actor and zero new troop orders retained");}
            if(cameraSelectionLost)EnsureCameraActor(em,state.SessionId);
            if(Focus(targetPosition,now,em,session))phase=cameraSelectionLost?1:3;
            return;
        }
        if(phase==2&&cameraSelectionLost){EnsureCameraActor(em,state.SessionId);phase=1;Write("PublicHomeCameraReselectRequired",em,session,"Map closed; public exact-card selection required before any ground order");return;}
        if(!ExclusiveSelected(em,members)){
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Defender cohort selection changed before native order");
        }
        bool attack=target!=Entity.Null;
        if(attack&&phase<=4&&(!em.Exists(target)||!SkirmishFogService.IsVisibleToFaction(em,target,1)||!S005NativeOwnedActorBridge1619.IsLivingOwnedActor(em,target,2)||!NearDefender(em,state.SessionId,target)))
        {
            previousTargetId=targetId;
            previousTargetLiving=em.Exists(target)&&em.HasComponent<UnitHealth>(target)&&em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(target).Current>0;
            previousTargetVisible=em.Exists(target)&&SkirmishFogService.IsVisibleToFaction(em,target,1);
            previousTargetNear=NearDefender(em,state.SessionId,target);
            var alternate=VisibleThreat(em,state.SessionId,target);
            if(alternate!=Entity.Null)
            {
                if(preTouchRetargets>=2)throw new InvalidOperationException("Bounded pre-Touch visible home-threat retargets exhausted");
                target=alternate;targetId=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(target).StableObjectId.ToString();preTouchRetargets++;phase=2;cameraStep=cameraAttempts=0;mapLayoutFrame=-1;mapLayoutOwner=null;next=now+.2;
                Write("PublicAlternateVisibleThreatBeforeTouch",em,session,"Prior visible target vanished before any public ground Attack Touch; new living visible current-attempt target bound",visible:true);
                return;
            }
            if(attempt!=state.SessionId.ToString()||!initialDefenseReady||!S005ManualFogFallback1619.SuspendedForDefense||attackAttempts!=0||receipt!=0||touch.CompletedGestures!=touch.DispatchedGestures||touch.IsBusy||!em.HasComponent<SkirmishBaseAssaultFactComponent>(session)||em.GetComponentData<SkirmishBaseAssaultFactComponent>(session).PlayerDesignatedAlive==0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Vanished-threat handback lacks current attempt, no prior public Attack, settled public Touch, or living home base");
            var openMap=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<MatchHudFullMapPopupView>(FindObjectsInactive.Include);
            if(openMap!=null&&openMap.IsOpen){if(Tap(openMap.CloseAction))Write("PublicHomeMapCloseForVanishedThreat",em,session,"Actual full-map Close Touch before clean route handback");return;}
            string handback=previousTargetLiving&&previousTargetVisible?"ThreatOutsideNearRangeBeforeTouch":"ThreatVanishedBeforeTouch";
            Write(handback,em,session,"No different living visible near-range home threat; no ground Attack Touch, receipt, or defender effect claimed");
            Restore(handback);
            return;
        }
        attack=target!=Entity.Null;if(!attack)targetPosition=home;else if(em.Exists(target)&&SkirmishFogService.IsVisibleToFaction(em,target,1)&&em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(target))targetPosition=em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(target).Position;
        if(phase==2){if(Focus(targetPosition,now,em,session))phase=3;return;}
        if(phase==3){if(!GroundPoint(targetPosition,out _)){phase=2;return;}string wanted=attack?"Attack":"Move";if(UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadMatchHudCommandState(out var mode)&&mode.ActiveCommandMode.ToString()==wanted){phase=4;return;}Tap(attack?controls.AttackButton:controls.MoveButton);return;}
        if(phase==4){if(attack){queuedFrame=Time.frameCount;CaptureCommandCensus("BeforePublicAttackTouch",em,session);}if(Ground(targetPosition)){receipt=0;attackMoveAccepted=effectSeen=bindingSeen=neutralizedRecorded=false;combatFrames.Clear();CaptureBaseline(em,state.SessionId);queuedFrame=Time.frameCount;queuedElapsed=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent>(session).ElapsedSeconds;completedBefore=(int)touch.CompletedGestures;lastPairedCensus=-1;if(attack)attackAttempts++;phase=5;Write("PublicWorldTouch",em,session,attack?"VisibleHomeThreat":"PublicHomeFrontage",visible:attack);}else{phase=2;}return;}
        if(phase==5){if(attack&&lastPairedCensus!=queuedFrame&&!touch.IsBusy&&touch.CompletedGestures==touch.DispatchedGestures&&touch.CompletedGestures>completedBefore){CaptureCommandCensus("AfterPairedPublicAttackTouch",em,session);lastPairedCensus=queuedFrame;}if(receipt==0){if(attack){float elapsed=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent>(session).ElapsedSeconds;if(Time.frameCount<queuedFrame+4||elapsed<queuedElapsed+2f||touch.CompletedGestures!=touch.DispatchedGestures||touch.CompletedGestures<=completedBefore)return;int pending=PendingCommands(em);CaptureCommandCensus("NoReceiptDecision",em,session);if(HasAcceptedNativeResult(em))throw new InvalidOperationException("Native accepted result lacks verified receipt; preserve acceptance, do not duplicate public order");if(pending>0){if(elapsed>queuedElapsed+8f)throw new InvalidOperationException("Native defender command remains pending; no duplicate request or fabricated failure");return;}if(attackAttempts>=3)throw new InvalidOperationException("Three actual paired public Attack touches produced no accepted native receipt");Write("NoReceiptRetry",em,session,"PairedTouchNoReceiptNoPending: reacquire current public visible threat");target=VisibleThreat(em,state.SessionId);targetId=target!=Entity.Null?em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(target).StableObjectId.ToString():"";if(target==Entity.Null){Restore("NoVisibleThreatAfterUnacceptedTouch");return;}phase=2;next=now+.2;return;}if(now>deadline-90)throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual native defender command was not accepted; no receipt fabricated");return;}if(attack){if(!attackMoveAccepted)return;foreach(var e in members){if(!em.HasComponent<SkirmishMoveIntentComponent>(e))return;var intent=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishMoveIntentComponent>(e);if(intent.Active==0||intent.Order!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishGroupOrderKind.Attack||intent.AttackTarget!=target||(!em.HasComponent<AttackMoveOrder>(e)&&!em.HasComponent<UnitPathRequest>(e)))return;}}if(!attack){if(members.Count!=1||!em.HasComponent<UnitPathRequest>(members[0])||!em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(members[0]))return;nativeMoveGoal=em.GetComponentData<UnitPathRequest>(members[0]).Goal;nativeMoveGoalCaptured=true;moveOrigin=em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(members[0]).Position;if(em.HasComponent<UnitTarget>(members[0])&&!em.GetComponentData<UnitTarget>(members[0]).Cell.Equals(nativeMoveGoal))throw new InvalidOperationException("Native projected Move target differs from its path request");}Write("AcceptedDefenderCommand",em,session,attack?"VisibleHomeThreatAttack":"HomeMove",visible:attack&&em.Exists(target)&&SkirmishFogService.IsVisibleToFaction(em,target,1));if(attack){phase=6;attackUntil=now+40;}else phase=7;return;}
        if(phase==6){if(!effectSeen){bool dead=em.Exists(target)&&em.HasComponent<UnitHealth>(target)&&em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(target).Current<=0;if(!dead&&!em.Exists(target))foreach(var sample in combatFrames.Values)if(sample.targetHealth==0){dead=true;break;}if(dead){if(!neutralizedRecorded){if(combatFrames.TryGetValue(Time.frameCount,out var census))WriteCombat("TargetNeutralizedBeforeDefenderEffect",em,session,census,null,0);neutralizedRecorded=true;}var alternate=VisibleThreat(em,state.SessionId,target);if(alternate!=Entity.Null&&attackAttempts<3){target=alternate;targetId=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(target).StableObjectId.ToString();receipt=0;attackMoveAccepted=effectSeen=bindingSeen=neutralizedRecorded=false;combatFrames.Clear();phase=2;next=now+.2;Write("PublicAlternateVisibleLivingThreatChosen",em,session,"Prior target reached zero health without exact defender effect; new public Attack Touch required",visible:true);return;}if(now<attackUntil)return;throw new InvalidOperationException("No different visible living threat for bounded public defender re-attack after unattributed target death");}if(now<attackUntil)return;throw new InvalidOperationException("Bounded native defense lacks exact source-attributed public Attack effect");}Write("PublicVisibleThreatEffectCompleted",em,session,"Exact native attributed public defender effect");Restore("PublicVisibleThreatAttackEffect");return;}
        if(phase==7){if(members.Count!=1||!nativeMoveGoalCaptured)throw new InvalidOperationException("Accepted public home Move lost exact living actor or native projected goal");var actor=members[0];observedMovePath|=em.HasComponent<UnitPathFollow>(actor);if(now>=movementNext){Write("NativeDefenderMovementFrame",em,session,"Exact selected actor, native projected goal, position, HP and path state");movementNext=now+1;}var position=em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor).Position;bool arrived=Vector3.Distance(position,home)<=6f;if(!arrived&&defenderRole=="ApcArmored"){
                var car=InitialHeldCar(em,state.SessionId);
                bool settled=car!=Entity.Null&&observedMovePath&&em.HasComponent<UnitGrid>(actor)&&em.GetComponentData<UnitGrid>(actor).Cell.Equals(nativeMoveGoal)&&!em.HasComponent<UnitPathRequest>(actor)&&!em.HasComponent<UnitPathFollow>(actor)&&!em.HasComponent<UnitLongDistanceMove>(actor)&&!em.HasComponent<UnitPathRetryCooldown>(actor)&&!em.HasComponent<EngageTarget>(actor)&&Vector3.Distance(position,moveOrigin)>=15f&&Vector3.Distance(position,home)<=15f&&Vector3.Distance(position,carHome)<=20f&&Vector3.Distance(position,em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(car).Position)>=8f;
                if(!settled){projectedSettledFrame=-1;return;}
                if(projectedSettledFrame<0){projectedSettledFrame=Time.frameCount;projectedSettledPosition=position;return;}
                if(Time.frameCount<projectedSettledFrame+4||Vector3.Distance(position,projectedSettledPosition)>.25f)return;
                Write("NativeProjectedFrontageSettled",em,session,"Native accepted vehicle Move projected the public click to its own reachable grid goal; same actor completed path and stopped near Car");
                nativeProjectedSettlement=true;armoredHome=position;home=position;targetPosition=position;
                arrived=true;
            }if(!arrived)return;phase=8;return;}
        if(phase==8){if(Tap(controls.HoldButton)){phase=9;Write("PublicHoldTouch",em,session,"Actual presented Hold control");}return;}
        if(phase==9){if(!Held(em,members))return;placed=true;if(defenderRole=="Car"){initialDefenseReady=true;initialCarGroup=group;initialCarId=defender;}else if(defenderRole=="ApcArmored"&&initialDefenseReady){armoredStaged=true;}else throw new InvalidOperationException("Public home staging finished with wrong defender role/order");Write("NativeHomeHeld",em,session,"Actual Hold Touch and held living cohort; Hold has no SelectionOrder receipt");Restore("PublicDefenseCompletedResumeAssault");}
    }catch(Exception error){failed=true;failure=error.ToString();Write("Failure",reason:failure,pass:false);Debug.LogError("[S005ManualHomeDefense1619] failed=1 reason="+error.Message);Stop("FailedDefenseCleanup");}}
}

// External read-only bridge to the exact shared native authority; no copied predicate/fallback.
public static class S005NativeOwnedActorBridge1619
{
    static readonly Func<EntityManager,Entity,byte,bool,bool> Native=Resolve();
    static Func<EntityManager,Entity,byte,bool,bool> Resolve()
    {
        var type=typeof(SkirmishFogService).Assembly.GetType("Game.Runtime.SkirmishActorCommandAuthority",true);
        var method=type.GetMethod("IsLivingOwnedActor",BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.Static,null,new[]{typeof(EntityManager),typeof(Entity),typeof(byte),typeof(bool)},null);
        if(method==null||method.ReturnType!=typeof(bool))throw new MissingMethodException("Exact native IsLivingOwnedActor authority unavailable");
        return (Func<EntityManager,Entity,byte,bool,bool>)method.CreateDelegate(typeof(Func<EntityManager,Entity,byte,bool,bool>));
    }
    public static void Validate(){if(Native==null)throw new InvalidOperationException("Native owned-actor authority bridge unavailable");}
    public static bool IsLivingOwnedActor(EntityManager em,Entity actor,byte faction)=>Native(em,actor,faction,false);
}

    static SkirmishMapLayoutConfig ReadRoute()
    {
        var layout=AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<SkirmishMapLayoutConfig>(Asset);
        if(layout==null||layout.LayoutId!="layout.skirmish.db.ba"||!layout.HasWorldBinding||!layout.TryGetRoute(Route,out var route)||route.Kind!=SkirmishMeasuredRouteKind.FlankSouthSweep||!route.AllowsVehicles||route.WaypointAnchorIds.Length!=6)throw new InvalidOperationException("Existing typed southern Campaign route unavailable; no route fabricated.");
        for(int i=0;i<4;i++)if(route.WaypointAnchorIds[i+1]!=Anchors[i])throw new InvalidOperationException("Authored southern route identity changed.");
        return layout;
    }
    public static void ValidatePreparedRoute(){ReadRoute();if(ResumeDone==null)throw new MissingFieldException("Native recovery completion bridge absent.");}
    public static void Start()
    {
        ValidatePreparedRoute();using(File.Open(Output,FileMode.CreateNew,FileAccess.Write,FileShare.Read)){}using(File.Open(ReinforcementOutput,FileMode.CreateNew,FileAccess.Write,FileShare.Read)){}
        attempted=complete=qualifiedDamageSeen=reinforcementComplete=directArmed=directVisibleAtArm=false;leg=completedFrame=directReceiptFrame=directTargetHp=0;directReceipt=0;directTarget=Entity.Null;directTargetBinding=null;attempt="";points.Clear();actors.Clear();continuous.Clear();completionShots.Clear();directShots.Clear();seenEvents.Clear();
        var layout=ReadRoute();foreach(var id in Anchors){if(!layout.TryGetAnchorById(id,out var a)||!layout.TryProjectLocalToMap(a.WorldX,a.WorldZ,out float x,out float z))throw new InvalidOperationException("Existing route projection unavailable.");points.Add(new Vector3(x,0,z));}
    }
    public static bool WantsSelectedCohort(EntityManager em,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state)
    {
        if(attempted||!S005EarlyRoute1619.PaidReady)return false;
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SelectedUnitTag),typeof(SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(SkirmishUnitRoleComponent),typeof(UnitHealth));using var selected=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);
        if(selected.Length==0)return false;
        foreach(var actor in selected){var o=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);if(o.FactionId!=1||!o.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId)||o.IsStructure!=0||em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(actor).Current<=0||em.GetComponentData<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor).Role!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.Rocketeer)return false;}
        return true;
    }
    static bool FreshReinforcementActor(EntityManager em,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state,Entity actor)
    {
        if(!complete||!em.Exists(actor)||actors.ContainsKey(actor)||!em.HasComponent<SelectedUnitTag>(actor)||!em.HasComponent<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor)||!em.HasComponent<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor)||!em.HasComponent<UnitHealth>(actor))return false;
        var own=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);
        if(own.FactionId!=1||!own.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId)||own.IsStructure!=0||own.ReservationId==0||own.CapacityReleased!=0||em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(actor).Current<=0||em.HasComponent<UnitAirMovement>(actor)||em.GetComponentData<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor).Role!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.Rocketeer||!em.HasComponent<SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(actor))return false;
        uint group=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(actor).GroupId;
        using var sessions=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent));
        if(sessions.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)return false;
        var session=sessions.GetSingletonEntity();
        if(!em.HasBuffer<SkirmishProductionReservation>(session))return false;
        foreach(var paid in em.GetBuffer<SkirmishProductionReservation>(session,true))
            if(paid.ReservationId==own.ReservationId&&paid.ProductionGroupId==group&&paid.FactionId==1&&paid.Role==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.Rocketeer&&paid.Phase==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishReservationPhase.Live&&paid.MaterialsPaid==120&&paid.DeliveredMembers>0)return true;
        return false;
    }
    public static bool IsFreshReinforcementCohort(EntityManager em,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state,IReadOnlyList<Entity> cohort)
    {
        if(cohort==null||cohort.Count==0)return false;
        uint receipt=0,group=0;
        foreach(var actor in cohort){if(!FreshReinforcementActor(em,state,actor))return false;var own=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);var member=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(actor);if(receipt==0){receipt=own.ReservationId;group=member.GroupId;}else if(receipt!=own.ReservationId||group!=member.GroupId)return false;}
        return true;
    }
    public static bool WantsFreshReinforcementCohort(EntityManager em,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state)
    {
        if(!complete)return false;
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SelectedUnitTag),typeof(SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(SkirmishUnitRoleComponent),typeof(UnitHealth));using var selected=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);
        if(selected.Length==0)return false;
        foreach(var actor in selected)if(!FreshReinforcementActor(em,state,actor))return false;
        return true;
    }
    static void WriteReinforcement(Row row,int round)
    {
        row.frame=Time.frameCount;row.attempt=attempt;row.route=Route;row.reason="CurrentAttemptSelectedRocketeer_PublicTouchOnly_ExistingTypedAnchors";

    }
    [Serializable] public sealed class ReinforcementRow
    {
        public string stage="",route="",anchor="",attempt="",stable="",reason="";
        public int frame,round,leg,livingMembers,reachedMembers;public uint receipt;
        public float elapsed,goalX,goalZ,x,z,distance;public bool allLivingReached,postPaid,ownCurrent;

    public static void BeginReinforcement(EntityManager em,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state,IReadOnlyList<Entity> cohort,int round,float elapsed)
    {
        if(!IsFreshReinforcementCohort(em,state,cohort)||state.SessionId.ToString()!=attempt||ReadRoute().LayoutId!="layout.skirmish.db.ba")throw new InvalidOperationException("Fresh public Rocketeer cohort/authored route unavailable.");
        foreach(var actor in cohort){var own=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);WriteReinforcement(new Row{stage="ReinforcementRouteStarted",anchor=Anchors[0],stable=own.StableObjectId.ToString(),leg=0,elapsed=elapsed,goalX=points[0].x,goalZ=points[0].z,postPaid=true,ownCurrent=true},round);}
    }
    public static bool TryArriveReinforcement(EntityManager em,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state,IReadOnlyList<Entity> cohort,int round,int routeLeg,uint receipt,float elapsed)
    {
        if(receipt==0||routeLeg<0||routeLeg>=4||!complete||state.SessionId.ToString()!=attempt)throw new InvalidOperationException("Fresh southern route lacks accepted public AttackMove receipt/identity.");
        int living=0,reached=0;foreach(var actor in cohort){if(!em.Exists(actor)||!em.HasComponent<UnitHealth>(actor)||em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(actor).Current<=0)continue;
            if(!FreshReinforcementActor(em,state,actor)||!em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor))throw new InvalidOperationException("Fresh reinforcement authority/selection changed.");
            var own=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);var p=(Vector3)em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor).Position;float distance=Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(p.x,p.z),new Vector2(points[routeLeg].x,points[routeLeg].z));living++;if(distance<=12)reached++;
            WriteReinforcement(new Row{stage="ReinforcementRouteActor",anchor=Anchors[routeLeg],stable=own.StableObjectId.ToString(),leg=routeLeg,receipt=receipt,elapsed=elapsed,x=p.x,z=p.z,goalX=points[routeLeg].x,goalZ=points[routeLeg].z,distance=distance,postPaid=true,ownCurrent=true},round);
        }
        if(living==0)throw new InvalidOperationException("No living fresh reinforcement remains at authored waypoint.");
        bool all=reached==living;WriteReinforcement(new Row{stage=all?"ReinforcementWaypointReached":"ReinforcementWaypointWaiting",anchor=Anchors[routeLeg],leg=routeLeg,receipt=receipt,elapsed=elapsed,goalX=points[routeLeg].x,goalZ=points[routeLeg].z,livingMembers=living,reachedMembers=reached,allLivingReached=all,postPaid=true,ownCurrent=true},round);
        if(all&&routeLeg==3){reinforcementComplete=true;WriteReinforcement(new Row{stage="ReinforcementRouteCompleted",leg=4,receipt=receipt,elapsed=elapsed,livingMembers=living,reachedMembers=reached,allLivingReached=true,postPaid=true,ownCurrent=true},round);}
        return all;
    }
    public static void Begin(EntityManager em,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state,IReadOnlyList<Entity> cohort,Vector3 enemy,Vector3 player)
    {
        if(attempted||!S005EarlyRoute1619.PaidReady)throw new InvalidOperationException("Southern route requires genuine paid force first; no overlapping setup input.");
        using var setupQuery=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishResolvedSetupRecord));if(setupQuery.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual resolved layout authority not unique.");
        var setup=em.GetComponentObject<SkirmishResolvedSetupRecord>(setupQuery.GetSingletonEntity()).Setup;
        if(setup.LayoutId!=ReadRoute().LayoutId||setup.CatalogId!="S005")throw new InvalidOperationException("Runtime public setup/layout mismatch.");
        attempted=true;attempt=state.SessionId.ToString();leg=0;
        foreach(var e in cohort){var o=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(e);if(o.FactionId!=1||o.SessionId.ToString()!=attempt||em.GetComponentData<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(e).Role!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.Rocketeer)throw new InvalidOperationException("Route selected cohort must be current own Rocketeer.");actors[e]=o.StableObjectId.ToString();continuous.Add(o.StableObjectId.ToString());}
        if(continuous.Count==0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual southern assault cohort absent.");
        for(int i=0;i<points.Count;i++){var g=points[i];var axis=enemy-player;axis.y=0;var d=g-player;float cross=Mathf.Abs(axis.x*d.z-axis.z*d.x)/axis.magnitude;Write(new Row{stage="RouteGeometry",anchor=Anchors[i],leg=i,goalX=g.x,goalZ=g.z,playerX=player.x,playerZ=player.z,enemyX=enemy.x,enemyZ=enemy.z,routeAxisDistance=cross,postPaid=true,reason="ExistingSerializedLayoutProjection_PublicObjectiveAnchors_NoHiddenEnemyTransform"});}
    }
    public static bool TryArrive(EntityManager em,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state,uint receipt,float elapsed)
    {
        if(complete)return true;if(receipt==0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Authored route arrival lacks native accepted AttackMove receipt.");
        var reached=new HashSet<string>();var living=new HashSet<string>();foreach(var pair in actors){var e=pair.Key;if(!em.Exists(e)||!em.HasComponent<UnitHealth>(e)||em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(e).Current<=0||!em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(e))continue;
            var o=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(e);if(o.SessionId.ToString()!=attempt||o.FactionId!=1||o.StableObjectId.ToString()!=pair.Value)throw new InvalidOperationException("Flank cohort authority changed.");
            living.Add(pair.Value);var p=(Vector3)em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(e).Position;float distance=Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(p.x,p.z),new Vector2(points[leg].x,points[leg].z));
            Write(new Row{stage="RouteActor",anchor=Anchors[leg],stable=pair.Value,role="Rocketeer",sourceIndex=e.Index,sourceVersion=e.Version,x=p.x,z=p.z,goalX=points[leg].x,goalZ=points[leg].z,distance=distance,receipt=receipt,elapsed=elapsed,alive=true,ownCurrent=true,selected=em.HasComponent<SelectedUnitTag>(e),postPaid=true});
            if(distance<=12&&continuous.Contains(pair.Value))reached.Add(pair.Value);
        }
        if(living.Count==0)throw new InvalidOperationException("No living original route cohort remains.");
        Write(new Row{stage="WaypointCohortArrival",reason="WaitingForAllLivingOriginalMembers",livingMembers=living.Count,reachedMembers=reached.Count,allLivingReached=reached.SetEquals(living),receipt=receipt,elapsed=elapsed,postPaid=true});
        if(!reached.SetEquals(living))return false;
        continuous.IntersectWith(reached);foreach(var id in continuous)Write(new Row{stage="WaypointReached",stable=id,anchor=Anchors[leg],goalX=points[leg].x,goalZ=points[leg].z,receipt=receipt,elapsed=elapsed,alive=true,ownCurrent=true,postPaid=true});
        leg++;if(leg==points.Count){complete=true;completedFrame=Time.frameCount;foreach(var pair in actors)if(continuous.Contains(pair.Value)&&em.Exists(pair.Key)){completionShots[pair.Value]=em.HasComponent<UnitAttackTraceComponent>(pair.Key)?em.GetComponentData<UnitAttackTraceComponent>(pair.Key).ShotCounter:0;Write(new Row{stage="RouteCompleted",stable=pair.Value,receipt=receipt,elapsed=elapsed,alive=true,ownCurrent=true,postPaid=true,baselineShot=completionShots[pair.Value],completedFrame=completedFrame});}}
        return true;
    }
    static bool TryReadDesignatedTargetBinding(EntityManager em,Entity session,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state,Entity target,Row row)
    {
        var own=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(target);
        var identity=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishStructureIdentityComponent>(target);
        var objectiveRole=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishObjectiveRoleComponent>(target);
        row.targetAttempt=own.SessionId.ToString();row.targetOwnerFaction=own.FactionId;row.targetObjectiveFaction=objectiveRole.FactionId;
        row.targetStructureId=identity.StructureId.ToString();row.targetObjective=objectiveRole.StableObjectId.ToString();row.targetObjectiveKind=objectiveRole.Role.ToString();
        row.targetNativeDesignated=identity.DesignatedBase!=0;row.targetCurrentAttempt=own.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId);
        row.targetTypedEnemyBase=objectiveRole.Role==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishObjectiveRoleKind.EnemyBase;
        if(em.HasComponent<RuntimeBuildingCombatInfo>(target))row.targetRuntimeId=em.GetComponentData<RuntimeBuildingCombatInfo>(target).RuntimeBuildingId;
        if(!em.HasComponent<SkirmishResolvedSetupRecord>(session))return false;
        var setup=em.GetComponentObject<SkirmishResolvedSetupRecord>(session).Setup;if(setup==null||setup.Structures==null)return false;
        foreach(var resolved in setup.Structures)
        {
            if(resolved.FactionId!=2||!resolved.DesignatedBase)continue;
            row.resolvedBindingCount++;row.resolvedStructureId=resolved.StructureId;row.resolvedObjectiveRoleId=resolved.ObjectiveRoleId;
            row.resolvedBaseStable=resolved.StructureId+"."+resolved.FactionId;
            row.resolvedBindingExact=row.targetStructureId==resolved.StructureId&&row.targetObjective==resolved.ObjectiveRoleId&&own.StableObjectId.ToString()==row.resolvedBaseStable;
        }
        using(var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(SkirmishStructureIdentityComponent),typeof(SkirmishObjectiveRoleComponent)))
        using(var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp))foreach(var e in es)
        {
            var o=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(e);var i=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishStructureIdentityComponent>(e);var r=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishObjectiveRoleComponent>(e);
            if(o.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId)&&o.FactionId==2&&o.IsStructure!=0&&o.CapacityReleased==0&&i.DesignatedBase!=0&&r.Role==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishObjectiveRoleKind.EnemyBase&&r.FactionId==2)
            {row.typedDesignatedCount++;if(e==target)row.uniqueTypedDesignatedTarget=true;}
        }
        return own.IsStructure!=0&&own.CapacityReleased==0&&row.targetCurrentAttempt&&row.targetOwnerFaction==2&&row.targetObjectiveFaction==2&&row.targetNativeDesignated&&row.targetTypedEnemyBase&&row.resolvedBindingCount==1&&row.resolvedBindingExact&&row.typedDesignatedCount==1&&row.uniqueTypedDesignatedTarget&&row.targetRuntimeId>0;
    }
    public static bool TryReadVisibleDesignatedTarget(EntityManager em,Entity session,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state,SkirmishMatchView hud,out VisibleTargetProof proof)
    {
        proof=new VisibleTargetProof();if(hud==null||!hud.EnemyBaseMarkerVisible||!hud.EnemyBaseTargetVisible)return false;
        var point=hud.VisibleEnemyBasePoint;var es=UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current;
        if(float.IsNaN(point.x)||float.IsInfinity(point.x)||float.IsNaN(point.y)||float.IsInfinity(point.y)||!Screen.safeArea.Contains(point)||es==null||!es.isActiveAndEnabled)return false;
        foreach(var popup in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<MatchHudFullMapPopupView>(UnityEngine.FindObjectsInactive.Include))if(popup.IsOpen)return false;
        var hits=new List<UnityEngine.EventSystems.RaycastResult>();es.RaycastAll(new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(es){position=point},hits);if(hits.Count!=0)return false;
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(SkirmishStructureIdentityComponent),typeof(SkirmishObjectiveRoleComponent),typeof(UnitHealth));
        using var targets=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);int count=0;
        foreach(var e in targets)
        {
            var own=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(e);var role=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishObjectiveRoleComponent>(e);
            if(!own.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId)||own.FactionId!=2||role.Role!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishObjectiveRoleKind.EnemyBase)continue;
            if(em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(e).Current<=0)return false;var row=new Row();if(!TryReadDesignatedTargetBinding(em,session,state,e,row))return false;
            count++;proof=new VisibleTargetProof{targetStable=own.StableObjectId.ToString(),targetIndex=e.Index,targetVersion=e.Version,targetX=point.x,targetY=point.y};
        }
        return count==1;
    }
    public struct VisibleTargetProof {public string targetStable;public int targetIndex,targetVersion;public float targetX,targetY;}
    public static void ArmDirectAttack(EntityManager em,Entity session,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state,Entity target,int receiptFrame,uint receipt,IReadOnlyList<Entity> cohort)
    {
        if(!complete||directArmed||attempt!=state.SessionId.ToString()||receiptFrame<=completedFrame||receipt==0||cohort==null||cohort.Count!=4||!em.Exists(target)||!em.HasComponent<UnitHealth>(target)||em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(target).Current<=0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Exact post-route direct Barracks attack cannot be armed");
        directShots.Clear();
        foreach(var actor in cohort)
        {
            if(!actors.TryGetValue(actor,out var id)||!continuous.Contains(id)||!em.Exists(actor)||!em.HasComponent<UnitHealth>(actor)||em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(actor).Current<=0||!em.HasComponent<UnitAttackTraceComponent>(actor))throw new InvalidOperationException("Direct damage baseline missing original living actor");
            directShots.Add(id,em.GetComponentData<UnitAttackTraceComponent>(actor).ShotCounter);
        }
        var frozenBinding=new Row();var hud=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<SkirmishMatchView>();
        if(!TryReadDesignatedTargetBinding(em,session,state,target,frozenBinding)||hud==null||!hud.EnemyBaseTargetVisible)throw new InvalidOperationException("Typed public Barracks binding/visibility absent at direct damage baseline");
        directTarget=target;directTargetBinding=frozenBinding;directVisibleAtArm=true;directTargetHp=em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(target).Current;directReceiptFrame=receiptFrame;directReceipt=receipt;directArmed=true;
        Write(new Row{stage="DirectAttackDamageBaseline",targetIndex=target.Index,targetVersion=target.Version,healthAfter=directTargetHp,directTargetHp=directTargetHp,directReceiptFrame=directReceiptFrame,receipt=directReceipt,livingMembers=directShots.Count,postPaid=true,visible=true,targetStable=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(target).StableObjectId.ToString(),targetNativeDesignated=frozenBinding.targetNativeDesignated,targetCurrentAttempt=frozenBinding.targetCurrentAttempt,targetTypedEnemyBase=frozenBinding.targetTypedEnemyBase,resolvedBindingExact=frozenBinding.resolvedBindingExact,uniqueTypedDesignatedTarget=frozenBinding.uniqueTypedDesignatedTarget,reason="AfterExactFourNativeReceiptPairsAndFourOriginalActorIntents"});
    }
    public static void ObserveDamage(EntityManager em,Entity session,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state)
    {
        if(!complete||state.SessionId.ToString()!=attempt)return;
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(CombatDamageObservationQueueComponent),typeof(CombatDamageObservationElement));if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)return;
        var hud=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<SkirmishMatchView>();
        foreach(var hit in em.GetBuffer<CombatDamageObservationElement>(q.GetSingletonEntity(),true)){
            if(hit.Frame<=completedFrame||!seenEvents.Add(hit.EventId)||hit.DamageApplied<=0||!em.Exists(hit.SourceEntity)||!em.HasComponent<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(hit.SourceEntity))continue;
            var own=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(hit.SourceEntity);string id=own.StableObjectId.ToString();if(!continuous.Contains(id))continue;
            bool target=em.Exists(hit.TargetEntity)&&em.HasComponent<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(hit.TargetEntity)&&em.HasComponent<SkirmishStructureIdentityComponent>(hit.TargetEntity)&&em.HasComponent<SkirmishObjectiveRoleComponent>(hit.TargetEntity);string targetStable="",targetObjective="";
            string targetRole="";if(em.Exists(hit.TargetEntity)){if(em.HasComponent<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(hit.TargetEntity))targetStable=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(hit.TargetEntity).StableObjectId.ToString();if(em.HasComponent<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(hit.TargetEntity))targetRole=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(hit.TargetEntity).Role.ToString();else if(em.HasComponent<SkirmishStructureIdentityComponent>(hit.TargetEntity))targetRole="Structure";}
            var binding=new Row();if(target){target=TryReadDesignatedTargetBinding(em,session,state,hit.TargetEntity,binding);targetObjective=binding.targetObjective;}
            bool frozenLethal=!em.Exists(hit.TargetEntity)&&directArmed&&directVisibleAtArm&&directTargetBinding!=null&&hit.TargetEntity==directTarget&&hit.Frame>directReceiptFrame&&hit.TargetHealthAfter==0&&directTargetHp>0&&directTargetBinding.targetCurrentAttempt&&directTargetBinding.targetNativeDesignated&&directTargetBinding.targetTypedEnemyBase&&directTargetBinding.resolvedBindingExact&&directTargetBinding.uniqueTypedDesignatedTarget;
            if(frozenLethal){binding=directTargetBinding;target=true;targetStable=binding.resolvedBaseStable;targetObjective=binding.targetObjective;targetRole="Structure";}
            int shot=em.HasComponent<UnitAttackTraceComponent>(hit.SourceEntity)?em.GetComponentData<UnitAttackTraceComponent>(hit.SourceEntity).ShotCounter:0;
            int baseline=directShots.TryGetValue(id,out var frozenShot)?frozenShot:int.MaxValue;
            bool actualVisible=hud!=null&&hud.EnemyBaseTargetVisible;
            bool qualified=directArmed&&hit.Frame>directReceiptFrame&&hit.TargetEntity==directTarget&&hit.TargetHealthAfter<directTargetHp&&target&&own.FactionId==1&&own.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId)&&em.HasComponent<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(hit.SourceEntity)&&em.GetComponentData<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(hit.SourceEntity).Role==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.Rocketeer&&(actualVisible||frozenLethal)&&shot>baseline&&(hit.SourceKind==CombatDamageSourceKind.GroundMissile||hit.SourceKind==CombatDamageSourceKind.DirectFire);
            if(qualified)qualifiedDamageSeen=true;
            Write(new Row{stage="FlankBaseDamage",stable=id,role="Rocketeer",sourceIndex=hit.SourceEntity.Index,sourceVersion=hit.SourceEntity.Version,targetIndex=hit.TargetEntity.Index,targetVersion=hit.TargetEntity.Version,targetStable=targetStable,targetObjective=targetObjective,targetRole=targetRole,targetAttempt=binding.targetAttempt,targetStructureId=binding.targetStructureId,targetObjectiveKind=binding.targetObjectiveKind,resolvedStructureId=binding.resolvedStructureId,resolvedObjectiveRoleId=binding.resolvedObjectiveRoleId,resolvedBaseStable=binding.resolvedBaseStable,targetOwnerFaction=binding.targetOwnerFaction,targetObjectiveFaction=binding.targetObjectiveFaction,targetRuntimeId=binding.targetRuntimeId,resolvedBindingCount=binding.resolvedBindingCount,typedDesignatedCount=binding.typedDesignatedCount,targetNativeDesignated=binding.targetNativeDesignated,targetCurrentAttempt=binding.targetCurrentAttempt,targetTypedEnemyBase=binding.targetTypedEnemyBase,resolvedBindingExact=binding.resolvedBindingExact,uniqueTypedDesignatedTarget=binding.uniqueTypedDesignatedTarget,eventId=hit.EventId,eventFrame=hit.Frame,completedFrame=completedFrame,damage=hit.DamageApplied,healthAfter=hit.TargetHealthAfter,sourceKind=hit.SourceKind.ToString(),shotCounter=shot,baselineShot=completionShots[id],directBaselineShot=baseline,directReceiptFrame=directReceiptFrame,directTargetHp=directTargetHp,receipt=directReceipt,designatedBase=target,visible=actualVisible,visibleBeforeLethalHit=frozenLethal,targetDespawnedAfterLethal=frozenLethal,ownCurrent=own.FactionId==1&&own.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId),qualified=qualified});
        }
    }
}

// Readonly recorder/helper operands. No counter correction/reset/invented touch credit.
// External QA strategy only. It chooses a genuinely paid, living Rocketeer card
// through the presented public Touch UI, then hands the existing Touch driver to
// the same typed-route controller. This never selects ECS actors or writes orders.
public static class S005FreshRocketeerPlanner1619
{
    public static bool HasFailed => failed;
    public static string FailureReason => failure;
    const string Evidence="/Users/farhad/Projects/WarlineCapture/Design/AgentReports/SkirmishExpansion/S005/native1619-fresh-rocketeer-planner.jsonl";
    static readonly Type Harness=typeof(SkirmishS002AriaRunHarness);
    static readonly BindingFlags Flags=BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Static;
    static readonly FieldInfo Driver=Harness.GetField("manualTouch",Flags),Due=Harness.GetField("manualDue",Flags),Actions=Harness.GetField("manualActions",Flags);
    static readonly MethodInfo Point=Harness.GetMethod("TryPresentedButtonPoint",Flags);
    static bool started,active,transferred,failed,completed;
    static int lastFrame=-1,pages,gestures;
    static uint group,reservation;static string attempt="",failure="";
    static double borrowedDue,deadline,next;
    public static bool OwnsSelection=>active&&!transferred;
    [Serializable] sealed class Row {public string stage="",attempt="",reason="";public int frame,pages,gestures,dispatched,completed;public uint group,reservation;public float elapsed;public bool paidLive,selected,routeComplete,originalDamage,reinforcementComplete,playerBaseAlive,enemyBaseAlive,normalSpeed,passed;}
    static void Write(string stage,float elapsed=0,string reason="",bool paidLive=false,bool selected=false,bool playerBaseAlive=true,bool enemyBaseAlive=true,bool passed=true)
    {
        var touch=Driver?.GetValue(null) as AriaTouchInputUiSystemHelper;
        File.AppendAllText(Evidence,JsonUtility.ToJson(new Row{stage=stage,attempt=attempt,reason=reason,frame=Time.frameCount,pages=pages,gestures=gestures,dispatched=(int)(touch?.DispatchedGestures??0),completed=(int)(touch?.CompletedGestures??0),group=group,reservation=reservation,elapsed=elapsed,paidLive=paidLive,selected=selected,routeComplete=S005SouthFlank1619.RouteComplete,originalDamage=S005SouthFlank1619.QualifiedDamageSeen,reinforcementComplete=S005SouthFlank1619.ReinforcementComplete,playerBaseAlive=playerBaseAlive,enemyBaseAlive=enemyBaseAlive,normalSpeed=Mathf.Approximately(Time.timeScale,1),passed=passed})+"\n");
    }
    public static void Validate(){if(Driver==null||Due==null||Actions==null||Point==null)throw new MissingMemberException("Exact native manual public-input scheduler/point bridge unavailable");}
    public static void Start(){Validate();if(started||File.Exists(Evidence))throw new IOException("Preserve fresh Rocketeer planner evidence");using(File.Open(Evidence,FileMode.CreateNew,FileAccess.Write,FileShare.Read)){}started=true;active=transferred=failed=completed=false;lastFrame=-1;pages=gestures=0;group=reservation=0;attempt=failure="";Canvas.willRenderCanvases+=Tick;Write("Started");}
    public static void Stop(string reason){if(!started)return;Canvas.willRenderCanvases-=Tick;if(active&&!transferred)Due.SetValue(null,borrowedDue);Write("Closed",reason:reason+(failed?";"+failure:""),passed:!failed&&(!active||transferred));started=false;active=false;}
    static bool Tap(Button button,AriaTouchInputUiSystemHelper touch,double now)
    {
        if(button==null)return false;object[] args={button,default(Vector2)};if(!(bool)Point.Invoke(null,args))return false;
        if(!touch.TryGesture((Vector2)args[1],(Vector2)args[1],.15f,0,Time.unscaledTime))return false;
        Actions.SetValue(null,(int)Actions.GetValue(null)+1);gestures++;next=now+.8;return true;
    }
    static bool PaidGroup(EntityManager em,Entity session,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state,uint candidate,out uint paidReceipt)
    {
        paidReceipt=0;if(candidate==0||!em.HasBuffer<SkirmishProductionReservation>(session))return false;
        foreach(var paid in em.GetBuffer<SkirmishProductionReservation>(session,true))
            if(paid.FactionId==1&&paid.Role==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.Rocketeer&&paid.Phase==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishReservationPhase.Live&&paid.MaterialsPaid==120&&paid.DeliveredMembers>0&&paid.ProductionGroupId==candidate){if(paidReceipt!=0)return false;paidReceipt=paid.ReservationId;}
        if(paidReceipt==0)return false;
        int living=0;using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent),typeof(SkirmishUnitRoleComponent),typeof(UnitHealth));using var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);
        foreach(var actor in es){var own=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);if(own.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId)&&own.FactionId==1&&own.IsStructure==0&&own.CapacityReleased==0&&own.ReservationId==paidReceipt&&em.GetComponentData<SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(actor).GroupId==candidate&&em.GetComponentData<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(actor).Role==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.Rocketeer&&em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(actor).Current>0)living++;}
        return living>0;
    }
    static bool ExactSelectedPaidGroup(EntityManager em,SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent state)
    {
        if(group==0||reservation==0)return false;
        int selected=0;using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SelectedUnitTag),typeof(SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent));using var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);
        foreach(var actor in es){var own=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);if(!own.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId)||own.FactionId!=1||own.ReservationId!=reservation||em.GetComponentData<SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(actor).GroupId!=group)return false;selected++;}
        return selected>0&&S005SouthFlank1619.WantsFreshReinforcementCohort(em,state);
    }
    static void Tick()
    {
        if(!started||failed||!EditorApplication.isPlaying||Time.frameCount==lastFrame)return;lastFrame=Time.frameCount;
        try{
            if(transferred){if(!completed&&S005SouthFlank1619.ReinforcementComplete){completed=true;Write("ReinforcementCompleted");}return;}
            if(active&&EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup>deadline)throw new TimeoutException("45-second public fresh Rocketeer selection expired during a competing QA lease");
            if(!S005EarlyRoute1619.PaidReady||!S005SouthFlank1619.RouteComplete||!S005SouthFlank1619.QualifiedDamageSeen||S005ManualFogFallback1619.OwnsLease||S005ManualHomeDefense1619.OwnsLease)return;
            var world=World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;if(world==null||!world.IsCreated)return;var em=world.EntityManager;
            using var sessions=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent),typeof(SkirmishMatchState),typeof(SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent),typeof(SkirmishBaseAssaultFactComponent));if(sessions.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)return;
            var session=sessions.GetSingletonEntity();var state=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(session);var match=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishMatchState>(session);var clock=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent>(session);var facts=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishBaseAssaultFactComponent>(session);double now=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;
            if(match.Phase!=SkirmishPhase.Playing||facts.PlayerDesignatedAlive==0||facts.EnemyDesignatedAlive==0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Fresh Rocketeer route must start before natural terminal with both bases alive");
            if(!Mathf.Approximately(Time.timeScale,1)||clock.Paused!=0||UiShellRuntimeGateway.ReadAriaPlay().Active)throw new InvalidOperationException("Fresh public planner requires normal unpaused manual game");
            var touch=Driver.GetValue(null) as AriaTouchInputUiSystemHelper;if(touch==null||!touch.IsRunning||touch.PlayerInterrupted||touch.UnexpectedSamples!=0||touch.PhysicalInterventions!=0||AriaCommandEvidence.Violations!=0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual native manual Touch owner unavailable");
            if(!active){if(touch.IsBusy)return;attempt=state.SessionId.ToString();borrowedDue=(double)Due.GetValue(null);deadline=now+45;Due.SetValue(null,deadline+5);active=true;Write("LeaseStarted",clock.ElapsedSeconds,"Original qualified damage plus live public game",playerBaseAlive:true,enemyBaseAlive:true);}
            if(now>deadline)throw new TimeoutException("45-second public fresh Rocketeer card selection expired; no synthetic selection or order");
            if(touch.IsBusy||now<next)return;
            if(ExactSelectedPaidGroup(em,state)){
                Write("VerifiedSelected",clock.ElapsedSeconds,"Exact paid reservation and group were selected by public card Touch",paidLive:true,selected:true);
                S005ManualFogFallback1619.BeginFreshReinforcement(em,session,state,borrowedDue);transferred=true;active=false;Write("Handoff",clock.ElapsedSeconds,"Exact same native Touch driver and selected paid cohort",paidLive:true,selected:true);return;
            }
            var drawer=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<BuildDrawerView>(FindObjectsInactive.Include);if(drawer!=null&&drawer.IsOpen){if(Tap(drawer.CloseButton,touch,now))Write("PublicDrawerCloseTouch",clock.ElapsedSeconds);return;}
            var commands=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<MatchOverlayCommandControlsView>(FindObjectsInactive.Include);var tray=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<MatchHudSquadTrayView>(FindObjectsInactive.Include);
            if(commands==null||tray==null||!UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadExpandedSquadPage(out var page)||!UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadMatchHudSquadTray(out var model))return;
            if(UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadMatchHudCommandState(out var mode)&&mode.ActiveCommandMode!=Game.Tactical.Contracts.TacticalCommandMode.Select){if(Tap(commands.SelectButton,touch,now))Write("PublicSelectModeTouch",clock.ElapsedSeconds);return;}
            if(page.SelectedGroups>0){if(Tap(tray.ClearSelectionButton,touch,now))Write("PublicClearSelectionTouch",clock.ElapsedSeconds);return;}
            for(int i=0;i<5;i++){var card=model.GetCard(i);if(!card.Visible||card.GroupId==0||card.AliveCount<=0||!PaidGroup(em,session,state,card.GroupId,out uint paid))continue;group=card.GroupId;reservation=paid;if(Tap(tray.VisibleCardButton(i),touch,now))Write("PublicCardTouch",clock.ElapsedSeconds,"Current visible paid Rocketeer group",paidLive:true);return;}
            if(page.NextPage&&pages<3){if(Tap(tray.NextPageButton,touch,now)){pages++;Write("PublicNextPageTouch",clock.ElapsedSeconds);}return;}
            if(page.PreviousPage&&pages<4){if(Tap(tray.PreviousPageButton,touch,now)){pages++;Write("PublicPreviousPageTouch",clock.ElapsedSeconds);}return;}
            throw new InvalidOperationException("No live paid fresh Rocketeer card found within bounded public roster pages");
        }catch(Exception error){failed=true;failure=error.ToString();Write("Failure",reason:failure,passed:false);Debug.LogError("[S005FreshRocketeerPlanner1619] failed=1 reason="+error.Message);Stop("FailedPlannerCleanup");}
    }
}
