// External public-input correction only. Owns the native QA resume frame, not gameplay.
public static class S005CatalogTouch1619
{
    public static bool HasFailed => failed;
    const string Evidence="/Users/farhad/Projects/WarlineCapture/Design/AgentReports/SkirmishExpansion/S005/native1619-catalog-touch-census.jsonl";
    const BindingFlags Flags=BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Static;
    static readonly Dictionary<SkirmishS005PlayerResumeFrame,bool> owners=new();
    static FieldInfo F(string n)=>typeof(SkirmishS002AriaRunHarness).GetField(n,Flags)??throw new MissingFieldException(n);
    static MethodInfo M(string n)=>typeof(SkirmishS002AriaRunHarness).GetMethod(n,Flags)??throw new MissingMethodException(n);
    static bool started,failed,selectionSeen;static int lastFrame=-1,actions,rows;static double nextCensus;
    [Serializable] sealed class Row
    {
        public string stage="",target="",targetPath="",scrollPath="",contentPath="",firstHit="",dragHit="",candidateHits="",reason="",selectedPrefab="",availability="";
        public int frame,step,action,completed,dispatched,unexpected,violations,materialsCost;
        public bool active,interactable,centerPresented,targetInteriorHit,dragStartInContent,sameScrollRect,fromInViewport,toInViewport,safeEndpoints,queued,failed;
        public float timeScale,screenWidth,screenHeight,buttonX,buttonY,buttonWidth,buttonHeight,viewportX,viewportY,viewportWidth,viewportHeight,contentX,contentY,contentWidth,contentHeight,normalizedY,velocityY,localCenterY,localMinY,localMaxY,fromX,fromY,toX,toY;
    }
    static string PathOf(Transform t)=>t==null?"":t.parent==null?t.name:PathOf(t.parent)+"/"+t.name;
    public static void Validate(){
        foreach(string n in new[]{"playerResumeProbeActive","playerResumeStep","playerResumeGuardReady","playerResumeLogisticsReady","playerResumePaidRole","playerResumeRequiredReadiness","playerResumeTouch","playerResumeDue"})F(n);
        M("TickPlayerResumeFrame");
        string sample=JsonUtility.ToJson(new Row{stage="NativeCatalogSelection",selectedPrefab="Unit_Veh_APC_Heavy",materialsCost=280});
        if(!sample.Contains("\"materialsCost\":280")||!sample.Contains("\"selectedPrefab\":\"Unit_Veh_APC_Heavy\""))throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual native primitive catalog serializer schema differs");
        Debug.Log("[S005CatalogTouch1619] loadOnlySchema="+sample);
    }
    public static void Start(){if(started||File.Exists(Evidence))throw new IOException("Preserve catalog1619 evidence");using(File.Open(Evidence,FileMode.CreateNew)){}started=true;EditorApplication.update+=OwnFrame;Canvas.willRenderCanvases+=Pump;}
    public static void Stop(string reason){if(!started)return;foreach(var owner in owners)if(owner.Key!=null)owner.Key.enabled=owner.Value;owners.Clear();EditorApplication.update-=OwnFrame;Canvas.willRenderCanvases-=Pump;Write(new Row{stage="Closed",reason=reason,failed=failed});started=false;}
    static void OwnFrame(){if(!EditorApplication.isPlaying)return;foreach(var owner in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<SkirmishS005PlayerResumeFrame>(FindObjectsSortMode.None)){if(!owners.ContainsKey(owner))owners.Add(owner,owner.enabled);owner.enabled=false;}}
    static Rect ScreenRect(RectTransform rect,Camera camera){var c=new Vector3[4];rect.GetWorldCorners(c);var a=RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(camera,c[0]);var b=RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(camera,c[2]);return new Rect(Mathf.Min(a.x,b.x),Mathf.Min(a.y,b.y),Mathf.Abs(a.x-b.x),Mathf.Abs(a.y-b.y));}
    static GameObject First(Vector2 point){if(UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current==null)return null;var hits=new List<UnityEngine.EventSystems.RaycastResult>();UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current.RaycastAll(new UnityEngine.EventSystems.PointerEventData(UnityEngine.EventSystems.EventSystem.current){position=point},hits);return hits.Count==0?null:hits[0].gameObject;}
    static bool Belongs(GameObject hit,Transform parent)=>hit!=null&&(hit.transform==parent||hit.transform.IsChildOf(parent));
    static bool Inside(RectTransform viewport,Camera camera,Vector2 point)=>RectTransformUtility.RectangleContainsScreenPoint(viewport,point,camera)&&Screen.safeArea.Contains(point);
    static bool ActualDragGraphic(GameObject hit,ScrollRect scroll){if(hit==null||scroll.content==null||!Belongs(hit,scroll.content))return false;var graphic=hit.GetComponent<Graphic>();return graphic!=null&&graphic.isActiveAndEnabled&&graphic.raycastTarget&&!graphic.canvasRenderer.cull&&hit.GetComponentInParent<ScrollRect>()==scroll;}
    static void Write(Row row){if(++rows>4096)throw new InvalidOperationException("Bounded catalog census exceeded");row.frame=Time.frameCount;row.timeScale=Time.timeScale;row.action=actions;row.violations=AriaCommandEvidence.Violations;File.AppendAllText(Evidence,JsonUtility.ToJson(row)+"\n");}
    static bool IntendedHeavyPurchase()
    {
        if(!(bool)F("playerResumeProbeActive").GetValue(null)||(int)F("playerResumeStep").GetValue(null)!=0||!(bool)F("playerResumeGuardReady").GetValue(null)||!(bool)F("playerResumeLogisticsReady").GetValue(null))return false;
        if((Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind)F("playerResumePaidRole").GetValue(null)!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.ApcHeavy)return false;
        if(!UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadSkirmish(out var model)||model.Finished||model.Paused||!Mathf.Approximately(Time.timeScale,1))return false;
        if(!UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadSkirmishReadiness(out var readiness)||readiness.Stage<(int)(Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishReadinessStage)F("playerResumeRequiredReadiness").GetValue(null))return false;
        var world=World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;if(world==null||!world.IsCreated)return false;var em=world.EntityManager;
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent),typeof(SkirmishMatchState));if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)return false;
        var session=q.GetSingletonEntity();if(em.HasBuffer<SkirmishProductionReservation>(session))foreach(var receipt in em.GetBuffer<SkirmishProductionReservation>(session,true))if(receipt.FactionId==1&&receipt.Role==Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.ApcHeavy&&(receipt.Phase is Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishReservationPhase.Reserved or Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishReservationPhase.Producing or Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishReservationPhase.Live))return false;
        return true;
    }
    static void Pump()
    {
        if(!started||!EditorApplication.isPlaying||Time.frameCount==lastFrame)return;lastFrame=Time.frameCount;
        try
        {
            OwnFrame();
            var touch=F("playerResumeTouch").GetValue(null) as AriaTouchInputUiSystemHelper;
            if(touch!=null&&(bool)F("playerResumeProbeActive").GetValue(null)){
                if(!touch.IsBusy&&EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup>=(double)F("playerResumeDue").GetValue(null)&&IntendedHeavyPurchase())TryCatalog(touch);
            }
            // Original native deadline, setup/readiness/cost/queue/Save/Continue gates remain authoritative.
            M("TickPlayerResumeFrame").Invoke(null,null);
        }
        catch(Exception error){failed=true;Write(new Row{stage="Failure",reason=error.ToString(),failed=true});Stop("FailedCatalogCleanup");Debug.LogError("[S005CatalogTouch1619] failed=1 reason="+error.Message);}
    }
    static void TryCatalog(AriaTouchInputUiSystemHelper touch)
    {
        var catalog=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<BuildDrawerCatalogRuntimeView>(FindObjectsInactive.Include);if(catalog==null)return;
        var button=catalog.ResolveCatalogTarget(Game.UI.Contracts.BuildDrawerCategory.Vehicles,SkirmishRoleCatalogConfig.RuntimePrefabKey(Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.ApcHeavy));if(button==null)return;
        if(!selectionSeen&&catalog.TryReadSelectedRequest(out string selected,out int cost,out var selectedFailure,out _)&&selected==SkirmishRoleCatalogConfig.RuntimePrefabKey(Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.ApcHeavy)){
            selectionSeen=true;Write(new Row{stage="NativeCatalogSelection",step=(int)F("playerResumeStep").GetValue(null),selectedPrefab=selected,materialsCost=cost,availability=selectedFailure.ToString(),target=button.name,targetPath=PathOf(button.transform),completed=(int)touch.CompletedGestures,dispatched=(int)touch.DispatchedGestures,unexpected=(int)touch.UnexpectedSamples});
        }
        var scroll=button.GetComponentInParent<ScrollRect>();if(scroll==null||scroll.viewport==null||scroll.content==null)return;
        var canvas=button.GetComponentInParent<Canvas>();Camera camera=canvas!=null&&canvas.renderMode!=RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay?canvas.worldCamera:null;
        var rect=(RectTransform)button.transform;var center=RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(camera,rect.TransformPoint(rect.rect.center));var hit=First(center);
        var b=ScreenRect(rect,camera);var v=ScreenRect(scroll.viewport,camera);var content=scroll.content;var local=scroll.viewport.InverseTransformPoint(rect.TransformPoint(rect.rect.center));
        var row=new Row{stage="Geometry",step=(int)F("playerResumeStep").GetValue(null),target=button.name,targetPath=PathOf(button.transform),scrollPath=PathOf(scroll.transform),contentPath=PathOf(content),active=button.IsActive(),interactable=button.IsInteractable(),firstHit=PathOf(hit?.transform),centerPresented=Screen.safeArea.Contains(center)&&Belongs(hit,button.transform),screenWidth=Screen.width,screenHeight=Screen.height,buttonX=b.x,buttonY=b.y,buttonWidth=b.width,buttonHeight=b.height,viewportX=v.x,viewportY=v.y,viewportWidth=v.width,viewportHeight=v.height,contentX=content.anchoredPosition.x,contentY=content.anchoredPosition.y,contentWidth=content.rect.width,contentHeight=content.rect.height,normalizedY=scroll.verticalNormalizedPosition,velocityY=scroll.velocity.y,localCenterY=local.y,localMinY=scroll.viewport.rect.yMin,localMaxY=scroll.viewport.rect.yMax,completed=(int)touch.CompletedGestures,dispatched=(int)touch.DispatchedGestures,unexpected=(int)touch.UnexpectedSamples};
        if(catalog.TryReadSelectedRequest(out string key,out _,out var failure,out _)){row.selectedPrefab=key;row.availability=failure.ToString();}
        if(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup>=nextCensus){nextCensus=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup+1;Write(row);}
        if(row.centerPresented||!row.active||!row.interactable)return;
        if(actions>=24)throw new InvalidOperationException("24 safe public catalog actions exhausted without native HeavyAPC center/selection; preserve geometry");
        // First use an actually visible part of the intended card; all candidate raycasts are read-only.
        for(int y=1;y<=4;y++)for(int x=1;x<=4;x++){
            var p=new Vector2(b.x+b.width*x/5f,b.y+b.height*y/5f);if(!Inside(scroll.viewport,camera,p))continue;var first=First(p);
            if(!Belongs(first,button.transform))continue;
            row.stage="PublicPartialCardTap";row.targetInteriorHit=true;row.fromInViewport=row.toInViewport=row.safeEndpoints=true;row.fromX=row.toX=p.x;row.fromY=row.toY=p.y;row.firstHit=PathOf(first.transform);
            if(touch.TryGesture(p,p,.15f,0,Time.unscaledTime)){actions++;row.queued=true;F("playerResumeDue").SetValue(null,EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup+.75);Write(row);}return;
        }
        bool up=local.y<scroll.viewport.rect.yMin;bool down=local.y>scroll.viewport.rect.yMax;if(!up&&!down)return;
        var bounds=scroll.viewport.rect;var candidates=new List<string>();
        for(int y=1;y<=4;y++)for(int x=1;x<=4;x++){
            var startLocal=new Vector3(bounds.xMin+bounds.width*x/5f,bounds.yMin+bounds.height*y/5f,0);
            if(up&&y>2||down&&y<3)continue;
            var from=RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(camera,scroll.viewport.TransformPoint(startLocal));var to=RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(camera,scroll.viewport.TransformPoint(startLocal+new Vector3(0,bounds.height*(up?.4f:-.4f),0)));
            var first=First(from);candidates.Add(from.x+","+from.y+":"+PathOf(first?.transform));if(!Inside(scroll.viewport,camera,from)||!Inside(scroll.viewport,camera,to)||!ActualDragGraphic(first,scroll))continue;
            row.stage="PublicSafeScroll";row.dragHit=PathOf(first.transform);row.dragStartInContent=row.sameScrollRect=row.fromInViewport=row.toInViewport=row.safeEndpoints=true;row.fromX=from.x;row.fromY=from.y;row.toX=to.x;row.toY=to.y;row.candidateHits=string.Join("|",candidates);
            if(touch.TryGesture(from,to,.5f,.9f,Time.unscaledTime)){actions++;row.queued=true;F("playerResumeDue").SetValue(null,EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup+1.5);Write(row);}return;
        }
        row.stage="NoSafeDragGraphic";row.candidateHits=string.Join("|",candidates);Write(row);
    }
}

    public static void Stop(string reason)
    {
        if(!started)return;EditorApplication.update-=Tick;if(resumeFrame!=null&&(!PaidReady||!EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode))resumeFrame.enabled=originalResumeEnabled;
        if(PaidReady&&EditorApplication.isPlaying){failed=true;Write(new Row{stage="Failure",reason="External owner stopping during paid native Play: "+reason});}
        // The removed callback belonged to this validation; native Finish also removes it.
        // Re-registering it after closure would resurrect a completed validation.
        if(qaStageBorrowed){F("stage").SetValue(null,originalQaStage);qaStageBorrowed=false;}
        Write(new Row{stage="Closed",reason=reason+(failed?";Failed":""),ownershipTransferred=removedStartup,routeStarted=PaidReady&&!OwnsSelection});started=false;
    }
    static bool Tap(Button b,AriaTouchInputUiSystemHelper touch)
    {
        if(b==null)return false;var args=new object[]{b,default(Vector2)};if(!(bool)M("TryPresentedButtonPoint").Invoke(null,args))return false;var point=(Vector2)args[1];
        if(!touch.TryGesture(point,point,.15f,0,Time.unscaledTime))return false;F("manualActions").SetValue(null,(int)F("manualActions").GetValue(null)+1);next=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup+.6;return true;
    }
    static bool RocketeerGroup(EntityManager em,FixedString64Bytes id,uint group)
    {
        using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent),typeof(SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent),typeof(SkirmishUnitRoleComponent),typeof(UnitHealth));using var es=q.ToEntityArray(Allocator.Temp);int count=0;
        foreach(var e in es){var own=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(e);if(own.FactionId!=1||!own.SessionId.Equals(id)||em.GetComponentData<SkirmishArmyGroupMembershipComponent>(e).GroupId!=group||em.GetComponentData<UnitHealth>(e).Current<=0)continue;if(em.GetComponentData<SkirmishUnitRoleComponent>(e).Role!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.Rocketeer||own.CapacityReleased!=0)return false;count++;}return count>0;
    }
    static void Tick()
    {
        if(!started||failed||!EditorApplication.isPlaying||Time.frameCount==frame)return;frame=Time.frameCount;
        try
        {
            var w=World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;if(w==null||!w.IsCreated)return;var em=w.EntityManager;
            using var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent),typeof(SkirmishMatchState),typeof(SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent),typeof(SkirmishBaseAssaultFactComponent));if(q.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)return;
            var session=q.GetSingletonEntity();var state=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(session);var match=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishMatchState>(session);var clock=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishObjectiveClockComponent>(session);var facts=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishBaseAssaultFactComponent>(session);double now=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;
            if(state.CatalogId.ToString()!="S005"||(attempt.Length>0&&attempt!=state.SessionId.ToString()))throw new InvalidOperationException("Foreign early route attempt");
            using var runtime=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(RuntimeGameplayStateComponent));if(runtime.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Unique actual runtime simulation state required");bool nativeSimulationActive=runtime.GetSingleton<RuntimeGameplayStateComponent>().SimulationActive!=0;
            var touch=F("manualTouch").GetValue(null)as AriaTouchInputUiSystemHelper;
            if(!PaidReady)
            {
                if((int)F("playerResumeStep").GetValue(null)!=-1||touch==null)return;
                if(match.Phase!=SkirmishPhase.Playing||!nativeSimulationActive||clock.ElapsedSeconds<=0||clock.Paused!=0||facts.EnemyDesignatedAlive==0||!Mathf.Approximately(Time.timeScale,1)||UiShellRuntimeGateway.ReadAriaPlay().Active||touch.IsBusy||!touch.IsRunning||touch.PlayerInterrupted||touch.UnexpectedSamples!=0||touch.PhysicalInterventions!=0||(int)F("manualAttackReceipts").GetValue(null)!=0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Early route paid handoff not pristine/alive-base/normal public input");
                uint id=(uint)F("playerResumeReceipt").GetValue(null);int matched=0;foreach(var receipt in em.GetBuffer<SkirmishProductionReservation>(session,true))if(receipt.ReservationId==id&&receipt.FactionId==1){if(receipt.Role!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishRoleKind.ApcHeavy||receipt.MaterialsPaid!=280||receipt.Phase!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishReservationPhase.Live||receipt.DeliveredMembers!=1||receipt.MemberCount!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Actual paid HeavyAPC delivery receipt unavailable");matched++;}if(id==0||matched!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Unique exact paid force receipt required");
                paidGestures=(uint)F("playerResumeCombatGestures").GetValue(null);uint prior=(uint)F("manualPriorGestures").GetValue(null);if(prior!=0||paidGestures==0||AriaCommandEvidence.CompletedTouches!=paidGestures+touch.CompletedGestures||AriaCommandEvidence.Violations!=0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Exact real paid setup/current Touch sum does not conserve native recorder");
                F("manualPriorGestures").SetValue(null,paidGestures);F("manualActions").SetValue(null,checked((int)F("manualActions").GetValue(null)+(int)paidGestures));
                borrowedDue=(double)F("manualDue").GetValue(null);selectionDeadline=now+35;defenseWaitStarted=now;F("manualDue").SetValue(null,selectionDeadline+185);
                S005CatalogTouch1619.Stop("VerifiedNativePaidSetupTransfer");foreach(var mb in UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<MonoBehaviour>(FindObjectsInactive.Include,FindObjectsSortMode.None))if(mb.GetType().FullName=="Game.Editor.SkirmishS005PlayerResumeFrame"){if(resumeFrame!=null)throw new InvalidOperationException("Duplicate exact native setup input frame");resumeFrame=mb;originalResumeEnabled=mb.enabled;mb.enabled=false;}if(resumeFrame==null)throw new InvalidOperationException("Exact native setup input owner missing");
                startupTick=(EditorApplication.CallbackFunction)Delegate.CreateDelegate(typeof(EditorApplication.CallbackFunction),M("Tick"));EditorApplication.update-=startupTick;removedStartup=true;attempt=state.SessionId.ToString();PaidReady=OwnsSelection=true;
                Write(new Row{stage="PaidSetupHandoff",receipt=id,paidReceiptLive=true,baseAlive=true,elapsed=clock.ElapsedSeconds,paidGestures=paidGestures,manualPrior=paidGestures,manualCompleted=touch.CompletedGestures,manualDispatched=touch.DispatchedGestures,manualRunning=true,manualBusy=false,ownershipTransferred=true,originalResumeEnabled=originalResumeEnabled,originalDue=borrowedDue,manualAttackReceipts=0});
            }
            if(now>=traceNext){M("AppendTrace").Invoke(null,new object[]{em,session,match,nativeSimulationActive,clock.ElapsedSeconds});traceNext=now+1;}
            if(match.Phase==SkirmishPhase.Finished)
            {
                if(!terminalStarted){var result=em.GetComponentData<SkirmishResultComponent>(session);if(result.Frozen==0||!result.SessionId.Equals(state.SessionId)||result.Outcome!=Game.Skirmish.Contracts.SkirmishOutcomeKind.Victory||match.Outcome!=SkirmishOutcome.Victory||nativeSimulationActive||clock.Paused==0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Real frozen same-attempt Victory terminal required");originalQaStage=(int)F("stage").GetValue(null);if(originalQaStage<3){F("stage").SetValue(null,3);qaStageBorrowed=true;}Write(new Row{stage="NativeTerminalHandoff",nativeFinished=true,resultFrozen=true,phase=match.Outcome.ToString(),elapsed=clock.ElapsedSeconds});terminalStarted=true;M("StartTerminalProbe").Invoke(null,new object[]{em,session,match,clock.ElapsedSeconds});}return;
            }
            if(!OwnsSelection)return;if(touch==null||!touch.IsRunning||touch.PlayerInterrupted||touch.UnexpectedSamples!=0||touch.PhysicalInterventions!=0||AriaCommandEvidence.Violations!=0)throw new InvalidOperationException("Early selection input interrupted");
            if(!S005ManualHomeDefense1619.InitialDefenseReady||!S005ManualHomeDefense1619.ArmoredStaged||S005ManualHomeDefense1619.OwnsLease)
            {
                if(now-defenseWaitStarted>45)throw new TimeoutException("Paid normal-input Car and Armored APC Move/Hold staging was not established before offensive route");
                return;
            }
            if(defenseWaitStarted>0)
            {
                if(touch.IsBusy||touch.CompletedGestures!=touch.DispatchedGestures)return;
                double inherited=(double)F("manualDue").GetValue(null);
                if(Math.Abs(inherited-(selectionDeadline+185))>.000001)throw new InvalidOperationException("Home-defense Touch did not restore exact early-route manualDue lease");
                selectionDeadline=now+35;F("manualDue").SetValue(null,selectionDeadline+185);
                defenseWaitStarted=0;S005ManualHomeDefense1619.RecordReservedSkip();
                Write(new Row{stage="HomeDefenseHandoff",reason="VerifiedPaidThenPublicCarAndArmoredHomeMoveHoldThenRouteSelection",baseAlive=facts.PlayerDesignatedAlive!=0,elapsed=clock.ElapsedSeconds,manualCompleted=touch.CompletedGestures,manualDispatched=touch.DispatchedGestures,manualPrior=(uint)F("manualPriorGestures").GetValue(null),originalDue=borrowedDue});
            }
            if(now>selectionDeadline)throw new TimeoutException("Actual public Rocketeer card selection unavailable in35seconds");
            // Native manual frame owns touch.Tick; its due lease prevents troop orders.
            if(touch.IsBusy||now<next)return;var drawer=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<BuildDrawerView>(FindObjectsInactive.Include);if(drawer!=null&&drawer.IsOpen){Tap(drawer.CloseButton,touch);return;}
            if(S005SouthFlank1619.WantsSelectedCohort(em,state)){S005ManualFogFallback1619.BeginEarlyRoute(em,session,state,borrowedDue);OwnsSelection=false;Write(new Row{stage="SelectedEarlyRoute",baseAlive=facts.EnemyDesignatedAlive!=0,routeStarted=true,elapsed=clock.ElapsedSeconds,manualCompleted=touch.CompletedGestures,manualDispatched=touch.DispatchedGestures,manualPrior=(uint)F("manualPriorGestures").GetValue(null),originalDue=borrowedDue});return;}
            var commands=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<MatchOverlayCommandControlsView>();if(UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadMatchHudCommandState(out var mode)&&mode.ActiveCommandMode!=Game.Tactical.Contracts.TacticalCommandMode.Select){Tap(commands?.SelectButton,touch);return;}
            var tray=UnityEngine.Object.FindAnyObjectByType<MatchHudSquadTrayView>();if(tray==null||!UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadMatchHudSquadTray(out var model)||!UiShellRuntimeGateway.TryReadExpandedSquadPage(out var page))return;
            if(page.SelectedGroups>0){Tap(tray.ClearSelectionButton,touch);return;}
            for(int i=0;i<5;i++){var card=model.GetCard(i);if(!card.Visible||card.GroupId==0||card.AliveCount<=0||!RocketeerGroup(em,state.SessionId,card.GroupId))continue;if(Tap(tray.VisibleCardButton(i),touch))Write(new Row{stage="PublicRocketeerCardTouch",group=card.GroupId,slot=i,baseAlive=facts.EnemyDesignatedAlive!=0,elapsed=clock.ElapsedSeconds});return;}
            if(page.NextPage&&pages<3){if(Tap(tray.NextPageButton,touch))pages++;return;}throw new InvalidOperationException("No real live Rocketeer card within native visible pages");
        }
        catch(Exception error){failed=true;Write(new Row{stage="Failure",reason=error.ToString()});Debug.LogError("[S005EarlyRoute1619] failure="+error.Message);Stop("FailedEarlyRouteCleanup");}
    }
}

// QA diagnostics only: native observers and existing logging switches, never gameplay orders/state.
public static class S005GroundTouchDiagnostic1619
{
    const string Output="/Users/farhad/Projects/WarlineCapture/Design/AgentReports/SkirmishExpansion/S005/native1619-ground-touch-diagnostic.jsonl";
    static bool active,oldClick,oldMove,failed; static int untilFrame=-1,lastCanvas=-1,lastEditor=-1;
    static uint dispatch; static string attempt=""; static Game.UI.Runtime.AriaTouchInputUiSystemHelper driver;
    static readonly System.Collections.Generic.List<Unity.Entities.Entity> actors=new();
    [System.Serializable] sealed class Row
    {
        public string stage="",samplingPhase="",attempt="",reason="",kind="",message="",stable="",role="",targetStable="";
        public int frame,worldSequence,inputCount,runtimeCount,introCount,entityIndex,entityVersion,queueCount,requestId,requestFrame,receipt,reasonCode,commandMode,targetIndex,targetVersion,targetCellX,targetCellZ;
        public uint dispatched,completed;public float elapsed,screenWidth,screenHeight,pointerX,pointerY,x,z;
        public bool enabledClick,enabledMove,oldClick,oldMove,restoredExact,failed,touchAvailable,touchPressed,touchBegan,touchEnded,mouseAvailable,mousePressed,mouseBegan,mouseEnded;
        public int pointerDevice,mouseDevice,pointerPressedOverUi,isDraggingSelection,ignoreNextRelease,skipNextRelease,ignoreUntil,ignoreUiUntilRelease,hasQueuedMove,activeMode,activeModeOneShot,activeModeRequiresWorld,boardDirection,boardDrag,selectionMode,suppressNextClick,simulationActive,playRequested,fullscreenMap,fullscreenIso,buildMode,introLocked,introState;
        public int hasTargetCell,hasTargetEntity,hasWorldPosition,explicitAttack,accepted,hasResult,hp,selected,held,disabled,canAttack,pathRequested,pathFollowing,targetPresent;
    }
    static Row Base(string stage,string phase)=>new Row{stage=stage,samplingPhase=phase,frame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount,attempt=attempt,dispatched=driver?.DispatchedGestures??0,completed=driver?.CompletedGestures??0,enabledClick=Game.Runtime.SelectionRuntimeDiagnosticsSystemHelper.EditorClickDiagnosticsEnabled,enabledMove=Game.Runtime.SelectionRuntimeDiagnosticsSystemHelper.EditorMoveCommandTraceEnabled,oldClick=oldClick,oldMove=oldMove,screenWidth=UnityEngine.Screen.width,screenHeight=UnityEngine.Screen.height};
    static void Write(Row r)=>System.IO.File.AppendAllText(Output,UnityEngine.JsonUtility.ToJson(r)+"\n");
    public static void Start()
    {
        if(active||System.IO.File.Exists(Output))throw new System.IO.IOException("Preserve1619 ground diagnostic evidence");
        using(System.IO.File.Open(Output,System.IO.FileMode.CreateNew,System.IO.FileAccess.Write,System.IO.FileShare.Read)){}
        oldClick=Game.Runtime.SelectionRuntimeDiagnosticsSystemHelper.EditorClickDiagnosticsEnabled;oldMove=Game.Runtime.SelectionRuntimeDiagnosticsSystemHelper.EditorMoveCommandTraceEnabled;
        active=true;failed=false;Game.Runtime.SelectionRuntimeDiagnosticsSystemHelper.EditorClickDiagnosticsEnabled=true;Game.Runtime.SelectionRuntimeDiagnosticsSystemHelper.EditorMoveCommandTraceEnabled=true;
        UnityEngine.Canvas.willRenderCanvases+=Canvas;UnityEditor.EditorApplication.update+=Editor;UnityEngine.Application.logMessageReceived+=NativeLog;
        try{Write(Base("Started","ExternalQA"));}catch{Stop("StartFailure");throw;}
    }
    public static void Stop(string reason)
    {
        if(!active)return;UnityEngine.Canvas.willRenderCanvases-=Canvas;UnityEditor.EditorApplication.update-=Editor;UnityEngine.Application.logMessageReceived-=NativeLog;
        Game.Runtime.SelectionRuntimeDiagnosticsSystemHelper.EditorClickDiagnosticsEnabled=oldClick;Game.Runtime.SelectionRuntimeDiagnosticsSystemHelper.EditorMoveCommandTraceEnabled=oldMove;
        var r=Base("Closed","ExternalQA");r.reason=reason;r.failed=failed;r.restoredExact=r.enabledClick==oldClick&&r.enabledMove==oldMove;Write(r);active=false;
    }
    public static void Track(Game.UI.Runtime.AriaTouchInputUiSystemHelper input,string currentAttempt,System.Collections.Generic.List<Unity.Entities.Entity> cohort,UnityEngine.Vector3 goal)
    {
        if(!active||failed||input==null||!input.IsRunning||string.IsNullOrEmpty(currentAttempt))throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Owned native ground diagnostics unavailable");
        driver=input;attempt=currentAttempt;dispatch=input.DispatchedGestures;actors.Clear();actors.AddRange(cohort);untilFrame=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount+12;
        var r=Base("GroundDispatch","CanvasWillRenderCanvases");r.x=goal.x;r.z=goal.z;Write(r);Capture("CanvasDispatch");
    }
    static void NativeLog(string message,string stack,UnityEngine.LogType type)
    {
        if(!active||failed||driver==null||UnityEngine.Time.frameCount>untilFrame||(!message.StartsWith("[SelectionClick]",System.StringComparison.Ordinal)&&!message.StartsWith("[MoveCommandTrace]",System.StringComparison.Ordinal)))return;
        try{var r=Base("NativeHandlerLog","NativeLogCallback");r.message=message;Write(r);}catch{failed=true;}
    }
    static void Canvas(){if(lastCanvas==UnityEngine.Time.frameCount)return;lastCanvas=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount;Capture("CanvasWillRenderCanvases");}
    static void Editor(){if(lastEditor==UnityEngine.Time.frameCount)return;lastEditor=UnityEngine.Time.frameCount;Capture("EditorUpdate");}
    static void Capture(string phase)
    {
        if(!active||failed||driver==null||UnityEngine.Time.frameCount>untilFrame)return;
        // A completed match disposes its world before this Editor-only observer
        // receives its final update. Stop() still records and checks restoration.
        if(!UnityEditor.EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode)return;
        try
        {
            var world=Unity.Entities.World.DefaultGameObjectInjectionWorld;if(world==null||!world.IsCreated)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Tracked native world absent");var em=world.EntityManager;
            using var sessions=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent),typeof(Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState));if(sessions.CalculateEntityCount()!=1)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Tracked native session not unique");var session=sessions.GetSingletonEntity();var state=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishExpandedSessionComponent>(session);if(state.SessionId.ToString()!=attempt)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Tracked native attempt changed");
            var r=Base("PointerAndGates",phase);r.worldSequence=(int)world.SequenceNumber;r.elapsed=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishMatchState>(session).ElapsedSeconds;
            // Read actual device controls; do not call GamePointerInput because that accessor mutates its static tracking.
            var screen=UnityEngine.InputSystem.Touchscreen.current;r.touchAvailable=screen!=null;if(screen!=null){var t=screen.primaryTouch;r.pointerDevice=screen.deviceId;r.pointerX=t.position.ReadValue().x;r.pointerY=t.position.ReadValue().y;r.touchPressed=t.press.isPressed;r.touchBegan=t.press.wasPressedThisFrame;r.touchEnded=t.press.wasReleasedThisFrame;}
            var mouse=UnityEngine.InputSystem.Mouse.current;r.mouseAvailable=mouse!=null;if(mouse!=null){r.mouseDevice=mouse.deviceId;r.mousePressed=mouse.leftButton.isPressed;r.mouseBegan=mouse.leftButton.wasPressedThisFrame;r.mouseEnded=mouse.leftButton.wasReleasedThisFrame;}
            using(var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.RtsSelectionInputStateComponent))){r.inputCount=q.CalculateEntityCount();if(r.inputCount!=1)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Native input owner not unique");var e=q.GetSingletonEntity();var i=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.RtsSelectionInputStateComponent>(e);r.entityIndex=e.Index;r.entityVersion=e.Version;r.pointerPressedOverUi=i.PointerPressedOverUi;r.isDraggingSelection=i.IsDraggingSelection;r.ignoreNextRelease=i.IgnoreNextLeftMouseRelease;r.skipNextRelease=i.SkipNextWorldReleaseAfterSelection;r.ignoreUntil=i.IgnoreWorldCommandsUntilFrame;r.ignoreUiUntilRelease=i.IgnoreUiClickUntilRelease;r.hasQueuedMove=i.HasQueuedMoveOrder;r.activeMode=i.ActiveCommandMode;r.activeModeOneShot=i.ActiveCommandModeOneShot;r.activeModeRequiresWorld=i.ActiveCommandModeRequiresWorldTarget;r.boardDirection=i.ActiveBoardCommandDirection;r.boardDrag=i.BoardPassengerDragArmed;
                if(em.HasBuffer<Game.Components.RtsSelectionCommandIntentRequestElement>(e)){var b=em.GetBuffer<Game.Components.RtsSelectionCommandIntentRequestElement>(e,true);for(int k=0;k<b.Length;k++){var v=b[k];var a=Base("NativeCommandRequest",phase);a.queueCount=b.Length;a.kind=v.Kind.ToString();a.requestId=v.RequestId;a.requestFrame=v.Frame;a.receipt=(int)v.InputReceipt;a.explicitAttack=v.ExplicitAttackTargetMode;a.hasTargetCell=v.HasTargetCell;a.hasTargetEntity=v.HasTargetEntity;a.hasWorldPosition=v.HasWorldPosition;a.targetIndex=v.TargetEntity.Index;a.targetVersion=v.TargetEntity.Version;a.targetCellX=v.TargetCell.x;a.targetCellZ=v.TargetCell.y;Write(a);}}
                if(em.HasBuffer<Game.Components.RtsSelectionCommandResultElement>(e)){var b=em.GetBuffer<Game.Components.RtsSelectionCommandResultElement>(e,true);for(int k=0;k<b.Length;k++){var v=b[k];var a=Base("NativeCommandResult",phase);a.queueCount=b.Length;a.kind=v.Kind.ToString();a.requestId=v.RequestId;a.requestFrame=v.Frame;a.accepted=v.Accepted;a.hasResult=v.HasCommandResult;a.reasonCode=v.ReasonCode;a.commandMode=v.CommandMode;a.hasTargetCell=v.HasTargetCell;a.hasTargetEntity=v.HasTargetEntity;a.hasWorldPosition=v.HasWorldPosition;a.targetIndex=v.TargetEntity.Index;a.targetVersion=v.TargetEntity.Version;a.targetCellX=v.TargetCell.x;a.targetCellZ=v.TargetCell.y;a.message=v.Message.ToString();Write(a);}}}
            using(var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.RuntimeGameplayStateComponent))){r.runtimeCount=q.CalculateEntityCount();if(r.runtimeCount!=1)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Native runtime owner not unique");var s=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.RuntimeGameplayStateComponent>(q.GetSingletonEntity());r.selectionMode=s.SelectionModeActive;r.suppressNextClick=s.SuppressNextWorldClick;r.simulationActive=s.SimulationActive;r.playRequested=s.PlayRequested;r.fullscreenMap=s.FullscreenMapOpen;r.fullscreenIso=s.FullscreenMapIsoMode;r.buildMode=s.BuildModeActive;}
            using(var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.UI.Shell.Contracts.Ecs.MatchIntroTransitionComponent))){r.introCount=q.CalculateEntityCount();if(r.introCount==1){var v=em.GetComponentData<Game.UI.Shell.Contracts.Ecs.MatchIntroTransitionComponent>(q.GetSingletonEntity());r.introLocked=v.InputLocked;r.introState=(int)v.State;}}
            Write(r);
            using(var q=em.CreateEntityQuery(typeof(Game.Components.SelectionHudFeedbackQueueComponent),typeof(Game.Components.SelectionHudFeedbackElement))){using var es=q.ToEntityArray(Unity.Collections.Allocator.Temp);foreach(var e in es){var b=em.GetBuffer<Game.Components.SelectionHudFeedbackElement>(e,true);foreach(var v in b){var a=Base("NativeFeedback",phase);a.kind=v.Kind.ToString();a.accepted=v.CommandAccepted;a.reasonCode=v.ReasonCode;a.commandMode=v.CommandMode;a.message=v.Message.ToString();Write(a);}}}
            foreach(var actor in actors){if(!em.Exists(actor))continue;var a=Base("OriginalCohortState",phase);a.entityIndex=actor.Index;a.entityVersion=actor.Version;if(!em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor))throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Tracked cohort ownership absent");var o=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.SkirmishAttemptOwnedComponent>(actor);if(o.SessionId.ToString()!=attempt||o.FactionId!=1)throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Tracked cohort attempt/faction changed");a.stable=o.StableObjectId.ToString();a.selected=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.SelectedUnitTag>(actor)?1:0;a.held=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.HoldPositionOrderTag>(actor)?1:0;a.disabled=em.HasComponent<Unity.Entities.Disabled>(actor)?1:0;if(em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(actor))a.hp=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitHealth>(actor).Current;if(em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitCombat>(actor))a.canAttack=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitCombat>(actor).CanAttack;if(em.HasComponent<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor)){var t=em.GetComponentData<Unity.Transforms.LocalTransform>(actor);a.x=t.Position.x;a.z=t.Position.z;}a.pathRequested=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitPathRequest>(actor)?1:0;a.pathFollowing=em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitPathFollow>(actor)?1:0;if(em.HasComponent<Game.Components.UnitTarget>(actor)){var t=em.GetComponentData<Game.Components.UnitTarget>(actor);a.targetPresent=1;a.targetCellX=t.Cell.x;a.targetCellZ=t.Cell.y;}Write(a);}
        }
        catch(System.Exception error){failed=true;var r=Base("DiagnosticFailure",phase);r.failed=true;r.reason=error.ToString();Write(r);}
    }
}
